# lib/sandbox

Prototype runtime libraries live here while they are still being hardened.

These files may be imported by active runtime code, but their folder name is a reminder that the API is still experimental.

Once a prototype becomes stable, move it into a permanent runtime location such as:

- `lib/rendering/<feature>`
- `lib/engine/renderers/<feature>`
- `lib/<feature>`

const FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT =
  "The reason sin, cos, and π matter is that they are a compact way to describe repeating motion. Imagine a dot going around a wheel. π marks half of a full turn, and 2π marks one whole turn. Sine and cosine track where that dot is as it turns. The graph matters because it unwraps the turning into a timeline, so you can see highs, lows, returns, and repeats.";

const DEFAULT_INTERACTION: VisualStoryInteractionPhase = {
  unlock_after: "story_complete",
  controls: {
    orbit: true,
    zoom: true,
    pan: false,
    drag_objects: true,
    toggle_layers: true,
    scrub_story: true,
    replay: true,
    pause: true,
  },
  free_exploration: {
    enabled: true,
    instructions: "Drag the scene to rotate it, scroll to zoom, or switch to move-dot mode to scrub the motion.",
  },
  manipulators: [
    {
      id: "scrub_phase",
      type: "scrub_phase",
      label: "Move the dot around the cycle",
      target_entity_id: "dot",
    },
  ],
};

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function readString(value: unknown, fallback: string, max = 1600) {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0
    ? value.trim().slice(0, max)
    : fallback;
}

function readOptionalString(value: unknown, max = 900) {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0
    ? value.trim().slice(0, max)
    : null;
}

function readNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number, min = 200, max = 20000) {
  const numberValue = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(numberValue)
    ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, numberValue))
    : fallback;
}

function sanitizeId(value: unknown, fallback: string) {
  const raw = typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 ? value.trim() : fallback;
  const cleaned = raw
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_\-\s]/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
    .slice(0, 80);
  return cleaned || fallback;
}

function readStringArray(value: unknown, fallback: string[] = [], max = 20) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return fallback;
  return value
    .filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.trim().length > 0)
    .map((item) => item.trim().slice(0, 120))
    .slice(0, max);
}

function tuple3(value: unknown): [number, number, number] | undefined {
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length < 2) return undefined;
  const x = Number(value[0]);
  const y = Number(value[1]);
  const z = Number(value[2] ?? 0);
  return Number.isFinite(x) && Number.isFinite(y) && Number.isFinite(z) ? [x, y, z] : undefined;
}

function clamp(value: number, min = 0, max = 1) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function easeInOut(value: number) {
  const t = clamp(value);
  return t < 0.5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2;
}

function cleanOrientationScript(value: string) {
  const normalized = value.replace(/\r/g, "").trim();
  if (!normalized) return FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT;

  const lines = normalized.split("\n");
  const kept: string[] = [];

  for (const line of lines) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      if (kept.length > 0) break;
      continue;
    }

    if (/^[•\-*]\s+/.test(trimmed)) break;
    if (/^(key takeaways?|recap|summary)\s*:?/i.test(trimmed)) break;
    if (/^think of\b/i.test(trimmed) && kept.length > 0) break;

    kept.push(trimmed);
  }

  const joined = kept.join(" ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return joined || FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT;
}

function splitIntoSentences(script: string) {
  const normalized = script.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  if (!normalized) return [FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT];
  return (normalized.match(/[^.!?]+[.!?]+|[^.!?]+$/g) ?? [normalized])
    .map((sentence) => sentence.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 8);
}

function titleFromSegment(segment: string, index: number) {
  const words = segment.replace(/["“”]/g, "").split(/\s+/).slice(0, 5).join(" ");
  return words || `Beat ${index + 1}`;
}

function readRawVisualStory(probe: EngineRenderableProbe) {
  const params: Record<string, unknown> = isRecord(probe.renderer_params)
    ? (probe.renderer_params as Record<string, unknown>)
    : {};

  if (isRecord(params.visual_story)) return params.visual_story;
  if (isRecord(params.visualStory)) return params.visualStory;
  if (isRecord(params.visual_story_v1)) return params.visual_story_v1;

  return params;
}

function normalizeEntities(rawEntities: unknown, orientationScript: string): VisualStoryEntity[] {
  const entities = Array.isArray(rawEntities)
    ? rawEntities
        .map((item, index): VisualStoryEntity | null => {
          const entity = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
          const id = sanitizeId(entity.id, `entity_${index + 1}`);
          const kind = readString(entity.kind ?? entity.type, "custom", 80);
          return {
            id,
            kind,
            semantic_role: readString(entity.semantic_role ?? entity.role, "visible_idea", 140),
            display_name: readOptionalString(entity.display_name ?? entity.label ?? entity.name, 100),
            meaning: readOptionalString(entity.meaning ?? entity.description, 420),
            visual_style: isRecord(entity.visual_style ?? entity.visualStyle)
              ? (entity.visual_style ?? entity.visualStyle) as VisualStoryEntity["visual_style"]
              : null,
            geometry: isRecord(entity.geometry) ? entity.geometry : null,
          };
        })
        .filter((entity): entity is VisualStoryEntity => Boolean(entity))
        .slice(0, 32)
    : [];

  if (entities.length) return entities;

  const lower = orientationScript.toLowerCase();
  const motion = /sin|cos|sine|cosine|π|pi|wheel|circle|turn|wave|cycle|graph/.test(lower);

  if (motion) {
    return [
      { id: "wheel", kind: "wheel", semantic_role: "source_motion", display_name: "wheel", meaning: "A visible cycle that makes repeating motion concrete." },
      { id: "dot", kind: "dot", semantic_role: "tracked_point", display_name: "dot", meaning: "A point whose position changes as the wheel turns." },
      { id: "pi_arc", kind: "circle_arc", semantic_role: "half_turn_marker", display_name: "π", meaning: "Halfway around the turn." },
      { id: "two_pi_arc", kind: "circle_arc", semantic_role: "full_turn_marker", display_name: "2π", meaning: "One whole turn." },
      { id: "wave_graph", kind: "graph_2d", semantic_role: "unwrapped_motion_graph", display_name: "timeline graph", meaning: "The turning motion stretched into a readable wave." },
    ];
  }

  return [
    { id: "anchor", kind: "node", semantic_role: "real_world_anchor", display_name: "real example" },
    { id: "hidden_relationship", kind: "connector", semantic_role: "hidden_relationship", display_name: "hidden link" },
    { id: "visible_model", kind: "node", semantic_role: "visible_model", display_name: "visible model" },
  ];
}

function normalizeRelationships(value: unknown): VisualStoryRelationship[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value
    .map((item, index): VisualStoryRelationship | null => {
      const relation = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
      const sourceId = relation.source_entity_id ?? relation.sourceEntityId ?? relation.source_id ?? relation.source ?? relation.from;
      const targetId = relation.target_entity_id ?? relation.targetEntityId ?? relation.target_id ?? relation.target ?? relation.to;
      return {
        id: sanitizeId(relation.id, `relationship_${index + 1}`),
        source_entity_id: sanitizeId(sourceId, "source"),
        target_entity_id: sanitizeId(targetId, "target"),
        relationship_type: readString(relation.relationship_type ?? relation.type, "maps_to", 80),
        explanation: readString(relation.explanation ?? relation.meaning ?? relation.description ?? relation.label, "These visible parts are connected.", 520),
      };
    })
    .filter((relation): relation is VisualStoryRelationship => Boolean(relation))
    .slice(0, 28);
}

function normalizeTransformations(value: unknown): VisualStoryTransformation[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value
    .map((item, index): VisualStoryTransformation | null => {
      const transformation = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
      const rawEntityIds = transformation.entity_ids ?? transformation.entityIds;
      const from = transformation.from;
      const to = transformation.to;
      const entityIds = [
        ...readStringArray(rawEntityIds, [], 12),
        ...(typeof from === "string" ? [from] : []),
        ...(typeof to === "string" ? [to] : []),
        ...(Array.isArray(to) ? readStringArray(to, [], 12) : []),
      ];

      return {
        id: sanitizeId(transformation.id, `transformation_${index + 1}`),
        type: readString(transformation.type, "show_cause_effect", 100),
        description: readString(transformation.description ?? transformation.meaning ?? transformation.label, "Show the hidden relationship visually.", 620),
        entity_ids: Array.from(new Set(entityIds.map((id) => sanitizeId(id, id)))).slice(0, 16),
        concept_goal: readOptionalString(transformation.concept_goal ?? transformation.conceptGoal ?? transformation.goal, 420),
      };
    })
    .filter((transformation): transformation is VisualStoryTransformation => Boolean(transformation))
    .slice(0, 24);
}

function examplesFromScript(script: string): VisualStorySupportingExample[] {
  const lower = script.toLowerCase();
  const candidates: Array<[string, string, string]> = [
    ["sound", "sound", "A sound wave repeats as pressure changes over time."],
    ["engine", "engine", "An engine cycle repeats mechanical motion."],
    ["engines", "engine", "An engine cycle repeats mechanical motion."],
    ["tide", "tide", "Tides rise and fall in a repeating cycle."],
    ["tides", "tide", "Tides rise and fall in a repeating cycle."],
    ["season", "season", "Seasons repeat as a cycle over time."],
    ["seasons", "season", "Seasons repeat as a cycle over time."],
    ["signal", "signal", "A signal can repeat as a wave pattern."],
    ["signals", "signal", "A signal can repeat as a wave pattern."],
    ["wheel", "wheel", "A wheel makes repeating motion easy to see."],
    ["wheels", "wheel", "A wheel makes repeating motion easy to see."],
    ["wave", "wave", "A wave makes the repeated high-low pattern visible."],
    ["waves", "wave", "A wave makes the repeated high-low pattern visible."],
  ];

  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const examples: VisualStorySupportingExample[] = [];

  for (const [needle, type, why] of candidates) {
    if (!lower.includes(needle) || seen.has(type)) continue;
    seen.add(type);
    examples.push({
      id: type,
      label: needle,
      example_type: type,
      why_it_matters: why,
      explicitly_mentioned: true,
    });
  }

  return examples.slice(0, 6);
}

function normalizeSupportingExamples(value: unknown, orientationScript: string): VisualStorySupportingExample[] {
  const fromPlan = Array.isArray(value)
    ? value
        .map((item, index): VisualStorySupportingExample | null => {
          const example = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
          const label = readString(example.label ?? example.mentioned_text ?? example.mentionedText ?? example.id, `example ${index + 1}`, 100);
          const explicitText = readOptionalString(example.mentioned_text ?? example.mentionedText, 140);
          return {
            id: sanitizeId(example.id ?? example.entity_id ?? example.entityId ?? label, `example_${index + 1}`),
            label,
            example_type: readString(example.example_type ?? example.type ?? example.entity_id ?? example.entityId ?? label.toLowerCase(), label.toLowerCase(), 100),
            why_it_matters: readString(example.why_it_matters ?? example.whyItMatters ?? example.maps_back_to ?? example.mapsBackTo ?? example.meaning ?? example.description, "This example shares the same visible pattern.", 460),
            explicitly_mentioned: example.explicitly_mentioned === true || example.explicitlyMentioned === true || Boolean(explicitText),
          };
        })
        .filter((example): example is VisualStorySupportingExample => Boolean(example))
        .slice(0, 10)
    : [];

  return fromPlan.length ? fromPlan : examplesFromScript(orientationScript);
}

function normalizeActions(value: unknown): VisualStoryAction[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value
    .map((item, index): VisualStoryAction | null => {
      const action = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
      const target = action.entity_id ?? action.entityId ?? action.target_id ?? action.targetId ?? action.target;
      const params: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      for (const key of ["value", "label", "from", "to", "effect", "duration_ms", "durationMs"]) {
        if (key in action) params[key] = action[key];
      }
      if (isRecord(action.params)) Object.assign(params, action.params);

      return {
        id: sanitizeId(action.id, `action_${index + 1}`),
        type: readString(action.type, "highlight_entity", 100),
        entity_id: readOptionalString(target, 100),
        target_entity_id: readOptionalString(action.target_entity_id ?? action.targetEntityId ?? action.to, 100),
        params: Object.keys(params).length ? params : null,
        easing: readOptionalString(action.easing, 80),
      };
    })
    .filter((action): action is VisualStoryAction => Boolean(action))
    .slice(0, 28);
}

function normalizeBeats(value: unknown, orientationScript: string): VisualStoryBeat[] {
  const rawBeats = Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  const beats = rawBeats
    .map((item, index): VisualStoryBeat | null => {
      const beat = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
      const scriptSegment = readString(
        beat.script_segment ?? beat.scriptSegment ?? beat.script_text ?? beat.scriptText ?? beat.words ?? beat.text,
        "",
        900,
      );
      if (!scriptSegment) return null;
      const camera = isRecord(beat.camera) ? beat.camera : {};
      const textOverlaySource = beat.text_overlay ?? beat.textOverlay;
      const textOverlay = isRecord(textOverlaySource) ? textOverlaySource : {};
      return {
        id: sanitizeId(beat.id, `beat_${index + 1}`),
        script_segment: cleanOrientationScript(scriptSegment),
        title: readOptionalString(beat.title, 120) ?? titleFromSegment(scriptSegment, index),
        duration_ms: readNumber(beat.duration_ms ?? beat.durationMs, 4800, 1800, 12000),
        active_entity_ids: readStringArray(beat.active_entity_ids ?? beat.activeEntityIds ?? beat.focus_object_ids, [], 12).map((id) => sanitizeId(id, id)),
        actions: normalizeActions(beat.actions ?? beat.scene_actions ?? beat.action_ids),
        camera: Object.keys(camera).length
          ? {
              mode: readOptionalString(camera.mode ?? camera.move, 80) ?? undefined,
              focus_entity_id: readOptionalString(camera.focus_entity_id ?? camera.focusEntityId, 100),
              position: tuple3(camera.position),
              look_at: tuple3(camera.look_at ?? camera.lookAt),
              zoom: typeof camera.zoom === "number" ? camera.zoom : undefined,
              duration_ms: typeof camera.duration_ms === "number" ? camera.duration_ms : undefined,
              easing: readOptionalString(camera.easing, 60) ?? undefined,
            }
          : null,
        text_overlay: isRecord(textOverlay) && Object.keys(textOverlay).length
          ? { layout: readOptionalString(textOverlay.layout, 80) ?? undefined, emphasis: readOptionalString(textOverlay.emphasis, 80) ?? undefined }
          : null,
      };
    })
    .filter((beat): beat is VisualStoryBeat => Boolean(beat))
    .slice(0, 10);

  if (beats.length) return beats;

  return splitIntoSentences(orientationScript).map((segment, index) => ({
    id: `beat_${index + 1}`,
    script_segment: segment,
    title: titleFromSegment(segment, index),
    duration_ms: index === 0 ? 4200 : 5200,
    active_entity_ids: [],
    actions: [],
    camera: null,
    text_overlay: null,
  }));
}

function normalizeInteraction(value: unknown): VisualStoryInteractionPhase {
  if (!isRecord(value)) return DEFAULT_INTERACTION;

  const controls: Record<string, unknown> = isRecord(value.controls)
    ? value.controls
    : {};

  const freeSource = value.free_exploration ?? value.freeExploration;
  const free: Record<string, unknown> | null = isRecord(freeSource)
    ? freeSource
    : null;
  const manipulatorsRaw = Array.isArray(value.manipulators) ? value.manipulators : [];
  const manipulators = manipulatorsRaw
    .map((item, index) => {
      const manipulator = isRecord(item) ? item : {};
      return {
        id: sanitizeId(manipulator.id, `manipulator_${index + 1}`),
        type: readString(manipulator.type, "scrub_phase", 80),
        label: readString(manipulator.label, "Explore the scene", 120),
        target_entity_id: readOptionalString(manipulator.target_entity_id ?? manipulator.targetEntityId, 100) ?? undefined,
        params: isRecord(manipulator.params) ? manipulator.params : undefined,
      };
    })
    .slice(0, 8);

  return {
    unlock_after: readString(value.unlock_after ?? value.unlockAfter, "story_complete", 80),
    controls: {
      orbit: controls.orbit !== false,
      zoom: controls.zoom !== false,
      pan: controls.pan === true,
      drag_objects: controls.drag_objects !== false && controls.dragObjects !== false,
      toggle_layers: controls.toggle_layers !== false && controls.toggleLayers !== false,
      scrub_story: controls.scrub_story !== false && controls.scrubStory !== false,
      replay: controls.replay !== false,
      pause: controls.pause !== false,
    },
    free_exploration: free
      ? {
          enabled: free.enabled !== false,
          instructions: readOptionalString(free.instructions, 320) ?? DEFAULT_INTERACTION.free_exploration?.instructions,
          highlight_interactive_entities: readStringArray(free.highlight_interactive_entities ?? free.highlightInteractiveEntities, [], 8),
        }
      : DEFAULT_INTERACTION.free_exploration,
    manipulators: manipulators.length ? manipulators : DEFAULT_INTERACTION.manipulators,
  };
}

export function normalizeVisualStoryScene(probe: EngineRenderableProbe): VisualStoryScene {
  const rawStoryContract = readRawVisualStory(probe);
  const story = isRecord(rawStoryContract.story) ? rawStoryContract.story : rawStoryContract;
  const visualSemantics = isRecord(story.visual_semantics ?? story.visualSemantics)
    ? (story.visual_semantics ?? story.visualSemantics) as Record<string, unknown>
    : {};

  const orientationScript = cleanOrientationScript(readString(
    story.orientation_script ??
      story.orientationScript ??
      rawStoryContract.canonical_orientation_script ??
      rawStoryContract.orientation_text ??
      rawStoryContract.teaching_script ??
      probe.prompt.reshaping_explanation ??
      probe.prompt.full_prompt ??
      probe.prompt.task,
    FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT,
    2200,
  ));

  const title = readString(story.title ?? rawStoryContract.title, probe.prompt.task || "Visual story", 180);
  const targetTakeaway = readString(
    story.target_takeaway ?? story.targetTakeaway ?? rawStoryContract.learning_goal ?? rawStoryContract.story_goal,
    probe.prompt.reshaping_explanation || "Use the scene to see the hidden relationship.",
    520,
  );
  const entities = normalizeEntities(visualSemantics.key_entities ?? rawStoryContract.scene_objects, orientationScript);
  const beats = normalizeBeats(story.beats ?? rawStoryContract.script_beats ?? rawStoryContract.beats, orientationScript);
  const lower = orientationScript.toLowerCase();
  const sceneFamily = readString(
    rawStoryContract.scene_family ?? story.scene_family ?? story.sceneFamily,
    /sin|cos|sine|cosine|π|pi|wheel|circle|turn|wave|cycle|graph/.test(lower) ? "motion_to_graph" : "generic_concept_scene",
    100,
  );

  const scriptDisplay = isRecord(story.script_display ?? story.scriptDisplay) ? (story.script_display ?? story.scriptDisplay) as Record<string, unknown> : {};

  return {
    schema_version: readString(rawStoryContract.schema_version, "myway_visual_story_renderer_v1", 100),
    scene_kind: readString(rawStoryContract.scene_kind ?? rawStoryContract.sceneKind, "visual_story", 80),
    title,
    orientation_script: orientationScript,
    root_problem_focus: readString(story.root_problem_focus ?? story.rootProblemFocus, "The learner needs a visual bridge from words to the underlying relationship.", 420),
    target_takeaway: targetTakeaway,
    scene_family: sceneFamily,
    entities,
    relationships: normalizeRelationships(visualSemantics.key_relationships ?? rawStoryContract.key_relationships),
    transformations: normalizeTransformations(visualSemantics.key_transformations ?? rawStoryContract.key_transformations),
    supporting_examples: normalizeSupportingExamples(visualSemantics.supporting_examples, orientationScript),
    beats,
    script_display: {
      show_full_script: scriptDisplay.show_full_script !== false && scriptDisplay.showFullScript !== false,
      reveal_mode: readString(scriptDisplay.reveal_mode ?? scriptDisplay.revealMode, "beat_synced", 80),
      layout: readString(scriptDisplay.layout, "bottom_caption", 80),
      max_visible_segments: readNumber(scriptDisplay.max_visible_segments ?? scriptDisplay.maxVisibleSegments, 1, 1, 3),
      animate_text: scriptDisplay.animate_text !== false && scriptDisplay.animateText !== false,
    },
    interaction_phase: normalizeInteraction(rawStoryContract.interaction_phase ?? rawStoryContract.interactionPhase),
    optional_check: isRecord(rawStoryContract.optional_check ?? rawStoryContract.optionalCheck)
      ? (rawStoryContract.optional_check ?? rawStoryContract.optionalCheck) as VisualStoryScene["optional_check"]
      : null,
  };
}

export function hasVisualStoryContract(probe: EngineRenderableProbe) {
  const params: Record<string, unknown> = isRecord(probe.renderer_params)
    ? (probe.renderer_params as Record<string, unknown>)
    : {};
  const story = readRawVisualStory(probe);

  return (
    isRecord(params.visual_story) ||
    isRecord(params.visualStory) ||
    isRecord(params.visual_story_v1) ||
    story.schema_version === "myway_visual_story_renderer_v1" ||
    story.scene_kind === "visual_story" ||
    isRecord(story.story) ||
    typeof story.canonical_orientation_script === "string" ||
    Array.isArray(story.script_beats) ||
    Array.isArray(story.beats)
  );
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// MyWay Visual Story Renderer Schema
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//
// Purpose:
// A Visual Story is a scene-first teaching contract. The orientation script is
// the source of truth. The visual plan, timeline, camera motion, and post-story
// interaction are derived from that same script instead of from a separate
// staging paragraph or recap.
//
// This schema is intentionally renderer-facing, not provider-specific. Models
// draft it; MyWay validates, normalizes, and renders it using trusted scene
// primitives and interaction controls.

export type VisualStoryRendererSchemaVersion = "myway_visual_story_renderer_v1";

export type VisualStorySceneKind = "visual_story";

export type VisualStoryRendererParams = {
  schema_version: VisualStoryRendererSchemaVersion;
  scene_kind: VisualStorySceneKind;

  /**
   * The actual teaching experience. The story.orientation_script is the
   * canonical learner-facing script and must drive the visual plan.
   */
  story: VisualStoryStory;

  /**
   * What becomes explorable after the autoplay story finishes.
   */
  interaction_phase?: VisualStoryInteractionPhase | null;

  /**
   * Optional lightweight check after the visual story.
   * The story teaches first; the check measures after.
   */
  optional_check?: VisualStoryOptionalCheck | null;

  /**
   * Temporary compatibility mirror for the current VisualStoryProbe renderer.
   * New code should prefer story.orientation_script and story.beats, but the
   * current renderer can still read these top-level fields while migrating.
   */
  canonical_orientation_script?: string;
  title?: string;
  learning_goal?: string;
  scene_family?: VisualStorySceneFamily;
  script_beats?: VisualStoryCompatScriptBeat[];
  scene_objects?: VisualStoryCompatSceneObject[];
  scene_actions?: VisualStoryCompatSceneAction[];
  camera_plan?: VisualStoryCompatCameraBeat[];
  success_criteria?: VisualStorySuccessCriterion[];
};

export type VisualStoryStory = {
  /**
   * The authoritative learner-facing orientation paragraph.
   * Do not include recap bullets or a second staging explanation here.
   */
  orientation_script: string;

  /** Short scene title. */
  title?: string;

  /** The specific gap/pattern this story is trying to repair. */
  root_problem_focus: string;

  /** The target mental model the learner should leave with. */
  target_takeaway: string;

  /**
   * Visual semantic extraction from orientation_script. This is what keeps the
   * scene tied to the words instead of becoming a generic template.
   */
  visual_semantics: VisualStoryVisualSemantics;

  /** Ordered cinematic beats. Each beat maps to a specific script segment. */
  beats: VisualStoryBeat[];

  /** Scene-level defaults and rendering hints. */
  scene_defaults?: VisualStorySceneDefaults | null;

  /** Caption/script behavior. */
  script_display?: VisualStoryScriptDisplay | null;
};

export type VisualStorySceneFamily =
  | "motion_to_graph"
  | "mechanism"
  | "process_flow"
  | "compare_contrast"
  | "spatial_structure"
  | "quantity_change"
  | "cause_effect"
  | "classification_boundary"
  | "generic_concept_scene";

export type VisualStoryVisualSemantics = {
  /** Core objects/concepts that should appear visually. */
  key_entities: VisualStoryEntitySpec[];

  /** Important relationships the learner should see. */
  key_relationships: VisualStoryRelationshipSpec[];

  /** Important motions, mappings, or transformations. */
  key_transformations: VisualStoryTransformationSpec[];

  /**
   * Examples mentioned by the orientation script that can be visual echoes.
   * Do not force examples that are not in the orientation_script.
   */
  supporting_examples?: VisualStorySupportingExampleSpec[];

  /** Entity or relationship ids that should be emphasized. */
  emphasis_targets?: string[];
};

export type VisualStoryEntityKind =
  | "wheel"
  | "dot"
  | "graph_2d"
  | "graph_3d"
  | "wave"
  | "circle_arc"
  | "label"
  | "axis"
  | "surface"
  | "point"
  | "vector"
  | "machine"
  | "particle"
  | "flow"
  | "container"
  | "token"
  | "custom";

export type VisualStoryEntitySpec = {
  id: string;
  kind: VisualStoryEntityKind;
  semantic_role: string;
  display_name?: string;

  /** Human meaning, not just geometry. */
  meaning?: string;

  /** Renderer hints. MyWay still owns final rendering and validation. */
  visual_style?: {
    primitive_style?: "minimal" | "diagrammatic" | "cinematic" | "glassy" | "chalk" | "neon";
    color_hint?: string;
    emphasis?: "low" | "medium" | "high";
    persistent?: boolean;
  } | null;

  /** Optional geometry hints. Avoid relying on raw generated code. */
  geometry?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
};

export type VisualStoryRelationshipType =
  | "maps_to"
  | "drives"
  | "corresponds_to"
  | "becomes"
  | "traces"
  | "controls"
  | "contrasts_with"
  | "explains"
  | "is_example_of";

export type VisualStoryRelationshipSpec = {
  id: string;
  source_entity_id: string;
  target_entity_id: string;
  relationship_type: VisualStoryRelationshipType;
  explanation: string;
};

export type VisualStoryTransformationType =
  | "trace_motion"
  | "unwrap_motion_into_graph"
  | "build_expression_piece_by_piece"
  | "highlight_slice"
  | "morph_shape"
  | "compare_examples"
  | "expand_to_related_examples"
  | "show_cause_effect"
  | "camera_reveal";

export type VisualStoryTransformationSpec = {
  id: string;
  type: VisualStoryTransformationType;
  description: string;
  entity_ids?: string[];
  concept_goal?: string;
};

export type VisualStorySupportingExampleType =
  | "wave"
  | "engine"
  | "tide"
  | "season"
  | "sound"
  | "signal"
  | "orbit"
  | "wheel"
  | "custom";

export type VisualStorySupportingExampleSpec = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  example_type: VisualStorySupportingExampleType;
  why_it_matters: string;

  /** true when the example is actually mentioned in orientation_script. */
  explicitly_mentioned?: boolean;

  /** Optional entity id for a rendered visual echo. */
  entity_id?: string;
};

export type VisualStoryBeat = {

  /** Exact or near-exact slice of story.orientation_script. */

  background_style?: "dark_stage" | "soft_gradient" | "graph_space" | "whiteboard";
  interaction_after_story?: boolean;

};

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { mkdir, readdir, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

type RenderRequest = {
  prompt?: string;
  scene_plan?: unknown;
  frames?: number;
  fps?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
};

type ProcessResult = {
  code: number | null;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
};

const DEFAULT_FRAMES = 1;
const DEFAULT_FPS = 12;
const DEFAULT_WIDTH = 960;
const DEFAULT_HEIGHT = 540;
const MAX_FRAMES = 72;
const MAX_RENDER_MS = 180_000;

function asNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number, min: number, max: number) {
  const numberValue = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(numberValue)) return fallback;
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.round(numberValue)));
}

function sanitizeRenderId(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi, "_").replace(/_+/g, "_").slice(0, 80);
}

function findBlenderExecutable() {
  const envValue = process.env.MYWAY_BLENDER_EXE?.trim();
  if (envValue && existsSync(envValue)) return envValue;

  const candidates = [
    "C:\\Program Files\\Blender Foundation\\Blender 5.1\\blender.exe",
    "C:\\Program Files\\Blender Foundation\\Blender 5.0\\blender.exe",
    "C:\\Program Files\\Blender Foundation\\Blender 4.5\\blender.exe",
    path.join(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA ?? "", "Microsoft", "WindowsApps", "blender.exe"),
  ];

  return candidates.find((candidate) => candidate && existsSync(candidate)) ?? null;
}

function runProcess(command: string, args: string[], cwd: string, timeoutMs: number): Promise<ProcessResult> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(command, args, { cwd, windowsHide: true, shell: false });

    let stdout = "";
    let stderr = "";
    let settled = false;

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      child.kill("SIGTERM");
      reject(new Error(`Blender render timed out after ${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)}s.`));
    }, timeoutMs);

    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
      stdout += String(chunk);
      if (stdout.length > 24_000) stdout = stdout.slice(-24_000);
    });

    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
      stderr += String(chunk);
      if (stderr.length > 24_000) stderr = stderr.slice(-24_000);
    });

    child.on("error", (error) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      reject(error);
    });

    child.on("close", (code) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      resolve({ code, stdout, stderr });
    });
  });
}

export async function GET() {
  const blenderExe = findBlenderExecutable();
  const scriptPath = path.join(process.cwd(), "sandbox", "probe-lab", "scripts", "blender", "render-freeform-scene.py");

  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    route: "blender-playground/render",
    blender: {
      configured_env: process.env.MYWAY_BLENDER_EXE ?? null,
      resolved_exe: blenderExe,
      available: Boolean(blenderExe),
    },
    script: {
      path: scriptPath,
      exists: existsSync(scriptPath),
    },
    output_root: "/sandbox/generated-video-renders",
    mode: "png_frame_sequence",
  });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as RenderRequest;
  const blenderExe = findBlenderExecutable();

  if (!blenderExe) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error: "Blender executable was not found. Set MYWAY_BLENDER_EXE in .env.local to your blender.exe path and restart pnpm dev.",
      },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const scriptPath = path.join(process.cwd(), "sandbox", "probe-lab", "scripts", "blender", "render-freeform-scene.py");
  if (!existsSync(scriptPath)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: `Missing Blender script at ${scriptPath}` }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const frames = asNumber(body.frames, DEFAULT_FRAMES, 1, MAX_FRAMES);
  const fps = asNumber(body.fps, DEFAULT_FPS, 6, 24);
  const width = asNumber(body.width, DEFAULT_WIDTH, 640, 1280);
  const height = asNumber(body.height, DEFAULT_HEIGHT, 360, 720);
  const prompt = body.prompt?.trim() || "Create a monster hiding behind a wall about to scare a kid.";

  const renderId = sanitizeRenderId(`myway_playground_${Date.now()}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`);
  const publicRoot = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "sandbox", "generated-video-renders");
  const renderDir = path.join(publicRoot, renderId);
  const inputPath = path.join(renderDir, "freeform-scene-input.json");

  await rm(renderDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  await mkdir(renderDir, { recursive: true });

  const plan = body.scene_plan ?? { prompt };

  await writeFile(
    inputPath,
    JSON.stringify(
      {
        prompt,
        scene_plan: plan,
        render: { render_id: renderId, frames, fps, width, height },
      },
      null,
      2,
    ),
    "utf8",
  );

  const args = [
    "--background",
    "--python",
    scriptPath,
    "--",
    inputPath,
    renderDir,
    String(frames),
    String(fps),
    String(width),
    String(height),
  ];

  const result = await runProcess(blenderExe, args, process.cwd(), MAX_RENDER_MS);

  if (result.code !== 0) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error: `Blender exited with code ${result.code}.`,
        stdout: result.stdout.slice(-6000),
        stderr: result.stderr.slice(-6000),
        render_id: renderId,
        plan,
      },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }

  const files = (await readdir(renderDir)).filter((file) => /^frame_\d+\.png$/i.test(file)).sort();

  if (!files.length) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error: "Blender finished but did not create any PNG frames.",
        stdout: result.stdout.slice(-6000),
        stderr: result.stderr.slice(-6000),
        render_id: renderId,
        plan,
      },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    render_id: renderId,
    frame_urls: files.map((file) => `/sandbox/generated-video-renders/${renderId}/${file}`),
    frame_count: files.length,
    fps,
    duration_seconds: files.length / fps,
    elapsed_ms: Date.now() - startedAt,
    mode: "png_frame_sequence",
    plan,
    blender: { exe: blenderExe },
    stdout: result.stdout.slice(-5000),
    stderr: result.stderr.slice(-5000),
  });
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

type ScenePlanRequest = {
  prompt?: string;
  model?: string;
};

type NvidiaChatResponse = {
  choices?: Array<{
    message?: {
      content?: unknown;
    };
    finish_reason?: string;
  }>;
  usage?: {
    prompt_tokens?: number;
    completion_tokens?: number;
    total_tokens?: number;
  };
};

const NVIDIA_BASE_URL = process.env.NVIDIA_BASE_URL ?? "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1";
const DEFAULT_NVIDIA_MODEL = process.env.MYWAY_NVIDIA_ANIMATION_MODEL ?? "nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b";
const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 240_000;

function contentToString(content: unknown) {
  if (typeof content === "string") return content;
  if (Array.isArray(content)) {
    return content
      .map((part) => {
        if (typeof part === "string") return part;
        if (part && typeof part === "object") {
          const record = part as Record<string, unknown>;
          if (typeof record.text === "string") return record.text;
          if (typeof record.content === "string") return record.content;
        }
        return "";
      })
      .join("")
      .trim();
  }
  return "";
}

function stripCodeFence(value: string) {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed.startsWith("```")) return trimmed;
  return trimmed.replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, "").replace(/\s*```$/i, "").trim();
}

function safeParseJson(content: string) {
  const stripped = stripCodeFence(content);
  try {
    return JSON.parse(stripped) as unknown;
  } catch (error) {
    const firstBrace = stripped.indexOf("{");
    const lastBrace = stripped.lastIndexOf("}");
    if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
      return JSON.parse(stripped.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1)) as unknown;
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

function fetchWithTimeout(url: string, init: RequestInit, timeoutMs: number) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  return fetch(url, { ...init, signal: controller.signal }).finally(() => clearTimeout(timeout));
}

        animation: "walks slightly forward during the shot",

    objects: [

function buildSystemPrompt() {
  return [
    "You are a cinematic Blender scene planner for a local sandbox.",
    "Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown. No comments. No executable code.",
    "You are not writing Blender Python. You are describing a safe scene that a trusted Blender script can render.",
    "Your output should be much more specific than a normal image prompt. Give Blender-renderable direction for camera, composition, lighting, materials, environment, subjects, effects, and animation beats.",
    "Do not assume external assets. Use primitive-friendly geometry unless the prompt asks otherwise.",
    "Prefer high readability: clear silhouettes, strong foreground/background separation, and one focal subject.",
    "For cinematic freeform tests, avoid floating labels unless the user explicitly asks for text labels. The image itself should carry the scene.",
    "Use stylized, non-graphic, readable scenes. No gore. No horror violence. A playful scare is fine.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildUserPrompt(prompt: string) {
  return [
    "Create a rich Blender-friendly scene plan for this freeform prompt:",
    prompt,
    "",
    "Return exactly one JSON object with this shape:",
    "schema_version: \"myway_blender_playground_scene_v2\"",
    "title: short title",
    "prompt: original prompt",
    "scene_intent: { read, cinematic_goal, avoid: string[] }",
    "render_quality: { target, engine, resolution, samples, use_depth_of_field, use_volumetric_fog, use_beveled_geometry, use_reflective_floor, text_labels }",
    "composition: { layout, focal_subject, silhouette_priority, negative_space }",
    "camera: { lens_mm, framing, angle, focus_target, start: { position, look_at }, end: { position, look_at }, motion }",
    "environment: { kind, floor, walls, atmosphere, background }",
    "subjects: 1 to 4 objects with { id, kind, role, position_hint, pose, expression, color_hint, scale, material_notes, animation }",
    "objects: 0 to 8 objects with { id, kind, position_hint, purpose, material_notes }",
    "lighting: { key, fill, rim, practical, shadow_strength }",
    "effects: { fog, bloom, depth_of_field, vignette }",
    "animation_beats: exactly 3 beats with { frame_range, action }",
    "blender_tools_requested: 5 to 12 strings naming safe Blender tools/features to use",
    "",
    "Allowed subject kinds: monster, child, person, robot, creature, animal, abstract_character.",
    "Allowed object kinds: wall_corner, doorway_light, doorway, table, chair, window, box, machine, tree, rock, generic_prop.",
    "Allowed position hints: front_left, front_right, center, back_left, back_right, center_right, center_left.",
    "Allowed text_labels values: none, minimal, requested.",
    "Keep all scene content safe and non-graphic.",
    "Return JSON only.",
  ].join("\n");
}

export async function GET() {
  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    route: "blender-playground/scene-plan",
    schema_version: "myway_blender_playground_scene_v2",
    default_model: DEFAULT_NVIDIA_MODEL,
    has_nvidia_api_key: Boolean(process.env.NVIDIA_API_KEY?.trim()),
  });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as ScenePlanRequest;
  const prompt = body.prompt?.trim() || "Create a monster hiding behind a wall about to scare a kid.";
  const model = body.model?.trim() || DEFAULT_NVIDIA_MODEL;
  const apiKey = process.env.NVIDIA_API_KEY;

  if (!apiKey?.trim()) {
    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      fallback: true,
      warning: "Missing NVIDIA_API_KEY, so the sandbox returned a deterministic fallback scene plan.",
      plan: fallbackPlan(prompt),
    });
  }

  const startedAt = Date.now();

  try {
    const response = await fetchWithTimeout(
      `${NVIDIA_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
          "content-type": "application/json",
          accept: "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model,
          temperature: 0.18,
          top_p: 0.86,
          max_tokens: 3600,
          response_format: { type: "json_object" },
          chat_template_kwargs: { enable_thinking: false },
          messages: [
            { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt() },
            { role: "user", content: buildUserPrompt(prompt) },
          ],
        }),
      },
      REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS,
    );

    const elapsedMs = Date.now() - startedAt;

    if (!response.ok) {
      const detail = await response.text().catch(() => "");
      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        fallback: true,
        warning: `NVIDIA request failed (${response.status}); returned fallback scene plan.`,
        detail: detail.slice(0, 800),
        plan: fallbackPlan(prompt),
      });
    }

    const data = (await response.json()) as NvidiaChatResponse;
    const content = contentToString(data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
    const plan = safeParseJson(content);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      fallback: false,
      provider: "nvidia",
      model,
      plan,
      debug: {
        elapsed_ms: elapsedMs,
        finish_reason: data.choices?.[0]?.finish_reason ?? null,
        usage: data.usage ?? null,
        raw_content_preview: content.slice(0, 1600),
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      fallback: true,
      warning: "Scene-plan generation failed; returned fallback scene plan.",
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown scene-plan error",
      plan: fallbackPlan(prompt),
    });
  }
}

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { mkdir, readdir, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { compileVideoDirectorToProceduralPlan } from "@/sandbox/probe-lab/ui/learning-space/probes/generated-video/procedural-compiler";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

type BlenderRenderRequest = {
  director_contract?: unknown;
  request_context?: unknown;
  procedural_visual_plan?: unknown;
  frames?: number;
  fps?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
};

type ProcessResult = {
  code: number | null;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
};

const DEFAULT_FRAMES = 48;
const DEFAULT_FPS = 12;
const DEFAULT_WIDTH = 960;
const DEFAULT_HEIGHT = 540;
const MAX_FRAMES = 96;
const MAX_RENDER_MS = 180_000;

function asNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number, min: number, max: number) {
  const numberValue = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(numberValue)) return fallback;
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.round(numberValue)));
}

function sanitizeRenderId(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi, "_").replace(/_+/g, "_").slice(0, 80);
}

function findBlenderExecutable() {
  const envValue = process.env.MYWAY_BLENDER_EXE?.trim();
  if (envValue && existsSync(envValue)) return envValue;

  const candidates = [
    "C:\\Program Files\\Blender Foundation\\Blender 5.1\\blender.exe",
    "C:\\Program Files\\Blender Foundation\\Blender 5.0\\blender.exe",
    "C:\\Program Files\\Blender Foundation\\Blender 4.5\\blender.exe",
    path.join(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA ?? "", "Microsoft", "WindowsApps", "blender.exe"),
  ];

  return candidates.find((candidate) => candidate && existsSync(candidate)) ?? null;
}

function runProcess(command: string, args: string[], cwd: string, timeoutMs: number): Promise<ProcessResult> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(command, args, {
      cwd,
      windowsHide: true,
      shell: false,
    });

    let stdout = "";
    let stderr = "";
    let settled = false;

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      child.kill("SIGTERM");
      reject(new Error(`Blender render timed out after ${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)}s.`));
    }, timeoutMs);

    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
      stdout += String(chunk);
      if (stdout.length > 24_000) stdout = stdout.slice(-24_000);
    });

    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
      stderr += String(chunk);
      if (stderr.length > 24_000) stderr = stderr.slice(-24_000);
    });

    child.on("error", (error) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      reject(error);
    });

    child.on("close", (code) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      resolve({ code, stdout, stderr });
    });
  });
}

export async function GET() {
  const blenderExe = findBlenderExecutable();
  const scriptPath = path.join(process.cwd(), "sandbox", "probe-lab", "scripts", "blender", "render-myway-director.py");

  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    route: "blender-render",
    blender: {
      configured_env: process.env.MYWAY_BLENDER_EXE ?? null,
      resolved_exe: blenderExe,
      available: Boolean(blenderExe),
    },
    script: {
      path: scriptPath,
      exists: existsSync(scriptPath),
    },
    output_root: "/sandbox/generated-video-renders",
    mode: "png_frame_sequence",
    compiler: {
      enabled: true,
      schema_version: "myway_procedural_visual_plan_v1",
      description:
        "The route compiles model director JSON into safe procedural asset requests before calling the trusted Blender script.",
    },
  });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as BlenderRenderRequest;
  const blenderExe = findBlenderExecutable();

  if (!blenderExe) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Blender executable was not found. Set MYWAY_BLENDER_EXE in .env.local to your blender.exe path and restart pnpm dev.",
      },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const scriptPath = path.join(process.cwd(), "sandbox", "probe-lab", "scripts", "blender", "render-myway-director.py");
  if (!existsSync(scriptPath)) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error: `Missing Blender script at ${scriptPath}`,
      },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }

  const frames = asNumber(body.frames, DEFAULT_FRAMES, 12, MAX_FRAMES);
  const fps = asNumber(body.fps, DEFAULT_FPS, 6, 24);
  const width = asNumber(body.width, DEFAULT_WIDTH, 640, 1280);
  const height = asNumber(body.height, DEFAULT_HEIGHT, 360, 720);

  const proceduralVisualPlan =
    body.procedural_visual_plan ??
    compileVideoDirectorToProceduralPlan({
      directorContract: body.director_contract,
      requestContext: body.request_context,
      render: { frames, fps, width, height },
    });

  const renderId = sanitizeRenderId(`myway_blender_${Date.now()}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`);
  const publicRoot = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "sandbox", "generated-video-renders");
  const renderDir = path.join(publicRoot, renderId);
  const inputPath = path.join(renderDir, "director-input.json");

  await rm(renderDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  await mkdir(renderDir, { recursive: true });

  await writeFile(
    inputPath,
    JSON.stringify(
      {
        director_contract: body.director_contract ?? null,
        request_context: body.request_context ?? null,
        procedural_visual_plan: proceduralVisualPlan,
        render: {
          render_id: renderId,
          frames,
          fps,
          width,
          height,
        },
      },
      null,
      2,
    ),
    "utf8",
  );

  const args = [
    "--background",
    "--python",
    scriptPath,
    "--",
    inputPath,
    renderDir,
    String(frames),
    String(fps),
    String(width),
    String(height),
  ];

  const result = await runProcess(blenderExe, args, process.cwd(), MAX_RENDER_MS);

  if (result.code !== 0) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error: `Blender exited with code ${result.code}.`,
        stdout: result.stdout.slice(-6000),
        stderr: result.stderr.slice(-6000),
        render_id: renderId,
        procedural_visual_plan: proceduralVisualPlan,
      },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }

  const files = (await readdir(renderDir))
    .filter((file) => /^frame_\d+\.png$/i.test(file))
    .sort();

  if (!files.length) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        error: "Blender finished but did not create any PNG frames.",
        stdout: result.stdout.slice(-6000),
        stderr: result.stderr.slice(-6000),
        render_id: renderId,
        procedural_visual_plan: proceduralVisualPlan,
      },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    render_id: renderId,
    frame_urls: files.map((file) => `/sandbox/generated-video-renders/${renderId}/${file}`),
    frame_count: files.length,
    fps,
    duration_seconds: files.length / fps,
    elapsed_ms: Date.now() - startedAt,
    mode: "png_frame_sequence",
    compiler: {
      enabled: true,
      schema_version: "myway_procedural_visual_plan_v1",
    },
    procedural_visual_plan: proceduralVisualPlan,
    blender: {
      exe: blenderExe,
    },
    stdout: result.stdout.slice(-5000),
    stderr: result.stderr.slice(-5000),
  });
}

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import {
  buildEngineReelContract,
  normalizeEngineReelContract,
  stableContractId,
  type EngineReelContract,
} from "@/sandbox/probe-lab/ui/learning-space/probes/generated-video/remotion-reel/engine-reel-contract";

type OllamaContractRequest = {
  learner_signal?: string;
  model?: string;
};

type OllamaChatResponse = {
  message?: {
    content?: unknown;
  };
  done?: boolean;
  done_reason?: string;
  total_duration?: number;
  load_duration?: number;
  prompt_eval_count?: number;
  eval_count?: number;
};

const OLLAMA_URL = process.env.OLLAMA_BASE_URL ?? "http://127.0.0.1:11434";
const DEFAULT_MODEL = process.env.MYWAY_OLLAMA_ANIMATION_MODEL ?? "qwen2.5:3b";
const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 420_000;
const PING_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;

function buildSystemPrompt() {
  return [
    "You are MyWay's Animation Contract Generator.",
    "Return only valid JSON. No markdown. No comments.",
    "You are not writing React code. You are directing a trusted Remotion composition with JSON.",
    "Keep language clear for a learner. Avoid jargon unless the learner already used it.",
    "Create a small renderable video_explanation director contract.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildUserPrompt(learnerSignal: string) {
  return [
    `Learner message: ${learnerSignal}`,
    "Return only a compact JSON object with these keys:",
    "title, diagnosis_guess, learning_goal, scenes, checkpoint.",
    "scenes must be exactly 3 short objects with: id, kind, title, caption, focus.",
    "Allowed scene kind values: overview, ignition_power, checkpoint.",
    "Captions must be under 18 words each.",
    "Use the engine cutaway as the visual. Explain the missing cause/effect link.",
    "Do not include markdown. Do not include extra prose.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function stripCodeFence(value: string) {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed.startsWith("```")) return trimmed;
  return trimmed
    .replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, "")
    .replace(/\s*```$/i, "")
    .trim();
}

function safeParseJson(content: string) {
  const stripped = stripCodeFence(content);
  try {
    return JSON.parse(stripped) as unknown;
  } catch (error) {
    const firstBrace = stripped.indexOf("{");
    const lastBrace = stripped.lastIndexOf("}");
    if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
      return JSON.parse(stripped.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1)) as unknown;
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

async function fetchWithTimeout(url: string, init: RequestInit, timeoutMs: number) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

  try {
    return await fetch(url, {
      ...init,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}

function normalizeOllamaDraft(args: {
  draft: unknown;
  model: string;
  learnerSignal: string;
}): EngineReelContract {
  const draftRecord = args.draft && typeof args.draft === "object" ? (args.draft as Record<string, unknown>) : {};

  return normalizeEngineReelContract({
    ...draftRecord,
    schema_version: "myway_engine_reel_remotion_contract_v1",
    contract_id:
      typeof draftRecord.contract_id === "string" && draftRecord.contract_id.trim()
        ? draftRecord.contract_id
        : stableContractId(`${args.model}:${args.learnerSignal}:${JSON.stringify(draftRecord).slice(0, 280)}`),
    learner_signal: args.learnerSignal,
  });
}

async function callOllama(args: { learnerSignal: string; model: string }) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  const response = await fetchWithTimeout(
    `${OLLAMA_URL}/api/chat`,
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: args.model,
        stream: false,
        keep_alive: "10m",
        // Important: use lightweight JSON mode here instead of a huge strict JSON schema.
        // The sandbox normalizer below turns the model's compact draft into a full trusted contract.
        format: "json",
        options: {
          temperature: 0.1,
          top_p: 0.85,
          num_ctx: 1024,
          num_predict: 360,
        },
        messages: [
          { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt() },
          { role: "user", content: buildUserPrompt(args.learnerSignal) },
        ],
      }),
    },
    REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS,
  );

  const elapsed_ms = Date.now() - startedAt;

  if (!response.ok) {
    const detail = await response.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Ollama request failed (${response.status}) after ${elapsed_ms}ms. ${detail.slice(0, 420)}`);
  }

  const data = (await response.json()) as OllamaChatResponse;
  const content = data?.message?.content;

  if (typeof content !== "string" || !content.trim()) {
    throw new Error(`Ollama response did not include usable message.content after ${elapsed_ms}ms.`);
  }

  const draft = safeParseJson(content);
  const contract = normalizeOllamaDraft({ draft, model: args.model, learnerSignal: args.learnerSignal });

  return {
    contract,
    meta: {
      elapsed_ms,
      done_reason: data.done_reason ?? null,
      prompt_eval_count: data.prompt_eval_count ?? null,
      eval_count: data.eval_count ?? null,
      raw_content_preview: content.slice(0, 360),
    },
  };
}

function formatOllamaError(error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError" || /aborted/i.test(error.message)) {
      return `Ollama generation timed out or was aborted after ${Math.round(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)}s. The model may still be too slow on CPU for this prompt.`;
    }
    return error.message;
  }

  return "Unknown Ollama error";
}

export async function GET() {
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  try {
    const response = await fetchWithTimeout(`${OLLAMA_URL}/api/tags`, { method: "GET" }, PING_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const text = await response.text();

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: response.ok,
      ollama_base_url: OLLAMA_URL,
      default_model: DEFAULT_MODEL,
      status: response.status,
      elapsed_ms: Date.now() - startedAt,
      preview: text.slice(0, 800),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        ollama_base_url: OLLAMA_URL,
        default_model: DEFAULT_MODEL,
        elapsed_ms: Date.now() - startedAt,
        error: error instanceof Error ? `${error.name}: ${error.message}` : "Unknown ping error",
      },
      { status: 200 },
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as OllamaContractRequest;
  const learnerSignal = body.learner_signal?.trim() || "I do not get how a gas engine makes the piston move.";
  const model = body.model?.trim() || DEFAULT_MODEL;

  try {
    const result = await callOllama({ learnerSignal, model });

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      source: "ollama",
      model,
      contract: result.contract,
      warnings: [
        `Ollama generated a compact JSON draft in ${Math.round(result.meta.elapsed_ms / 1000)}s; MyWay normalized it into the trusted Remotion contract.`,
      ],
      debug: result.meta,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const fallbackContract = buildEngineReelContract(learnerSignal);
    const message = formatOllamaError(error);

    console.error("[myway-ollama-contract]", message);

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        source: "fallback",
        model,
        error: message,
        contract: fallbackContract,
        warnings: [
          "Ollama did not return a usable contract, so the sandbox returned the deterministic fallback contract.",
          "This route now uses lightweight JSON mode. If this still fails, check the pnpm dev terminal for [myway-ollama-contract].",
        ],
      },
      { status: 200 },
    );
  }
}

function stripCodeFence(value: string) {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed.startsWith("```")) return trimmed;
  return trimmed
    .replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, "")
    .replace(/\s*```$/i, "")
    .trim();
}

function safeParseJson(content: string) {
  const stripped = stripCodeFence(content);
  try {
    return JSON.parse(stripped) as unknown;
  } catch (error) {
    const firstBrace = stripped.indexOf("{");
    const lastBrace = stripped.lastIndexOf("}");
    if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
      return JSON.parse(stripped.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1)) as unknown;
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

function contentToString(content: unknown) {
  if (typeof content === "string") return content;

  if (Array.isArray(content)) {
    return content
      .map((part) => {
        if (typeof part === "string") return part;
        if (part && typeof part === "object") {
          const record = part as Record<string, unknown>;
          if (typeof record.text === "string") return record.text;
          if (typeof record.content === "string") return record.content;
        }
        return "";
      })
      .join("")
      .trim();
  }

  return "";
}

async function fetchWithTimeout(url: string, init: RequestInit, timeoutMs: number) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

  try {
    return await fetch(url, {
      ...init,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}

function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
}

function buildUniversalSceneFromDirector(args: {
  draftRecord: Record<string, unknown>;
  directorContract: MyWayVideoDirectorContract;
  context: VideoDirectorRequestContext;
  provider: UniversalSceneProvider;
  model: string;
}): UniversalSceneContract {
  const universalDraft =
    args.draftRecord.universal_scene_contract ??
    args.draftRecord.universalSceneContract ??
    args.draftRecord.universal_scene ??
    args.draftRecord.scene_graph ??
    args.draftRecord.sceneGraph ??
    args.draftRecord;

  const universalRecord = asRecord(universalDraft);
  const director = args.directorContract;

  return normalizeUniversalSceneContract(
    {
      ...universalRecord,
      schema_version: "myway_universal_scene_contract_v1",
      contract_id:
        typeof universalRecord.contract_id === "string" && universalRecord.contract_id.trim()
          ? universalRecord.contract_id
          : stableUniversalSceneContractId(
              `${args.provider}:${args.model}:${args.context.learner_message}:${director.contract_id}:${JSON.stringify(universalRecord).slice(0, 600)}`,
            ),
      title: universalRecord.title ?? director.title,
      learner_signal: args.context.learner_message,
      diagnosis_guess:
        universalRecord.diagnosis_guess ??
        director.learning_context.root_problem ??
        "The learner needs the hidden relationship turned into visible motion.",
      learning_goal:
        universalRecord.learning_goal ??
        director.creative_brief.why_this_should_unstick_the_learner ??
        "Show the missing link with an animated scene and a checkpoint.",
      director_plan: universalRecord.director_plan ?? {
        visual_intent: director.creative_brief.visual_metaphor,
        misconception_target: director.learning_context.misconception_target ?? "The current mental model is missing a relationship.",
        key_relationships: director.relationships.map((relationship) => relationship.explanation).slice(0, 5),
        design_principles: [
          director.creative_brief.aha_moment,
          "Use motion to reveal the hidden relationship.",
          "Keep personalization clarifying rather than decorative.",
        ],
      },
      checkpoint: universalRecord.checkpoint ?? director.checkpoint,
    },
    args.context.learner_message,
  );
}

function normalizeProviderDraft(args: {
  draft: unknown;
  model: string;
  provider: UniversalSceneProvider;
  context: VideoDirectorRequestContext;
}): { contract: UniversalSceneContract; director_contract: MyWayVideoDirectorContract } {
  const draftRecord = asRecord(args.draft);
  const directorDraft =
    draftRecord.director_contract ??
    draftRecord.directorContract ??
    draftRecord.video_director_contract ??
    draftRecord.videoDirectorContract ??
    draftRecord;

  const directorRecord = asRecord(directorDraft);
  const existingContractId = directorRecord.contract_id;

  const director_contract = normalizeVideoDirectorContract(
    {
      ...directorRecord,
      schema_version: "myway_video_director_contract_v1",
      contract_id:
        typeof existingContractId === "string" && existingContractId.trim()
          ? existingContractId
          : stableVideoDirectorContractId(`${args.provider}:${args.model}:${args.context.learner_message}:${JSON.stringify(draftRecord).slice(0, 700)}`),
    },
    args.context,
  );

  const contract = buildUniversalSceneFromDirector({
    draftRecord,
    directorContract: director_contract,
    context: args.context,
    provider: args.provider,
    model: args.model,
  });

  return { contract, director_contract };
}

async function callOllama(args: { context: VideoDirectorRequestContext; model: string }): Promise<ProviderGenerationResult> {
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  const response = await fetchWithTimeout(
    `${OLLAMA_URL}/api/chat`,
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: args.model,
        stream: false,
        keep_alive: "10m",
        format: "json",
        options: {
          temperature: 0.12,
          top_p: 0.85,
          num_ctx: 4096,
          num_predict: 2600,
        },
        messages: [
          { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt() },
          { role: "user", content: buildUserPrompt(args.context) },
        ],
      }),
    },
    REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS,
  );

  const elapsed_ms = Date.now() - startedAt;

  if (!response.ok) {
    const detail = await response.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Ollama request failed (${response.status}) after ${elapsed_ms}ms. ${detail.slice(0, 640)}`);
  }

  const data = (await response.json()) as OllamaChatResponse;
  const content = contentToString(data?.message?.content);

  if (!content.trim()) {
    throw new Error(`Ollama response did not include usable message.content after ${elapsed_ms}ms.`);
  }

  const draft = safeParseJson(content);
  const normalized = normalizeProviderDraft({
    draft,
    provider: "ollama",
    model: args.model,
    context: args.context,
  });

  return {
    ...normalized,
    meta: {
      elapsed_ms,
      done_reason: data.done_reason ?? null,
      prompt_eval_count: data.prompt_eval_count ?? null,
      eval_count: data.eval_count ?? null,
      raw_content_preview: content.slice(0, 1100),
    },
  };
}

async function callNvidia(args: { context: VideoDirectorRequestContext; model: string }): Promise<ProviderGenerationResult> {
  const apiKey = process.env.NVIDIA_API_KEY;

  if (!apiKey?.trim()) {
    throw new Error("Missing NVIDIA_API_KEY. Add it to .env.local and restart pnpm dev.");
  }

  const startedAt = Date.now();

  const response = await fetchWithTimeout(
    `${NVIDIA_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`,
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
        "content-type": "application/json",
        accept: "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: args.model,
        temperature: 0,
        top_p: 0.82,
        max_tokens: 3600,
        response_format: { type: "json_object" },
        chat_template_kwargs: {
          enable_thinking: false,
        },
        messages: [
          { role: "system", content: buildSystemPrompt() },
          { role: "user", content: buildUserPrompt(args.context) },
        ],
      }),
    },
    REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS,
  );

  const elapsed_ms = Date.now() - startedAt;

  if (!response.ok) {
    const detail = await response.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`NVIDIA request failed (${response.status}) after ${elapsed_ms}ms. ${detail.slice(0, 900)}`);
  }

  const data = (await response.json()) as NvidiaChatResponse;
  const content = contentToString(data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);

  if (!content.trim()) {
    throw new Error(`NVIDIA response did not include usable choices[0].message.content after ${elapsed_ms}ms.`);
  }

  const draft = safeParseJson(content);
  const normalized = normalizeProviderDraft({
    draft,
    provider: "nvidia",
    model: args.model,
    context: args.context,
  });

  return {
    ...normalized,
    meta: {
      elapsed_ms,
      finish_reason: data.choices?.[0]?.finish_reason ?? null,
      prompt_tokens: data.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? null,
      completion_tokens: data.usage?.completion_tokens ?? null,
      total_tokens: data.usage?.total_tokens ?? null,
      raw_content_preview: content.slice(0, 1400),
    },
  };
}

function normalizeProvider(value: unknown, model: string): UniversalSceneProvider {
  if (value === "nvidia" || model.startsWith("nvidia/")) return "nvidia";
  return "ollama";
}

function formatProviderError(error: unknown, provider: UniversalSceneProvider) {
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError" || /aborted/i.test(error.message)) {
      return `${provider} video-director generation timed out or was aborted after ${Math.round(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)}s.`;
    }
    return error.message;
  }

  return `Unknown ${provider} error`;
}

export async function GET() {
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  let ollamaStatus: unknown = null;

  try {
    const response = await fetchWithTimeout(`${OLLAMA_URL}/api/tags`, { method: "GET" }, PING_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const text = await response.text();
    ollamaStatus = {
      ok: response.ok,
      status: response.status,
      preview: text.slice(0, 800),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    ollamaStatus = {
      ok: false,
      error: error instanceof Error ? `${error.name}: ${error.message}` : "Unknown Ollama ping error",
    };
  }

  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    route: "universal-scene-contract",
    mode: "video-director-plus-universal-scene",
    elapsed_ms: Date.now() - startedAt,
    default_context: buildDefaultVideoDirectorRequestContext("I am stuck on this idea."),
    providers: {
      ollama: {
        base_url: OLLAMA_URL,
        default_model: DEFAULT_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        status: ollamaStatus,
      },
      nvidia: {
        base_url: NVIDIA_BASE_URL,
        default_model: DEFAULT_NVIDIA_MODEL,
        has_api_key: Boolean(process.env.NVIDIA_API_KEY?.trim()),
      },
    },
  });
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const rawBody = (await request.json().catch(() => ({}))) as UniversalSceneRequest;
  const learnerMessage =
    rawBody.learner_message?.trim() ||
    rawBody.learner_signal?.trim() ||
    "I do not understand why a baseball runner has to tag up.";

  const context = normalizeVideoDirectorRequestContext({
    ...rawBody,
    learner_message: learnerMessage,
  });

  const requestedModel = rawBody.model?.trim() || "";
  const provider = normalizeProvider(rawBody.provider, requestedModel);
  const model = requestedModel || (provider === "nvidia" ? DEFAULT_NVIDIA_MODEL : DEFAULT_OLLAMA_MODEL);

  try {
    const result =
      provider === "nvidia"
        ? await callNvidia({ context, model })
        : await callOllama({ context, model });

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      source: provider,
      provider,
      model,
      contract: result.contract,
      director_contract: result.director_contract,
      context,
      warnings: [
        `${provider} generated a video director contract in ${Math.round(
          Number(result.meta.elapsed_ms ?? 0) / 1000,
        )}s; MyWay normalized it for trusted 3D/hybrid rendering plus 2D fallback.`,
      ],
      debug: result.meta,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const director_contract = buildFallbackVideoDirectorContract(context);
    const contract = buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(context.learner_message);
    const message = formatProviderError(error, provider);

    console.error("[myway-video-director-contract]", provider, message);

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok: false,
        source: "fallback",
        provider,
        model,
        error: message,
        contract,
        director_contract,
        context,
        warnings: [
          `${provider} did not return a usable video director contract, so the sandbox returned MyWay's deterministic fallback contracts.`,
          "This route now expects high-level director JSON plus a universal 2D fallback scene. Check the pnpm dev terminal for [myway-video-director-contract].",
        ],
      },
      { status: 200 },
    );
  }
}
