export type UniversalSceneTone =
  | "purple"
  | "blue"
  | "cyan"
  | "green"
  | "yellow"
  | "orange"
  | "red"
  | "pink"
  | "gray"
  | "white";

export type UniversalSceneElementKind =
  | "card"
  | "rect"
  | "circle"
  | "ellipse"
  | "polygon"
  | "line"
  | "arrow"
  | "path"
  | "text"
  | "icon"
  | "highlight";

export type UniversalSceneActionKind =
  | "appear"
  | "fade"
  | "move_to"
  | "highlight"
  | "pulse"
  | "draw"
  | "scale_to";

export type UniversalScenePoint = [number, number];

export type UniversalSceneElement = {
  id: string;
  kind: UniversalSceneElementKind;
  label?: string;
  text?: string;
  icon?: string;
  x?: number;
  y?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  radius?: number;
  rx?: number;
  ry?: number;
  points?: UniversalScenePoint[];
  from?: UniversalScenePoint;
  to?: UniversalScenePoint;
  d?: string;
  tone?: UniversalSceneTone;
  opacity?: number;
  rotation?: number;
  scale?: number;
  layer?: number;
};

export type UniversalSceneAction = {
  target_id: string;
  kind: UniversalSceneActionKind;
  startFrame?: number;
  endFrame?: number;
  to?: {
    x?: number;
    y?: number;
    opacity?: number;
    scale?: number;
    rotation?: number;
  };
  label?: string;
};

export type UniversalSceneBeat = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  caption: string;
  durationInFrames: number;
  focus: string;
  visible_element_ids?: string[];
  spotlight_element_ids?: string[];
  actions?: UniversalSceneAction[];
};

export type UniversalSceneDirectorPlan = {
  visual_intent: string;
  misconception_target: string;
  key_relationships: string[];
  design_principles: string[];
};

export type UniversalSceneContract = {
  schema_version: "myway_universal_scene_contract_v1";
  contract_id: string;
  title: string;
  learner_signal: string;
  diagnosis_guess: string;
  learning_goal: string;
  director_plan: UniversalSceneDirectorPlan;
  format: {
    width: number;
    height: number;
    fps: number;
    aspect: "16:9_laptop";
  };
  renderer_strategy: {
    renderer: "Remotion Player";
    composition: "UniversalSceneRemotionComposition";
    model_writes_code: false;
    model_directs_scene_graph: true;
    explanation: string;
  };
  scene_style: {
    visual_language: string;
    background: "deep_space" | "paper" | "chalkboard" | "clean_room";
    notes: string[];
  };
  elements: UniversalSceneElement[];
  scenes: UniversalSceneBeat[];
  checkpoint: {
    prompt: string;
    expected_idea: string;
  };
  safety_notes: string[];
};

export const UNIVERSAL_SCENE_FPS = 30;
export const UNIVERSAL_SCENE_WIDTH = 1280;
export const UNIVERSAL_SCENE_HEIGHT = 720;

const SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN = 110;
const SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX = 1170;
const SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN = 135;
const SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX = 585;

const ELEMENT_KINDS = new Set<UniversalSceneElementKind>([
  "card",
  "rect",
  "circle",
  "ellipse",
  "polygon",
  "line",
  "arrow",
  "path",
  "text",
  "icon",
  "highlight",
]);

const ACTION_KINDS = new Set<UniversalSceneActionKind>([
  "appear",
  "fade",
  "move_to",
  "highlight",
  "pulse",
  "draw",
  "scale_to",
]);

const TONES = new Set<UniversalSceneTone>([
  "purple",
  "blue",
  "cyan",
  "green",
  "yellow",
  "orange",
  "red",
  "pink",
  "gray",
  "white",
]);

export function seconds(value: number) {
  return Math.round(value * UNIVERSAL_SCENE_FPS);
}

export function stableUniversalSceneContractId(input: string) {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let index = 0; index < input.length; index += 1) {
    hash = (hash * 31 + input.charCodeAt(index)) >>> 0;
  }
  return `universal_scene_contract_${hash.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
}

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
}

function asText(value: unknown, fallback: string, maxLength = 240) {
  if (typeof value !== "string") return fallback;
  const trimmed = value.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (!trimmed) return fallback;
  return trimmed.slice(0, maxLength);
}

function asNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number, min: number, max: number) {
  const numberValue = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(numberValue)) return fallback;
  return clamp(numberValue, min, max);
}

function asInteger(value: unknown, fallback: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.round(asNumber(value, fallback, min, max));
}

function asStringArray(value: unknown, fallback: string[], maxItems = 12, maxLength = 90) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return fallback;
  const cleaned = value
    .map((item) => asText(item, "", maxLength))
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, maxItems);
  return cleaned.length ? cleaned : fallback;
}

function asTone(value: unknown, fallback: UniversalSceneTone): UniversalSceneTone {
  return typeof value === "string" && TONES.has(value as UniversalSceneTone)
    ? (value as UniversalSceneTone)
    : fallback;
}

function asElementKind(value: unknown, fallback: UniversalSceneElementKind): UniversalSceneElementKind {
  return typeof value === "string" && ELEMENT_KINDS.has(value as UniversalSceneElementKind)
    ? (value as UniversalSceneElementKind)
    : fallback;
}

function asActionKind(value: unknown, fallback: UniversalSceneActionKind): UniversalSceneActionKind {
  return typeof value === "string" && ACTION_KINDS.has(value as UniversalSceneActionKind)
    ? (value as UniversalSceneActionKind)
    : fallback;
}

function sanitizeId(value: unknown, fallback: string) {
  return asText(value, fallback, 60).replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi, "_").replace(/_+/g, "_").toLowerCase();
}

function asPoint(value: unknown, fallback: UniversalScenePoint): UniversalScenePoint {
  if (Array.isArray(value) && value.length >= 2) {
    return [
      asNumber(value[0], fallback[0], SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX),
      asNumber(value[1], fallback[1], SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX),
    ];
  }

  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    return [
      asNumber(record.x, fallback[0], SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX),
      asNumber(record.y, fallback[1], SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX),
    ];
  }

  return [
    asNumber(fallback[0], 640, SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX),
    asNumber(fallback[1], 360, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX),
  ];
}

function normalizePoints(value: unknown, fallback: UniversalScenePoint[]): UniversalScenePoint[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return fallback;
  const points = value.slice(0, 12).map((point, index) => asPoint(point, fallback[index] ?? [640, 360]));
  return points.length >= 3 ? points : fallback;
}


function sanitizePathD(value: unknown): string | undefined {
  const d = asText(value, "", 280);
  if (!d) return undefined;

  const numbers = Array.from(d.matchAll(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)).map((match) => Number(match[0]));
  if (!numbers.length) return undefined;

  const hasClearlyOffCanvasNumber = numbers.some((number) => number < 0 || number > UNIVERSAL_SCENE_WIDTH);
  if (hasClearlyOffCanvasNumber) return undefined;

  return d;
}

function clampBoxCoordinate(args: {
  value: unknown;
  fallback: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  size: number;
}) {
  return asNumber(args.value, args.fallback, args.min, Math.max(args.min, args.max - args.size));
}

function normalizeElement(raw: unknown, index: number): UniversalSceneElement | null {
  const record = asRecord(raw);
  if (!Object.keys(record).length) return null;

  const kind = asElementKind(record.kind ?? record.type, index % 5 === 0 ? "card" : "rect");
  const id = sanitizeId(record.id, `element_${index + 1}`);
  const width = asNumber(record.width, kind === "text" ? 320 : kind === "card" ? 230 : 150, 12, 640);
  const height = asNumber(record.height, kind === "text" ? 70 : kind === "card" ? 120 : 90, 12, 380);
  const fallbackX = 190 + (index % 4) * 250;
  const fallbackY = 175 + Math.floor(index / 4) * 120;
  const usesTopLeft = kind === "card" || kind === "rect" || kind === "highlight";
  const x = usesTopLeft
    ? clampBoxCoordinate({ value: record.x, fallback: fallbackX, min: SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, max: SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX, size: width })
    : asNumber(record.x, fallbackX, SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX);
  const y = usesTopLeft
    ? clampBoxCoordinate({ value: record.y, fallback: fallbackY, min: SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, max: SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX, size: height })
    : asNumber(record.y, fallbackY, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX);

  return {
    id,
    kind,
    label: asText(record.label, "", 80) || undefined,
    text: asText(record.text, "", 120) || undefined,
    icon: asText(record.icon, "", 38) || undefined,
    x,
    y,
    width,
    height,
    radius: asNumber(record.radius ?? record.r, kind === "circle" ? 42 : 24, 4, 120),
    rx: asNumber(record.rx, width / 2, 4, 220),
    ry: asNumber(record.ry, height / 2, 4, 160),
    points: normalizePoints(record.points, [
      [x, Math.max(SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, y - 60)],
      [Math.min(SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX, x + 90), y],
      [x, Math.min(SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX, y + 60)],
      [Math.max(SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, x - 90), y],
    ]),
    from: asPoint(record.from, [x, y]),
    to: asPoint(record.to, [Math.min(SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX, x + 180), y]),
    d: sanitizePathD(record.d),
    tone: asTone(record.tone, ["purple", "blue", "cyan", "green", "yellow", "orange", "pink"][index % 7] as UniversalSceneTone),
    opacity: asNumber(record.opacity, 1, 0, 1),
    rotation: asNumber(record.rotation, 0, -360, 360),
    scale: asNumber(record.scale, 1, 0.2, 3),
    layer: asInteger(record.layer, index, -20, 120),
  };
}

function normalizeElements(value: unknown, learnerSignal: string): UniversalSceneElement[] {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    const cleaned = value
      .slice(0, 42)
      .map((item, index) => normalizeElement(item, index))
      .filter((item): item is UniversalSceneElement => Boolean(item));

    if (cleaned.length >= 3) return cleaned;
  }

  return buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(learnerSignal).elements;
}

function normalizeAction(raw: unknown, index: number): UniversalSceneAction | null {
  const record = asRecord(raw);
  if (!Object.keys(record).length) return null;

  const to = asRecord(record.to);
  const targetId = sanitizeId(record.target_id ?? record.targetId ?? record.target, "");
  if (!targetId) return null;

  return {
    target_id: targetId,
    kind: asActionKind(record.kind ?? record.action, index % 3 === 0 ? "highlight" : "appear"),
    startFrame: asInteger(record.startFrame ?? record.start_frame, 0, 0, seconds(9)),
    endFrame: asInteger(record.endFrame ?? record.end_frame, seconds(2), 1, seconds(12)),
    to: Object.keys(to).length
      ? {
          x: to.x === undefined ? undefined : asNumber(to.x, 640, SAFE_STAGE_X_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_X_MAX),
          y: to.y === undefined ? undefined : asNumber(to.y, 360, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MIN, SAFE_STAGE_Y_MAX),
          opacity: to.opacity === undefined ? undefined : asNumber(to.opacity, 1, 0, 1),
          scale: to.scale === undefined ? undefined : asNumber(to.scale, 1, 0.2, 3),
          rotation: to.rotation === undefined ? undefined : asNumber(to.rotation, 0, -360, 360),
        }
      : undefined,
    label: asText(record.label, "", 80) || undefined,
  };
}

function normalizeScenes(value: unknown, elements: UniversalSceneElement[], learnerSignal: string): UniversalSceneBeat[] {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    const allIds = new Set(elements.map((element) => element.id));
    const cleaned = value
      .slice(0, 7)
      .map((raw, index): UniversalSceneBeat | null => {
        const record = asRecord(raw);
        if (!Object.keys(record).length) return null;
        const visibleFallback = elements.slice(0, Math.min(elements.length, 8)).map((element) => element.id);
        const visible = asStringArray(record.visible_element_ids ?? record.visibleElements, visibleFallback, 24, 60).filter((id) => allIds.has(sanitizeId(id, id)) || allIds.has(id));
        const spotlight = asStringArray(record.spotlight_element_ids ?? record.spotlightElements, [], 8, 60).filter((id) => allIds.has(sanitizeId(id, id)) || allIds.has(id));
        const actions = Array.isArray(record.actions)
          ? record.actions
              .slice(0, 14)
              .map((item, actionIndex) => normalizeAction(item, actionIndex))
              .filter((action): action is UniversalSceneAction => action !== null && allIds.has(action.target_id))
          : [];

        return {
          id: sanitizeId(record.id, `scene_${index + 1}`),
          title: asText(record.title, index === 0 ? "Set up the idea" : `Scene ${index + 1}`, 72),
          caption: asText(record.caption, "Watch the pieces move so the hidden link becomes visible.", 190),
          durationInFrames: asInteger(record.durationInFrames ?? record.duration_in_frames ?? record.durationSeconds, seconds(4.5), seconds(2.5), seconds(8)),
          focus: asText(record.focus, "Focus on the part that changes.", 160),
          visible_element_ids: visible.length ? visible : visibleFallback,
          spotlight_element_ids: spotlight,
          actions,
        };
      })
      .filter((scene): scene is UniversalSceneBeat => Boolean(scene));

    if (cleaned.length >= 2) {
      const hasCheckpoint = cleaned.some((scene) => /check|try|explain|your/i.test(scene.title));
      if (!hasCheckpoint) {
        cleaned.push(buildDefaultCheckpointScene(elements));
      }
      return cleaned;
    }
  }

  return buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(learnerSignal).scenes;
}

function buildDefaultCheckpointScene(elements: UniversalSceneElement[]): UniversalSceneBeat {
  return {
    id: "checkpoint",
    title: "Checkpoint",
    caption: "Name the one link that changed your understanding.",
    durationInFrames: seconds(4),
    focus: "Ask the learner to explain the cause/effect link.",
    visible_element_ids: elements.slice(0, Math.min(elements.length, 10)).map((element) => element.id),
    spotlight_element_ids: elements.slice(0, 2).map((element) => element.id),
    actions: elements.slice(0, 2).map((element, index) => ({
      target_id: element.id,
      kind: index === 0 ? "pulse" : "highlight",
      startFrame: 8,
      endFrame: seconds(3),
    })),
  };
}

function makeRendererStrategy(): UniversalSceneContract["renderer_strategy"] {
  return {
    renderer: "Remotion Player",
    composition: "UniversalSceneRemotionComposition",
    model_writes_code: false,
    model_directs_scene_graph: true,
    explanation:
      "The model writes a director-level visual plan plus a universal scene graph. MyWay validates and stabilizes the JSON; Remotion renders it with trusted React/SVG code.",
  };
}

function defaultSceneStyle(input: unknown): UniversalSceneContract["scene_style"] {
  if (typeof input === "string") {
    return {
      visual_language: asText(input, "Clean animated educational diagram with glass labels, arrows, and gentle motion.", 160),
      background: "deep_space",
      notes: ["Use simple original shapes.", "Make the hidden relationship visible."],
    };
  }

  const record = asRecord(input);
  const background = asText(record.background, "deep_space", 40);
  return {
    visual_language: asText(record.visual_language, "Clean animated educational diagram with glass labels, arrows, and gentle motion.", 160),
    background: background === "paper" || background === "chalkboard" || background === "clean_room" ? background : "deep_space",
    notes: asStringArray(record.notes, ["Use simple original shapes.", "Make the hidden relationship visible."], 8, 120),
  };
}


function defaultDirectorPlan(learnerSignal: string): UniversalSceneDirectorPlan {
  return {
    visual_intent: "Turn the learner's stuck point into a visible relationship with a clear before, reveal, and corrected path.",
    misconception_target: "The learner may see separate pieces without seeing how one part changes or controls another.",
    key_relationships: ["current picture", "hidden link", "corrected explanation"],
    design_principles: ["Keep every important object visible.", "Make each scene introduce one visible change.", "Use motion to reveal the missing relationship."],
  };
}

function normalizeDirectorPlan(value: unknown, learnerSignal: string): UniversalSceneDirectorPlan {
  const fallback = defaultDirectorPlan(learnerSignal);
  const record = asRecord(value);

  return {
    visual_intent: asText(record.visual_intent ?? record.visualIntent, fallback.visual_intent, 260),
    misconception_target: asText(record.misconception_target ?? record.misconceptionTarget, fallback.misconception_target, 260),
    key_relationships: asStringArray(record.key_relationships ?? record.keyRelationships, fallback.key_relationships, 5, 90),
    design_principles: asStringArray(record.design_principles ?? record.designPrinciples, fallback.design_principles, 5, 110),
  };
}

export function normalizeUniversalSceneContract(input: unknown, learnerSignalFallback?: string): UniversalSceneContract {
  const record = asRecord(input);
  const learnerSignal = asText(record.learner_signal ?? record.learnerSignal, learnerSignalFallback ?? "I am stuck on this idea.", 420);
  const elements = normalizeElements(record.elements, learnerSignal);
  const scenes = normalizeScenes(record.scenes, elements, learnerSignal);
  const checkpoint = asRecord(record.checkpoint);

  return {
    schema_version: "myway_universal_scene_contract_v1",
    contract_id: asText(record.contract_id, stableUniversalSceneContractId(`${learnerSignal}:${JSON.stringify(record).slice(0, 600)}`), 80),
    title: asText(record.title, "A visual explanation built from your message", 84),
    learner_signal: learnerSignal,
    diagnosis_guess: asText(record.diagnosis_guess, "The learner needs the hidden relationship turned into visible motion.", 240),
    learning_goal: asText(record.learning_goal, "Show the missing link with an animated scene and a checkpoint.", 240),
    director_plan: normalizeDirectorPlan(record.director_plan ?? record.directorPlan, learnerSignal),
    format: {
      width: UNIVERSAL_SCENE_WIDTH,
      height: UNIVERSAL_SCENE_HEIGHT,
      fps: UNIVERSAL_SCENE_FPS,
      aspect: "16:9_laptop",
    },
    renderer_strategy: makeRendererStrategy(),
    scene_style: defaultSceneStyle(record.scene_style),
    elements,
    scenes,
    checkpoint: {
      prompt: asText(checkpoint.prompt, "What changed in your understanding after watching the animation?", 160),
      expected_idea: asText(checkpoint.expected_idea, "The learner names the main cause/effect or structure highlighted by the animation.", 220),
    },
    safety_notes: [
      "The model outputs JSON director contracts and scene graphs, not executable code.",
      "MyWay validates element kinds, stage-safe coordinates, labels, scene length, and actions before rendering.",
      "The scene can be novel, but it must be built from safe vector primitives and stabilized layout rules.",
    ],
  };
}

export function getUniversalSceneDurationInFrames(contract: UniversalSceneContract) {
  return contract.scenes.reduce((sum, scene) => sum + scene.durationInFrames, 0);
}

export function findUniversalSceneForFrame(contract: UniversalSceneContract, frame: number) {
  let cursor = 0;

  for (let index = 0; index < contract.scenes.length; index += 1) {
    const scene = contract.scenes[index]!;
    const startFrame = cursor;
    const endFrame = startFrame + scene.durationInFrames;

    if (frame <= endFrame || index === contract.scenes.length - 1) {
      return {
        scene,
        sceneIndex: index,
        startFrame,
        endFrame,
        progress: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (frame - startFrame) / Math.max(1, scene.durationInFrames))),
      };
    }

    cursor = endFrame;
  }

  const fallback = contract.scenes[0] ?? buildDefaultCheckpointScene(contract.elements);
  return {
    scene: fallback,
    sceneIndex: 0,
    startFrame: 0,
    endFrame: fallback.durationInFrames,
    progress: 0,
  };
}

function baseContract(args: {
  learnerSignal: string;
  title: string;
  diagnosis: string;
  goal: string;
  elements: UniversalSceneElement[];
  scenes: UniversalSceneBeat[];
  checkpoint: UniversalSceneContract["checkpoint"];
  style?: Partial<UniversalSceneContract["scene_style"]>;
}): UniversalSceneContract {
  return normalizeUniversalSceneContract({
    schema_version: "myway_universal_scene_contract_v1",
    contract_id: stableUniversalSceneContractId(args.learnerSignal),
    title: args.title,
    learner_signal: args.learnerSignal,
    diagnosis_guess: args.diagnosis,
    learning_goal: args.goal,
    scene_style: {
      visual_language: args.style?.visual_language ?? "Clean animated educational diagram with original vector shapes.",
      background: args.style?.background ?? "deep_space",
      notes: args.style?.notes ?? ["Fallback generated by MyWay.", "Use safe primitives."],
    },
    elements: args.elements,
    scenes: args.scenes,
    checkpoint: args.checkpoint,
  }, args.learnerSignal);
}

export function buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(learnerSignal: string): UniversalSceneContract {
  const signal = learnerSignal.trim() || "I am stuck on this idea.";
  const lower = signal.toLowerCase();

  if (/baseball|tag|runner|diamond|fly ball|base/.test(lower)) {
    const elements: UniversalSceneElement[] = [
      { id: "diamond", kind: "polygon", points: [[640,128], [986,360], [640,592], [294,360]], label: "baseball diamond", tone: "green", layer: 1 },
      { id: "home", kind: "rect", x: 610, y: 562, width: 60, height: 42, label: "home", tone: "white", layer: 2 },
      { id: "first", kind: "rect", x: 950, y: 338, width: 60, height: 42, label: "1st", tone: "white", layer: 2 },
      { id: "second", kind: "rect", x: 610, y: 102, width: 60, height: 42, label: "2nd", tone: "white", layer: 2 },
      { id: "third", kind: "rect", x: 270, y: 338, width: 60, height: 42, label: "3rd", tone: "white", layer: 2 },
      { id: "runner", kind: "circle", x: 300, y: 360, radius: 24, label: "runner", tone: "purple", layer: 4 },
      { id: "ball_path", kind: "arrow", from: [640, 360], to: [725, 122], label: "fly ball", tone: "yellow", layer: 3 },
      { id: "return_path", kind: "arrow", from: [470, 260], to: [310, 358], label: "tag up first", tone: "cyan", layer: 3 },
      { id: "advance_path", kind: "arrow", from: [330, 340], to: [620, 138], label: "then advance", tone: "orange", layer: 3 },
    ];

    return baseContract({
      learnerSignal: signal,
      title: "Why tagging up changes the runner's choice",
      diagnosis: "The learner may not see that the caught fly ball resets what the runner is allowed to do.",
      goal: "Show the runner returning to the base before trying to advance.",
      elements,
      scenes: [
        { id: "setup", title: "The field is the rule map", caption: "The runner is on third while the ball is hit into the air.", durationInFrames: seconds(4), focus: "Set up the diamond and runner.", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["runner", "ball_path"], actions: [{ target_id: "ball_path", kind: "draw", startFrame: 8, endFrame: seconds(3) }] },
        { id: "caught", title: "The catch changes the path", caption: "If the ball is caught, the runner must touch the original base first.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "Show the return requirement.", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["return_path", "third"], actions: [{ target_id: "runner", kind: "move_to", startFrame: 10, endFrame: seconds(3), to: { x: 310, y: 360 } }, { target_id: "return_path", kind: "draw", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(4) }] },
        { id: "advance", title: "Then the runner can go", caption: "After tagging up, advancing becomes legal if the timing works.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "Show cause and timing.", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["advance_path", "runner"], actions: [{ target_id: "runner", kind: "move_to", startFrame: 20, endFrame: seconds(4), to: { x: 640, y: 132 } }, { target_id: "advance_path", kind: "draw", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(4) }] },
      ],
      checkpoint: { prompt: "Why does the runner return to the base before advancing?", expected_idea: "Because a caught fly ball means the runner must tag up before leaving the base." },
      style: { background: "clean_room" },
    });
  }

  if (/bank|loan|money|borrow|deposit|interest|account/.test(lower)) {
    const elements: UniversalSceneElement[] = [
      { id: "borrower", kind: "icon", icon: "person", x: 170, y: 360, label: "borrower", tone: "cyan", layer: 4 },
      { id: "bank_lobby", kind: "rect", x: 420, y: 210, width: 370, height: 250, label: "bank", tone: "purple", layer: 1 },
      { id: "loan_desk", kind: "card", x: 505, y: 275, width: 200, height: 95, label: "loan promise", tone: "blue", layer: 2 },
      { id: "ledger", kind: "card", x: 835, y: 245, width: 230, height: 120, label: "ledger changes", tone: "green", layer: 2 },
      { id: "money_flow", kind: "arrow", from: [260, 360], to: [500, 330], label: "loan request", tone: "cyan", layer: 3 },
      { id: "credit_flow", kind: "arrow", from: [700, 326], to: [835, 320], label: "credit created", tone: "green", layer: 3 },
      { id: "repayment_flow", kind: "arrow", from: [820, 435], to: [260, 430], label: "repay over time", tone: "orange", layer: 3 },
    ];
    return baseContract({
      learnerSignal: signal,
      title: "How a loan moves through a bank system",
      diagnosis: "The learner may picture cash physically moving instead of ledger promises changing over time.",
      goal: "Show loan request, ledger credit, and later repayment as linked steps.",
      elements,
      scenes: [
        { id: "request", title: "Start with a request", caption: "The borrower asks for credit, not a pile of physical cash.", durationInFrames: seconds(4), focus: "Request enters the bank.", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["borrower", "money_flow"], actions: [{ target_id: "money_flow", kind: "draw", startFrame: 8, endFrame: seconds(3) }] },
        { id: "ledger", title: "The bank records credit", caption: "The important change is written in accounts and obligations.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "Ledger update.", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["ledger", "credit_flow"], actions: [{ target_id: "ledger", kind: "pulse", startFrame: 10, endFrame: seconds(4) }, { target_id: "credit_flow", kind: "draw", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(4) }] },
        { id: "repay", title: "Repayment closes the loop", caption: "Payments later reduce the obligation and add interest cost.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "Money flow over time.", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["repayment_flow"], actions: [{ target_id: "repayment_flow", kind: "draw", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(4) }] },
      ],
      checkpoint: { prompt: "What changes first in a bank loan: physical cash or account records?", expected_idea: "The account record/credit changes first; repayment moves value over time." },
      style: { background: "paper" },
    });
  }

  if (/saddle|x\^2|y\^2|graph|surface|parabola/.test(lower)) {
    const elements: UniversalSceneElement[] = [
      { id: "axis_x", kind: "line", from: [250, 455], to: [1020, 455], label: "x direction", tone: "cyan", layer: 1 },
      { id: "axis_y", kind: "line", from: [640, 150], to: [640, 620], label: "y direction", tone: "pink", layer: 1 },
      { id: "up_curve", kind: "path", d: "M320 460 C450 260 830 260 960 460", label: "x² rises", tone: "cyan", layer: 3 },
      { id: "down_curve", kind: "path", d: "M415 260 C530 500 750 500 865 260", label: "-y² falls", tone: "pink", layer: 3 },
      { id: "center", kind: "circle", x: 640, y: 455, radius: 28, label: "same center", tone: "yellow", layer: 4 },
      { id: "saddle_card", kind: "card", x: 860, y: 150, width: 280, height: 118, label: "one direction up, one direction down", tone: "purple", layer: 4 },
    ];
    return baseContract({
      learnerSignal: signal,
      title: "Why the surface becomes a saddle",
      diagnosis: "The learner may expect the surface to bend the same way in both directions.",
      goal: "Show x² rising while -y² bends the other way from the same center.",
      elements,
      scenes: [
        { id: "x", title: "One slice bends up", caption: "Across x, the square shape rises like a bowl slice.", durationInFrames: seconds(4), focus: "x² direction", visible_element_ids: ["axis_x", "up_curve", "center"], spotlight_element_ids: ["up_curve"], actions: [{ target_id: "up_curve", kind: "draw", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(3) }] },
        { id: "y", title: "The other slice bends down", caption: "The minus sign flips the y direction downward.", durationInFrames: seconds(4), focus: "-y² direction", visible_element_ids: ["axis_x", "axis_y", "up_curve", "down_curve", "center"], spotlight_element_ids: ["down_curve"], actions: [{ target_id: "down_curve", kind: "draw", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(3) }] },
        { id: "combine", title: "Together they make the saddle", caption: "The surface rises one way and falls the crossing way.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "combine directions", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["saddle_card", "center"], actions: [{ target_id: "saddle_card", kind: "appear", startFrame: 8, endFrame: seconds(3) }] },
      ],
      checkpoint: { prompt: "Which direction gets flipped by the minus sign?", expected_idea: "The y direction is flipped downward, while x² still bends upward." },
    });
  }

  const elements: UniversalSceneElement[] = [
    { id: "stuck_point", kind: "card", x: 120, y: 235, width: 280, height: 120, label: "what I see", text: signal, tone: "purple", layer: 2 },
    { id: "hidden_link", kind: "card", x: 500, y: 235, width: 280, height: 120, label: "hidden link", text: "the missing relationship", tone: "yellow", layer: 2 },
    { id: "new_view", kind: "card", x: 880, y: 235, width: 280, height: 120, label: "new view", text: "explain the connection", tone: "green", layer: 2 },
    { id: "arrow_1", kind: "arrow", from: [400, 295], to: [500, 295], label: "look for cause", tone: "cyan", layer: 3 },
    { id: "arrow_2", kind: "arrow", from: [780, 295], to: [880, 295], label: "connect it", tone: "cyan", layer: 3 },
    { id: "question", kind: "highlight", x: 505, y: 220, width: 270, height: 150, label: "focus here", tone: "orange", layer: 1 },
  ];

  return baseContract({
    learnerSignal: signal,
    title: "Turn the stuck point into a visible link",
    diagnosis: "The learner likely has pieces of the idea but not the relationship between them.",
    goal: "Separate the visible facts from the hidden link, then reconnect them.",
    elements,
    scenes: [
      { id: "visible", title: "Start with what is visible", caption: "First, name the part you already notice.", durationInFrames: seconds(4), focus: "visible piece", visible_element_ids: ["stuck_point"], spotlight_element_ids: ["stuck_point"], actions: [{ target_id: "stuck_point", kind: "appear", startFrame: 0, endFrame: seconds(2) }] },
      { id: "link", title: "Find the missing link", caption: "The confusing part is usually the relationship, not the pieces.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "hidden link", visible_element_ids: ["stuck_point", "hidden_link", "arrow_1", "question"], spotlight_element_ids: ["hidden_link", "question"], actions: [{ target_id: "arrow_1", kind: "draw", startFrame: 8, endFrame: seconds(3) }, { target_id: "hidden_link", kind: "pulse", startFrame: 20, endFrame: seconds(4) }] },
      { id: "connect", title: "Reconnect the pieces", caption: "Now explain how one part changes or causes the other.", durationInFrames: seconds(5), focus: "complete explanation", visible_element_ids: elements.map((e) => e.id), spotlight_element_ids: ["new_view"], actions: [{ target_id: "arrow_2", kind: "draw", startFrame: 8, endFrame: seconds(3) }, { target_id: "new_view", kind: "appear", startFrame: 15, endFrame: seconds(3) }] },
    ],
    checkpoint: { prompt: "What is the hidden link in your own words?", expected_idea: "The learner states how the pieces relate, not just what the pieces are." },
  });
}

"use client";

import { AbsoluteFill, interpolate, spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig } from "remotion";
import type {
  UniversalSceneAction,
  UniversalSceneContract,
  UniversalSceneElement,
  UniversalScenePoint,
  UniversalSceneTone,
} from "./universal-scene-contract";
import { findUniversalSceneForFrame } from "./universal-scene-contract";

export type UniversalSceneRemotionCompositionProps = {
  contract: UniversalSceneContract;
};

type VisualState = {
  x?: number;
  y?: number;
  opacity: number;
  scale: number;
  rotation: number;
  glow: number;
  draw: number;
};

const toneStyles: Record<UniversalSceneTone, { fill: string; stroke: string; text: string; glow: string; soft: string }> = {
  purple: { fill: "rgba(147,51,234,0.34)", stroke: "rgba(221,214,254,0.8)", text: "#faf5ff", glow: "rgba(168,85,247,0.46)", soft: "rgba(168,85,247,0.14)" },
  blue: { fill: "rgba(37,99,235,0.32)", stroke: "rgba(191,219,254,0.78)", text: "#eff6ff", glow: "rgba(59,130,246,0.42)", soft: "rgba(59,130,246,0.13)" },
  cyan: { fill: "rgba(8,145,178,0.32)", stroke: "rgba(165,243,252,0.78)", text: "#ecfeff", glow: "rgba(34,211,238,0.4)", soft: "rgba(34,211,238,0.13)" },
  green: { fill: "rgba(22,163,74,0.3)", stroke: "rgba(187,247,208,0.76)", text: "#f0fdf4", glow: "rgba(74,222,128,0.4)", soft: "rgba(74,222,128,0.13)" },
  yellow: { fill: "rgba(202,138,4,0.32)", stroke: "rgba(254,240,138,0.82)", text: "#fefce8", glow: "rgba(253,224,71,0.42)", soft: "rgba(253,224,71,0.16)" },
  orange: { fill: "rgba(234,88,12,0.32)", stroke: "rgba(254,215,170,0.82)", text: "#fff7ed", glow: "rgba(251,146,60,0.44)", soft: "rgba(251,146,60,0.14)" },
  red: { fill: "rgba(220,38,38,0.3)", stroke: "rgba(254,202,202,0.78)", text: "#fef2f2", glow: "rgba(248,113,113,0.42)", soft: "rgba(248,113,113,0.13)" },
  pink: { fill: "rgba(219,39,119,0.3)", stroke: "rgba(251,207,232,0.78)", text: "#fdf2f8", glow: "rgba(244,114,182,0.42)", soft: "rgba(244,114,182,0.13)" },
  gray: { fill: "rgba(75,85,99,0.36)", stroke: "rgba(229,231,235,0.66)", text: "#f9fafb", glow: "rgba(209,213,219,0.28)", soft: "rgba(209,213,219,0.1)" },
  white: { fill: "rgba(255,255,255,0.22)", stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.76)", text: "#ffffff", glow: "rgba(255,255,255,0.28)", soft: "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" },
};

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function ease(value: number) {
  const t = clamp(value, 0, 1);
  return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
}

function actionProgress(frame: number, action: UniversalSceneAction) {
  const start = action.startFrame ?? 0;
  const end = Math.max(start + 1, action.endFrame ?? start + 45);
  return clamp((frame - start) / (end - start), 0, 1);
}

function getElementState(element: UniversalSceneElement, actions: UniversalSceneAction[], localFrame: number, spotlight: boolean): VisualState {
  let state: VisualState = {
    x: element.x,
    y: element.y,
    opacity: element.opacity ?? 1,
    scale: element.scale ?? 1,
    rotation: element.rotation ?? 0,
    glow: spotlight ? 0.75 : 0,
    draw: 1,
  };

  for (const action of actions) {
    if (action.target_id !== element.id) continue;
    const progress = ease(actionProgress(localFrame, action));

    if (action.kind === "appear") {
      state.opacity = Math.max(state.opacity, progress);
      state.scale = state.scale * interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [0.92, 1]);
    }

    if (action.kind === "fade") {
      state.opacity = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [state.opacity, action.to?.opacity ?? 0.18]);
    }

    if (action.kind === "move_to") {
      if (typeof action.to?.x === "number") state.x = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [element.x ?? 0, action.to.x]);
      if (typeof action.to?.y === "number") state.y = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [element.y ?? 0, action.to.y]);
    }

    if (action.kind === "scale_to") {
      state.scale = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [state.scale, action.to?.scale ?? 1.14]);
    }

    if (action.kind === "highlight") {
      state.glow = Math.max(state.glow, progress);
    }

    if (action.kind === "pulse") {
      const pulse = Math.sin(localFrame * 0.16) * 0.5 + 0.5;
      state.glow = Math.max(state.glow, 0.35 + pulse * 0.65 * progress);
      state.scale = state.scale * (1 + 0.045 * pulse * progress);
    }

    if (action.kind === "draw") {
      state.draw = progress;
    }
  }

  return state;
}

function wrapText(value: string, maxChars: number) {
  const words = value.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let current = "";

  for (const word of words) {
    const next = current ? `${current} ${word}` : word;
    if (next.length > maxChars && current) {
      lines.push(current);
      current = word;
    } else {
      current = next;
    }
  }

  if (current) lines.push(current);
  return lines.slice(0, 5);
}

function labelFor(element: UniversalSceneElement) {
  return element.text || element.label || element.icon || element.id.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

function renderMultilineText(args: {
  text: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  maxChars: number;
  fontSize: number;
  fill: string;
  weight?: number;
  anchor?: "start" | "middle";
}) {
  const lines = wrapText(args.text, args.maxChars);
  return (
    <text x={args.x} y={args.y} textAnchor={args.anchor ?? "middle"} fill={args.fill} fontSize={args.fontSize} fontWeight={args.weight ?? 850}>
      {lines.map((line, index) => (
        <tspan key={`${line}-${index}`} x={args.x} dy={index === 0 ? 0 : args.fontSize * 1.25}>
          {line}
        </tspan>
      ))}
    </text>
  );
}

function polygonPoints(points?: UniversalScenePoint[]) {
  return (points ?? []).map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`).join(" ");
}

function pathForArrow(from: UniversalScenePoint, to: UniversalScenePoint) {
  const midX = (from[0] + to[0]) / 2;
  const midY = (from[1] + to[1]) / 2;
  const dx = to[0] - from[0];
  const dy = to[1] - from[1];
  const bend = clamp(Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * 0.12, 22, 86);
  return `M ${from[0]} ${from[1]} Q ${midX} ${midY - bend} ${to[0]} ${to[1]}`;
}

type Bounds = { minX: number; minY: number; maxX: number; maxY: number };

function mergeBounds(bounds: Bounds | null, next: Bounds): Bounds {
  if (!bounds) return next;
  return {
    minX: Math.min(bounds.minX, next.minX),
    minY: Math.min(bounds.minY, next.minY),
    maxX: Math.max(bounds.maxX, next.maxX),
    maxY: Math.max(bounds.maxY, next.maxY),
  };
}

function boundsForElement(element: UniversalSceneElement): Bounds {
  const x = element.x ?? 640;
  const y = element.y ?? 360;
  const width = element.width ?? 160;
  const height = element.height ?? 90;
  const radius = element.radius ?? 36;

  if (element.kind === "card" || element.kind === "rect" || element.kind === "highlight") {
    return { minX: x, minY: y, maxX: x + width, maxY: y + height };
  }

  if (element.kind === "circle") {
    return { minX: x - radius, minY: y - radius, maxX: x + radius, maxY: y + radius };
  }

  if (element.kind === "ellipse") {
    const rx = element.rx ?? width / 2;
    const ry = element.ry ?? height / 2;
    return { minX: x - rx, minY: y - ry, maxX: x + rx, maxY: y + ry };
  }

  if (element.kind === "line" || element.kind === "arrow") {
    const from = element.from ?? [x, y];
    const to = element.to ?? [x + width, y];
    return {
      minX: Math.min(from[0], to[0]),
      minY: Math.min(from[1], to[1]),
      maxX: Math.max(from[0], to[0]),
      maxY: Math.max(from[1], to[1]),
    };
  }

  if (element.kind === "polygon" && element.points?.length) {
    return element.points.reduce<Bounds | null>((bounds, point) => mergeBounds(bounds, {
      minX: point[0],
      minY: point[1],
      maxX: point[0],
      maxY: point[1],
    }), null) ?? { minX: x - width / 2, minY: y - height / 2, maxX: x + width / 2, maxY: y + height / 2 };
  }

  return { minX: x - width / 2, minY: y - height / 2, maxX: x + width / 2, maxY: y + height / 2 };
}

function getSceneCamera(elements: UniversalSceneElement[]) {
  const sceneBounds = elements.reduce<Bounds | null>((bounds, element) => mergeBounds(bounds, boundsForElement(element)), null) ?? {
    minX: 120,
    minY: 150,
    maxX: 1160,
    maxY: 570,
  };

  const padded = {
    minX: sceneBounds.minX - 70,
    minY: sceneBounds.minY - 60,
    maxX: sceneBounds.maxX + 70,
    maxY: sceneBounds.maxY + 60,
  };
  const sourceWidth = Math.max(1, padded.maxX - padded.minX);
  const sourceHeight = Math.max(1, padded.maxY - padded.minY);
  const target = { x: 82, y: 138, width: 1116, height: 420 };
  const scale = clamp(Math.min(target.width / sourceWidth, target.height / sourceHeight), 0.72, 1.22);
  const sourceCenterX = (padded.minX + padded.maxX) / 2;
  const sourceCenterY = (padded.minY + padded.maxY) / 2;
  const targetCenterX = target.x + target.width / 2;
  const targetCenterY = target.y + target.height / 2;

  return {
    transform: `translate(${targetCenterX} ${targetCenterY}) scale(${scale}) translate(${-sourceCenterX} ${-sourceCenterY})`,
  };
}


function IconGlyph({ element, state, tone }: { element: UniversalSceneElement; state: VisualState; tone: ReturnType<typeof getTone> }) {
  const x = state.x ?? element.x ?? 0;
  const y = state.y ?? element.y ?? 0;
  const icon = (element.icon || element.label || "idea").toLowerCase();

  if (/person|runner|learner|student|borrower/.test(icon)) {
    return (
      <g>
        <circle cx={x} cy={y - 30} r="22" fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="4" />
        <path d={`M ${x} ${y - 6} L ${x} ${y + 56} M ${x - 36} ${y + 16} L ${x + 36} ${y + 16} M ${x} ${y + 56} L ${x - 34} ${y + 96} M ${x} ${y + 56} L ${x + 34} ${y + 96}`} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" />
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (/bank|building|school|house/.test(icon)) {
    return (
      <g>
        <polygon points={`${x - 72},${y - 40} ${x},${y - 92} ${x + 72},${y - 40}`} fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="5" />
        <rect x={x - 64} y={y - 40} width="128" height="102" rx="10" fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="5" />
        {[x - 38, x, x + 38].map((column) => (
          <line key={column} x1={column} y1={y - 24} x2={column} y2={y + 48} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" />
        ))}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (/ball|baseball|dot/.test(icon)) {
    return (
      <g>
        <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="32" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.92)" stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="4" />
        <path d={`M ${x - 15} ${y - 25} C ${x - 2} ${y - 8} ${x - 2} ${y + 8} ${x - 15} ${y + 25}`} fill="none" stroke="rgba(239,68,68,0.75)" strokeWidth="3" />
        <path d={`M ${x + 15} ${y - 25} C ${x + 2} ${y - 8} ${x + 2} ${y + 8} ${x + 15} ${y + 25}`} fill="none" stroke="rgba(239,68,68,0.75)" strokeWidth="3" />
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (/money|dollar|coin|credit/.test(icon)) {
    return (
      <g>
        <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="48" fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="5" />
        <text x={x} y={y + 15} textAnchor="middle" fill={tone.text} fontSize="48" fontWeight="950">$</text>
      </g>
    );
  }

  return (
    <g>
      <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="48" fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="5" />
      <text x={x} y={y + 14} textAnchor="middle" fill={tone.text} fontSize="38" fontWeight="950">?</text>
    </g>
  );
}

function getTone(element: UniversalSceneElement) {
  return toneStyles[element.tone ?? "purple"] ?? toneStyles.purple;
}

function RenderElement({ element, state, spotlight }: { element: UniversalSceneElement; state: VisualState; spotlight: boolean }) {
  const tone = getTone(element);
  const x = state.x ?? element.x ?? 0;
  const y = state.y ?? element.y ?? 0;
  const width = element.width ?? 160;
  const height = element.height ?? 90;
  const radius = element.radius ?? 34;
  const label = labelFor(element);
  const glowOpacity = Math.max(state.glow, spotlight ? 0.7 : 0);
  const transform = `translate(${x} ${y}) rotate(${state.rotation}) scale(${state.scale}) translate(${-x} ${-y})`;
  const common = {
    opacity: state.opacity,
    transform,
    filter: glowOpacity > 0.06 ? `drop-shadow(0 0 ${18 + glowOpacity * 34}px ${tone.glow})` : undefined,
  };

  const labelBelow = (offsetY = 58) => element.label ? (
    <text x={x} y={y + offsetY} textAnchor="middle" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.82)" fontSize="20" fontWeight="850">
      {element.label}
    </text>
  ) : null;

  if (element.kind === "card") {
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <rect x={x} y={y} width={width} height={height} rx="24" fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="3" />
        <rect x={x + 10} y={y + 10} width={width - 20} height={height - 20} rx="18" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.055)" />
        {renderMultilineText({ text: label, x: x + width / 2, y: y + Math.max(32, height / 2 - 18), maxChars: Math.max(12, Math.round(width / 12)), fontSize: label.length > 55 ? 18 : 22, fill: tone.text })}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "rect") {
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <rect x={x} y={y} width={width} height={height} rx="18" fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="3" />
        {renderMultilineText({ text: label, x: x + width / 2, y: y + height / 2 - 8, maxChars: Math.max(8, Math.round(width / 12)), fontSize: 20, fill: tone.text })}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "circle") {
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={radius} fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="4" />
        {element.label ? renderMultilineText({ text: element.label, x, y: y + 7, maxChars: 12, fontSize: 18, fill: tone.text }) : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "ellipse") {
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <ellipse cx={x} cy={y} rx={element.rx ?? width / 2} ry={element.ry ?? height / 2} fill={tone.fill} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="4" />
        {element.label ? renderMultilineText({ text: element.label, x, y: y + 7, maxChars: 18, fontSize: 18, fill: tone.text }) : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "polygon") {
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <polygon points={polygonPoints(element.points)} fill={tone.soft} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="5" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        {element.label ? renderMultilineText({ text: element.label, x, y, maxChars: 18, fontSize: 22, fill: tone.text }) : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "line") {
    const from = element.from ?? [x, y];
    const to = element.to ?? [x + width, y];
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <line x1={from[0]} y1={from[1]} x2={to[0]} y2={to[1]} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="6" strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={`${Math.max(1, state.draw) * 1200} 1200`} />
        {element.label ? <text x={(from[0] + to[0]) / 2} y={(from[1] + to[1]) / 2 - 16} textAnchor="middle" fill={tone.text} fontSize="19" fontWeight="850">{element.label}</text> : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "arrow") {
    const from = element.from ?? [x, y];
    const to = element.to ?? [x + width, y];
    const d = pathForArrow(from, to);
    const dash = 1450;
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="7" strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={`${dash * state.draw} ${dash}`} markerEnd="url(#universalArrowHead)" />
        {element.label ? <text x={(from[0] + to[0]) / 2} y={(from[1] + to[1]) / 2 - 28} textAnchor="middle" fill={tone.text} fontSize="20" fontWeight="900">{element.label}</text> : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "path") {
    const dash = 1800;
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        <path d={element.d || `M ${x} ${y} C ${x + 160} ${y - 120} ${x + 320} ${y + 120} ${x + 480} ${y}`} fill="none" stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeDasharray={`${dash * state.draw} ${dash}`} />
        {element.label ? <text x={x + width / 2} y={y - 22} textAnchor="middle" fill={tone.text} fontSize="20" fontWeight="900">{element.label}</text> : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "text") {
    return (
      <g {...common}>
        {renderMultilineText({ text: label, x, y, maxChars: Math.max(12, Math.round(width / 11)), fontSize: element.height && element.height > 100 ? 24 : 22, fill: tone.text, weight: 900 })}
      </g>
    );
  }

  if (element.kind === "highlight") {
    return (
      <g {...common} opacity={Math.min(0.9, state.opacity * (0.45 + glowOpacity * 0.55))}>
        <rect x={x} y={y} width={width} height={height} rx="26" fill={tone.soft} stroke={tone.stroke} strokeWidth="4" strokeDasharray="12 12" />
        {element.label ? <text x={x + width / 2} y={y - 12} textAnchor="middle" fill={tone.text} fontSize="18" fontWeight="900">{element.label}</text> : null}
      </g>
    );
  }

  return (
    <g {...common}>
      <IconGlyph element={element} state={state} tone={tone} />
      {labelBelow(112)}
    </g>
  );
}

function Background({ contract }: { contract: UniversalSceneContract }) {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const drift = Math.sin(frame * 0.01) * 18;
  const background = contract.scene_style.background;

  if (background === "paper") {
    return (
      <>
        <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="#eee6d4" />
        <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="rgba(45,30,18,0.08)" />
        {Array.from({ length: 14 }).map((_, index) => (
          <line key={index} x1="0" x2="1280" y1={100 + index * 42} y2={100 + index * 42} stroke="rgba(92,64,36,0.08)" strokeWidth="2" />
        ))}
      </>
    );
  }

  if (background === "chalkboard") {
    return (
      <>
        <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="#10231f" />
        <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.025)" />
        {Array.from({ length: 20 }).map((_, index) => (
          <circle key={index} cx={(index * 97) % 1280} cy={(index * 61) % 720} r="1.5" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" />
        ))}
      </>
    );
  }

  if (background === "clean_room") {
    return (
      <>
        <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="#eef2ff" />
        <circle cx="1050" cy="110" r="260" fill="rgba(168,85,247,0.14)" />
        <circle cx="160" cy="620" r="230" fill="rgba(34,211,238,0.14)" />
        <rect x="64" y="96" width="1152" height="552" rx="44" fill="rgba(15,23,42,0.045)" stroke="rgba(15,23,42,0.08)" />
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="#05030b" />
      <circle cx={210 + drift} cy="115" r="290" fill="rgba(126,34,206,0.28)" />
      <circle cx={1030 - drift} cy="560" r="330" fill="rgba(6,182,212,0.14)" />
      <circle cx="810" cy="190" r="230" fill="rgba(236,72,153,0.12)" />
      {Array.from({ length: 13 }).map((_, index) => (
        <line key={index} x1={70 + index * 100} x2={70 + index * 100} y1="110" y2="650" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.045)" strokeWidth="1" />
      ))}
      {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, index) => (
        <line key={index} x1="70" x2="1210" y1={130 + index * 72} y2={130 + index * 72} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" strokeWidth="1" />
      ))}
    </>
  );
}

function SceneLayer({ contract }: { contract: UniversalSceneContract }) {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const active = findUniversalSceneForFrame(contract, frame);
  const localFrame = frame - active.startFrame;
  const scene = active.scene;
  const visible = new Set(scene.visible_element_ids?.length ? scene.visible_element_ids : contract.elements.map((element) => element.id));
  const spotlight = new Set(scene.spotlight_element_ids ?? []);
  const visibleElements = contract.elements
    .filter((element) => visible.has(element.id))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.layer ?? 0) - (b.layer ?? 0));
  const camera = getSceneCamera(visibleElements);

  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 1280 720" style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}>
      <defs>
        <marker id="universalArrowHead" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8.5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.86)" />
        </marker>
      </defs>
      <Background contract={contract} />
      <rect x="46" y="112" width="1188" height="512" rx="42" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.18)" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" />
      <g transform={camera.transform}>
        {visibleElements.map((element) => {
          const isSpotlight = spotlight.has(element.id);
          const state = getElementState(element, scene.actions ?? [], localFrame, isSpotlight);
          return <RenderElement key={element.id} element={element} state={state} spotlight={isSpotlight} />;
        })}
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
}

function TopTitle({ contract }: { contract: UniversalSceneContract }) {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const { fps } = useVideoConfig();
  const enter = spring({ frame, fps, config: { damping: 19, stiffness: 85 } });

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "absolute",
        left: 36,
        right: 36,
        top: 28,
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        gap: 18,
        padding: "18px 22px",
        borderRadius: 28,
        background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",
        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
        backdropFilter: "blur(18px)",
        opacity: enter,
        transform: `translateY(${interpolate(enter, [0, 1], [-18, 0])}px)`,
      }}
    >
      <div>
        <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.62)", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 950, letterSpacing: "0.18em", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
          MyWay director scene contract
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 6, color: "white", fontSize: 31, lineHeight: 1.05, fontWeight: 950 }}>
          {contract.title}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)", fontSize: 14, lineHeight: 1.35, maxWidth: 360, fontWeight: 750, textAlign: "right" }}>
        Director JSON → stabilized Remotion renderer
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CaptionPanel({ contract }: { contract: UniversalSceneContract }) {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const active = findUniversalSceneForFrame(contract, frame);
  const { fps } = useVideoConfig();
  const captionIn = spring({ frame: Math.max(0, frame - active.startFrame), fps, config: { damping: 18, stiffness: 90 } });
  const sceneNumber = `${active.sceneIndex + 1}/${contract.scenes.length}`;

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "absolute", left: 44, right: 44, bottom: 28 }}>
      <div
        style={{
          display: "grid",
          gridTemplateColumns: "auto minmax(0, 1fr)",
          gap: 18,
          alignItems: "center",
          padding: "18px 22px",
          borderRadius: 28,
          background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.72)",
          border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
          boxShadow: "0 28px 90px rgba(0,0,0,0.42)",
          opacity: captionIn,
          transform: `translateY(${interpolate(captionIn, [0, 1], [18, 0])}px)`,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: 84,
            height: 84,
            borderRadius: 24,
            display: "grid",
            placeItems: "center",
            color: "white",
            fontWeight: 950,
            fontSize: 24,
            background: "radial-gradient(circle at 30% 20%, rgba(255,255,255,0.25), transparent 34%), linear-gradient(145deg, #8b5cf6, #0891b2)",
            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18)",
          }}
        >
          {sceneNumber}
        </div>
        <div>
          <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.66)", fontSize: 14, letterSpacing: "0.14em", textTransform: "uppercase", fontWeight: 900 }}>
            {active.scene.title}
          </div>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 8, color: "white", fontSize: 28, lineHeight: 1.18, fontWeight: 900 }}>
            {active.scene.caption}
          </div>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 8, color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.56)", fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 1.35, fontWeight: 750 }}>
            Focus: {active.scene.focus}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CheckpointOverlay({ contract }: { contract: UniversalSceneContract }) {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const active = findUniversalSceneForFrame(contract, frame);
  const isLastScene = active.sceneIndex === contract.scenes.length - 1;
  const opacity = isLastScene ? ease(active.progress) : 0;

  if (opacity <= 0.04) return null;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "absolute",
        right: 72,
        top: 136,
        width: 390,
        padding: 24,
        borderRadius: 28,
        background: "rgba(17,24,39,0.82)",
        border: "1px solid rgba(253,224,71,0.28)",
        color: "white",
        opacity,
        transform: `scale(${interpolate(opacity, [0, 1], [0.94, 1])})`,
        boxShadow: "0 24px 80px rgba(0,0,0,0.38)",
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ color: "rgba(253,224,71,0.96)", fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 950, letterSpacing: "0.16em", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
        MyWay checkpoint
      </div>
      <div style={{ marginTop: 12, fontSize: 24, lineHeight: 1.22, fontWeight: 950 }}>
        {contract.checkpoint.prompt}
      </div>
      <div style={{ marginTop: 14, color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.68)", fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 1.45 }}>
        Expected idea: {contract.checkpoint.expected_idea}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function UniversalSceneRemotionComposition({ contract }: UniversalSceneRemotionCompositionProps) {
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ background: "#05030b", fontFamily: "Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif", overflow: "hidden" }}>
      <SceneLayer contract={contract} />
      <TopTitle contract={contract} />
      <CaptionPanel contract={contract} />
      <CheckpointOverlay contract={contract} />
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

  buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract,
  getUniversalSceneDurationInFrames,
  type UniversalSceneContract,
} from "./universal-scene-contract";
import {
  buildDefaultVideoDirectorRequestContext,
  buildFallbackVideoDirectorContract,
  VIDEO_DIRECTOR_LAB_SAMPLES,
  type MyWayVideoDirectorContract,
  type VideoDirectorBridgeLevel,
  type VideoDirectorDiagnosisLabel,
} from "../director";
import { GeneratedVideo3DPlayer } from "../hybrid-3d";

type GenerationProvider = "nvidia" | "ollama";
type GenerationSource = "deterministic" | GenerationProvider;

type UniversalSceneResponse = {
  ok: boolean;
  source: "nvidia" | "ollama" | "fallback";
  provider?: GenerationProvider;
  model: string;
  contract: UniversalSceneContract;
  director_contract: MyWayVideoDirectorContract;
  context?: unknown;
  error?: string;
  warnings?: string[];
  debug?: unknown;
};

const PROVIDER_DEFAULT_MODELS: Record<GenerationProvider, string> = {
  nvidia: "nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b",
  ollama: "qwen2.5:3b",
};

const PROVIDER_LABELS: Record<GenerationProvider, string> = {
  nvidia: "NVIDIA NIM",
  ollama: "Local Ollama",
};

const DIAGNOSIS_OPTIONS: VideoDirectorDiagnosisLabel[] = [
  "unknown",
  "recall_gap",
  "representation_gap",
  "procedure_gap",
  "discrimination_gap",
  "transfer_gap",
  "metacognitive_gap",
  "no_gap_detected",
];

const BRIDGE_OPTIONS: VideoDirectorBridgeLevel[] = ["bridge_0", "bridge_1", "bridge_2", "full_bridge"];

const shellStyle = {
  border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)",
  borderRadius: "2rem",
  background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  backdropFilter: "blur(18px)",
  boxShadow: "0 24px 80px rgba(0,0,0,0.28)",
} as const;

function TinyLabel({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <p
      style={{
        margin: 0,
        color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.58)",
        fontSize: "0.72rem",
        fontWeight: 900,
        letterSpacing: "0.16em",
        textTransform: "uppercase",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </p>
  );
}

function ActionButton({
  children,
  disabled,
  onClick,
  variant = "secondary",
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary";
}) {
  const primary = variant === "primary";

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onClick}
      style={{
        border: primary ? "1px solid rgba(221,214,254,0.46)" : "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
        borderRadius: "999px",
        background: primary
          ? "linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(147,51,234,0.88), rgba(8,145,178,0.86))"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
        color: "white",
        padding: "0.7rem 0.95rem",
        fontWeight: 900,
        cursor: disabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
        opacity: disabled ? 0.55 : 1,
        boxShadow: primary ? "0 18px 48px rgba(88,28,135,0.34)" : "none",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}
