function splitList(value: string) {
  return value
    .split(/[\n,]/g)
    .map((item) => item.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 8);
}

function UniversalSceneRemotionLabInner() {
  const firstSample = VIDEO_DIRECTOR_LAB_SAMPLES[0]?.context ?? buildDefaultVideoDirectorRequestContext("I am stuck.");
  const [learnerMessage, setLearnerMessage] = useState(firstSample.learner_message);
  const [topicLabel, setTopicLabel] = useState(firstSample.learning_context.topic_label ?? "");
  const [diagnosisLabel, setDiagnosisLabel] = useState<VideoDirectorDiagnosisLabel>(firstSample.learning_context.diagnosis_label ?? "unknown");
  const [rootProblem, setRootProblem] = useState(firstSample.learning_context.root_problem ?? "");
  const [misconceptionTarget, setMisconceptionTarget] = useState(firstSample.learning_context.misconception_target ?? "");
  const [bridgeLevel, setBridgeLevel] = useState<VideoDirectorBridgeLevel>(firstSample.learning_context.bridge_level);
  const [priorAttemptSummary, setPriorAttemptSummary] = useState(firstSample.learning_context.prior_attempt_summary ?? "");
  const [interests, setInterests] = useState(firstSample.personalization_profile.interests.join(", "));
  const [profileSummary, setProfileSummary] = useState(firstSample.personalization_profile.profile_summary ?? "");

  const [provider, setProvider] = useState<GenerationProvider>("nvidia");
  const [modelName, setModelName] = useState(PROVIDER_DEFAULT_MODELS.nvidia);
  const [contract, setContract] = useState<UniversalSceneContract>(() => buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(firstSample.learner_message));
  const [directorContract, setDirectorContract] = useState<MyWayVideoDirectorContract>(() => buildFallbackVideoDirectorContract(firstSample));
  const [source, setSource] = useState<GenerationSource>("deterministic");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("Ready. Add learner context, interests, and root problem, then ask the model for a video director contract.");
  const [lastError, setLastError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [lastPayload, setLastPayload] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [showFallback2D, setShowFallback2D] = useState(false);

  const requestContext = useMemo(() => {
    const fallback = buildDefaultVideoDirectorRequestContext(learnerMessage);
    const bridge = bridgeLevel;
    return {
      ...fallback,
      learner_message: learnerMessage,
      learning_context: {
        ...fallback.learning_context,
        topic_label: topicLabel,
        diagnosis_label: diagnosisLabel,
        root_problem: rootProblem,
        misconception_target: misconceptionTarget,
        bridge_level: bridge,
        language_policy: {
          jargon_level: (bridge === "bridge_0" ? "none" : bridge === "bridge_1" ? "light" : "standard") as "none" | "light" | "standard" | "full",
        },
        prior_attempt_summary: priorAttemptSummary || null,
      },
      personalization_profile: {
        ...fallback.personalization_profile,
        interests: splitList(interests),
        preferred_explanation_style: ["visual_description", "concrete_examples", "step_by_step"] as import("../director").VideoDirectorPresentationStyle[],
        avoidances: ["long captions", "generic textbook explainer", "decorative personalization"],
        known_good_metaphors: [],
        profile_summary: profileSummary || null,
      },
    };
  }, [bridgeLevel, diagnosisLabel, interests, learnerMessage, misconceptionTarget, priorAttemptSummary, profileSummary, rootProblem, topicLabel]);

  const durationInFrames = useMemo(() => getUniversalSceneDurationInFrames(contract), [contract]);
  const inputProps = useMemo(() => ({ contract }), [contract]);

  function selectProvider(nextProvider: GenerationProvider) {
    setProvider(nextProvider);
    setModelName(PROVIDER_DEFAULT_MODELS[nextProvider]);
    setStatus(`Selected ${PROVIDER_LABELS[nextProvider]}. Ready to generate a personalized video director contract.`);
  }

  function applySample(index: number) {
    const sample = VIDEO_DIRECTOR_LAB_SAMPLES[index];
    if (!sample) return;
    const context = sample.context;
    setLearnerMessage(context.learner_message);
    setTopicLabel(context.learning_context.topic_label ?? "");
    setDiagnosisLabel(context.learning_context.diagnosis_label ?? "unknown");
    setRootProblem(context.learning_context.root_problem ?? "");
    setMisconceptionTarget(context.learning_context.misconception_target ?? "");
    setBridgeLevel(context.learning_context.bridge_level);
    setPriorAttemptSummary(context.learning_context.prior_attempt_summary ?? "");
    setInterests(context.personalization_profile.interests.join(", "));
    setProfileSummary(context.personalization_profile.profile_summary ?? "");
    const fallbackDirector = buildFallbackVideoDirectorContract(context);
    setDirectorContract(fallbackDirector);
    setContract(buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(context.learner_message));
    setSource("deterministic");
    setLastPayload({ ok: true, source: "sample", context });
    setLastError(null);
    setStatus(`Loaded sample: ${sample.label}. Generate to ask the selected model for a custom director plan.`);
  }

  function useDeterministicFallback() {
    const fallback = buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(requestContext.learner_message);
    const directorFallback = buildFallbackVideoDirectorContract(requestContext);
    setContract(fallback);
    setDirectorContract(directorFallback);
    setSource("deterministic");
    setLastError(null);
    setLastPayload({ ok: true, source: "deterministic", contract: fallback, director_contract: directorFallback, context: requestContext });
    setStatus("Showing deterministic MyWay fallback contracts. This tests the generic 3D renderer without calling a model.");
  }

  async function generateWithProvider() {
    setIsGenerating(true);
    setLastError(null);

    const providerLabel = PROVIDER_LABELS[provider];
    setStatus(`Asking ${providerLabel} (${modelName}) to create a personalized video director plan plus 2D fallback scene...`);

    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/sandbox/probe-lab/generated-video/universal-scene-contract", {
        cache: "no-store",
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          provider,
          model: modelName,
          ...requestContext,
          learner_signal: requestContext.learner_message,
        }),
      });

      const data = (await response.json()) as UniversalSceneResponse;
      setLastPayload(data);
      setContract(data.contract);
      setDirectorContract(data.director_contract);

      if (data.ok) {
        const successfulProvider = data.provider ?? provider;
        setSource(successfulProvider);
        setLastError(null);
        setStatus(data.warnings?.join("\n") || `${providerLabel} generated a director contract and MyWay rendered the 3D/hybrid preview.`);
      } else {
        setSource("deterministic");
        setLastError(data.error ?? `Unknown ${providerLabel} route error`);
        setStatus(
          `${providerLabel} route returned ok=false:\n${JSON.stringify(
            {
              error: data.error,
              warnings: data.warnings,
              debug: data.debug,
            },
            null,
            2,
          ).slice(0, 3000)}`,
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[myway-universal-scene-ui]", error);
      const fallback = buildFallbackUniversalSceneContract(requestContext.learner_message);
      const directorFallback = buildFallbackVideoDirectorContract(requestContext);
      setContract(fallback);
      setDirectorContract(directorFallback);
      setSource("deterministic");
      setLastError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown client error");
      setLastPayload({ ok: false, source: "client_catch", error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), context: requestContext });
      setStatus("Client fetch failed. Showing deterministic director fallback so the 3D renderer still works.");
    } finally {
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[460px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
      <aside style={{ ...shellStyle, padding: "1rem" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: "1rem" }}>
          <Field label="Sample contexts">
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.45rem" }}>
              {VIDEO_DIRECTOR_LAB_SAMPLES.map((sample, index) => (
                <button
                  key={sample.label}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => applySample(index)}
                  style={{
                    textAlign: "left",
                    border: sample.context.learner_message === learnerMessage ? "1px solid rgba(221,214,254,0.34)" : "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)",
                    borderRadius: "1rem",
                    background: sample.context.learner_message === learnerMessage ? "rgba(221,214,254,0.14)" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.045)",
                    color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)",
                    padding: "0.64rem 0.72rem",
                    lineHeight: 1.35,
                    cursor: "pointer",
                    fontSize: "0.8rem",
                    fontWeight: 800,
                  }}
                >
                  {sample.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Field>

          <Field label="Learner message or attempt">
            <textarea
              value={learnerMessage}
              onChange={(event) => setLearnerMessage(event.target.value)}
              rows={5}
              placeholder="Type a real MyWay learner message or attempt..."
              style={{ ...textInputStyle(), resize: "vertical" }}
            />
          </Field>

          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Topic label">
              <input value={topicLabel} onChange={(event) => setTopicLabel(event.target.value)} style={textInputStyle()} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Diagnosis">
              <select value={diagnosisLabel} onChange={(event) => setDiagnosisLabel(event.target.value as VideoDirectorDiagnosisLabel)} style={textInputStyle()}>
                {DIAGNOSIS_OPTIONS.map((option) => <option key={option} value={option}>{option}</option>)}
              </select>
            </Field>
          </div>

          <Field label="Root problem">
            <textarea value={rootProblem} onChange={(event) => setRootProblem(event.target.value)} rows={3} style={{ ...textInputStyle(), resize: "vertical" }} />
          </Field>

          <Field label="Misconception target">
            <textarea value={misconceptionTarget} onChange={(event) => setMisconceptionTarget(event.target.value)} rows={2} style={{ ...textInputStyle(), resize: "vertical" }} />
          </Field>

          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Bridge level">
              <select value={bridgeLevel} onChange={(event) => setBridgeLevel(event.target.value as VideoDirectorBridgeLevel)} style={textInputStyle()}>
                {BRIDGE_OPTIONS.map((option) => <option key={option} value={option}>{option}</option>)}
              </select>
            </Field>
            <Field label="Interests">
              <input value={interests} onChange={(event) => setInterests(event.target.value)} style={textInputStyle()} />
            </Field>
          </div>

          <Field label="Prior attempt summary">
            <textarea value={priorAttemptSummary} onChange={(event) => setPriorAttemptSummary(event.target.value)} rows={2} placeholder="Optional: what happened on the last attempt?" style={{ ...textInputStyle(), resize: "vertical" }} />
          </Field>

          <Field label="Profile summary">
            <textarea value={profileSummary} onChange={(event) => setProfileSummary(event.target.value)} rows={2} style={{ ...textInputStyle(), resize: "vertical" }} />
          </Field>

          <Field label="Model provider">
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "0.55rem" }}>
              <ProviderButton provider="nvidia" selected={provider === "nvidia"} onSelect={() => selectProvider("nvidia")} />
              <ProviderButton provider="ollama" selected={provider === "ollama"} onSelect={() => selectProvider("ollama")} />
            </div>
          </Field>

          <Field label="Model name">
            <input value={modelName} onChange={(event) => setModelName(event.target.value)} style={{ ...textInputStyle(), borderRadius: "999px", fontWeight: 850 }} />
          </Field>

          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: "0.6rem" }}>
            <ActionButton disabled={isGenerating} onClick={useDeterministicFallback}>Fallback director</ActionButton>
            <ActionButton disabled={isGenerating} onClick={generateWithProvider} variant="primary">
              {isGenerating ? "Generating..." : `Generate with ${PROVIDER_LABELS[provider]}`}
            </ActionButton>
          </div>

          <section style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.6rem", border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)", borderRadius: "1.25rem", background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.055)", padding: "0.85rem" }}>
            <TinyLabel>Status</TinyLabel>
            <pre style={{ margin: 0, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", color: lastError ? "#fecaca" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.78)", fontSize: "0.8rem", lineHeight: 1.45, fontFamily: "inherit" }}>
              {status}
            </pre>
          </section>

          <section style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "0.55rem" }}>
            <StatCard label="provider" value={PROVIDER_LABELS[provider]} />
            <StatCard label="source" value={source} />
            <StatCard label="3D kind" value={directorContract.renderer_intent.scene_kind} />
            <StatCard label="2D duration" value={`${Math.round(durationInFrames / contract.format.fps)}s`} />
          </section>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section style={{ ...shellStyle, overflow: "hidden" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: "1rem", padding: "1rem" }}>
          <section style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.85rem" }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "1rem", alignItems: "end", flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
              <div>
                <TinyLabel>Hybrid 3D director preview</TinyLabel>
                <h2 style={{ margin: "0.35rem 0 0", color: "white", fontSize: "1.3rem", lineHeight: 1.2 }}>{directorContract.title}</h2>
              </div>
              <ActionButton onClick={() => setShowFallback2D((value) => !value)}>
                {showFallback2D ? "Hide 2D fallback" : "Show 2D fallback"}
              </ActionButton>
            </div>
            <GeneratedVideo3DPlayer contract={directorContract} />
          </section>

          {showFallback2D ? (
            <section style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.75rem" }}>
              <TinyLabel>Trusted Remotion/SVG fallback scene</TinyLabel>
              <div style={{ position: "relative", overflow: "hidden", borderRadius: "1.55rem", border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)", background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.32)" }}>
                <Player
                  component={UniversalSceneRemotionComposition}
                  inputProps={inputProps}
                  durationInFrames={durationInFrames}
                  compositionWidth={contract.format.width}
                  compositionHeight={contract.format.height}
                  fps={contract.format.fps}
                  controls
                  loop
                  style={{ width: "100%" }}
                />
              </div>
            </section>
          ) : null}

          <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,0.9fr)_minmax(0,1.1fr)]">
            <section style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.75rem", border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)", borderRadius: "1.4rem", background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.055)", padding: "1rem" }}>
              <TinyLabel>Model-created director plan</TinyLabel>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)", lineHeight: 1.55 }}>{directorContract.creative_brief.why_this_should_unstick_the_learner}</p>
              <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)", borderRadius: "1rem", background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.14)", padding: "0.75rem" }}>
                <TinyLabel>Aha moment</TinyLabel>
                <p style={{ margin: "0.35rem 0 0", color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)", lineHeight: 1.45, fontSize: "0.9rem", fontWeight: 850 }}>{directorContract.creative_brief.aha_moment}</p>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.5rem" }}>
                {directorContract.beats.map((beat, index) => (
                  <div key={beat.id} style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)", borderRadius: "1rem", background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.16)", padding: "0.75rem" }}>
                    <p style={{ margin: 0, color: "white", fontWeight: 900 }}>{index + 1}. {beat.purpose}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: "0.28rem 0 0", color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.68)", lineHeight: 1.45, fontSize: "0.86rem" }}>{beat.expected_realization}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)", borderRadius: "1.4rem", background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.055)", padding: "1rem" }}>
              <TinyLabel>Video director contract JSON</TinyLabel>
              <pre style={{ margin: "0.75rem 0 0", maxHeight: "42rem", overflow: "auto", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.76)", fontSize: "0.72rem", lineHeight: 1.45 }}>
                {JSON.stringify(directorContract, null, 2)}
              </pre>
            </section>
          </div>

          {lastPayload ? (
            <section style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)", borderRadius: "1.4rem", background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.045)", padding: "1rem" }}>
              <TinyLabel>Last route payload</TinyLabel>
              <pre style={{ margin: "0.75rem 0 0", maxHeight: "18rem", overflow: "auto", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.64)", fontSize: "0.7rem", lineHeight: 1.42 }}>
                {JSON.stringify(lastPayload, null, 2)}
              </pre>
            </section>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export function UniversalSceneRemotionLab() {
  const [hasMounted, setHasMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHasMounted(true);
  }, []);

  if (!hasMounted) {
    return (
      <div
        style={{
          display: "grid",
          placeItems: "center",
          minHeight: "28rem",
          border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)",
          borderRadius: "2rem",
          background: "radial-gradient(circle at top left, rgba(168,85,247,0.2), transparent 34%), rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
          color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.78)",
          fontWeight: 850,
        }}
      >
        Loading video director + 3D renderer sandbox.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <UniversalSceneRemotionLabInner />;
}

const DEFAULT_MESSAGE = "I keep mixing up when Spanish se is reflexive or passive.";
const STORAGE_KEY = "myway_openai_full_loop_v3_state";

function nowIso() {
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

function clamp01(value: number) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
}

function round3(value: number) {
  return Math.round(value * 1000) / 1000;
}

function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
}

function safeString(value: unknown, fallback = "") {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : fallback;
}

function stableIdFromLabel(label: string) {
  const slug = label
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
    .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 56);
  return `topic_${slug || "untitled"}`;
}

function topicSummary(topic: SandboxTopicState) {
  return {
    topic_id: topic.topic_id,
    topic_label: topic.topic_label,
    active_diagnosis: topic.active_diagnosis,
    confusion: topic.confusion,
    insight: topic.insight,
    mastery: topic.mastery,
    evidence_count: topic.evidence_count,
    verification_needed: topic.verification_needed,
    root_problem: topic.root_problem,
    misconception_patterns: topic.misconception_patterns,
    latest_probe_type: topic.latest_probe_type,
    message_count: topic.message_count,
  };
}

function createTopic(args: { topicId?: string | null; topicLabel: string; diagnosis?: string | null; rootProblem?: string | null }): SandboxTopicState {
  const topicLabel = args.topicLabel.trim() || "Untitled topic";
  return {
    topic_id: args.topicId?.trim() || stableIdFromLabel(topicLabel),
    topic_label: topicLabel,
    message_count: 0,
    confusion: 0.45,
    insight: 0.12,
    mastery: 0.05,
    evidence_count: 0,
    active_diagnosis: args.diagnosis ?? null,
    diagnosis_belief: args.diagnosis ? 0.58 : 0,
    diagnosis_confidence: args.diagnosis ? 0.45 : 0,
    diagnosis_status: "active",
    verification_needed: true,
    root_problem: args.rootProblem ?? null,
    misconception_patterns: [],
    latest_probe_type: null,
    updated_at: nowIso(),
    history: [],
  };
}

function defaultProfile(): PersonalizationProfile {
  return {
    bridge_level: "bridge_0",
    jargon_level: "none",
    preferred_style: "visual_first_then_rule",
    interests_text: "basketball, cars, cooking, maps, mechanics",
    interest_use_policy:
      "Use interests only when they make the hidden structure clearer. Prefer them for analogies, concrete examples, or motivation; avoid them when they distract from the target concept.",
    profile_summary:
      "This learner benefits from seeing the hidden structure first, then naming the rule. Use plain language, concrete examples, targeted contrast, and short verification probes. Avoid long abstract explanations. Treat a correct answer with thin evidence as progress, not proof of mastery.",
    learner_notes:
      "The learner is building MyWay and cares about probes feeling intelligent, specific, and not generic. Keep explanations useful and avoid decorating probes with interests unless the interest clarifies the idea.",
    teaching_recipe: {
      preferred_styles_text:
        "visual first then rule, concrete example first, compare and contrast, one targeted question, graph when the gap is spatial or mathematical",
      avoid_styles_text:
        "long lecture, generic encouragement, forced hobby theme, heavy jargon at bridge_0, answer reveal before attempt",
      explanation_shape:
        "Start with the learner's likely mental model, show the key contrast or representation, then ask the smallest probe that exposes the gap.",
      feedback_style:
        "Be specific, calm, and direct. Name what the attempt shows, praise the useful move, and give one next step without overclaiming.",
      challenge_level: "balanced",
      pacing: "normal",
      verification_preference:
        "If the learner is correct but the evidence is thin, generate one small verification probe instead of marking the topic mastered.",
    },
    personalization_events: [],
  };
}

function normalizeProfile(value: unknown): PersonalizationProfile {
  const base = defaultProfile();
  const incoming = asRecord(value);
  const incomingRecipe = asRecord(incoming.teaching_recipe);
  const baseRecipe = base.teaching_recipe;

  return {
    ...base,
    ...incoming,
    bridge_level:
      incoming.bridge_level === "bridge_1" ||
      incoming.bridge_level === "bridge_2" ||
      incoming.bridge_level === "full_bridge" ||
      incoming.bridge_level === "bridge_0"
        ? incoming.bridge_level
        : base.bridge_level,
    jargon_level:
      incoming.jargon_level === "light" ||
      incoming.jargon_level === "standard" ||
      incoming.jargon_level === "full" ||
      incoming.jargon_level === "none"
        ? incoming.jargon_level
        : base.jargon_level,
    preferred_style: safeString(incoming.preferred_style, base.preferred_style),
    interests_text: safeString(incoming.interests_text, base.interests_text),
    interest_use_policy: safeString(incoming.interest_use_policy, base.interest_use_policy),
    learner_notes: safeString(incoming.learner_notes, base.learner_notes),
    profile_summary: safeString(incoming.profile_summary, base.profile_summary),
    personalization_events: Array.isArray(incoming.personalization_events)
      ? incoming.personalization_events.slice(-30)
      : base.personalization_events,
    teaching_recipe: {
      ...baseRecipe,
      ...incomingRecipe,
      preferred_styles_text: safeString(incomingRecipe.preferred_styles_text, baseRecipe.preferred_styles_text),
      avoid_styles_text: safeString(incomingRecipe.avoid_styles_text, baseRecipe.avoid_styles_text),
      explanation_shape: safeString(incomingRecipe.explanation_shape, baseRecipe.explanation_shape),
      feedback_style: safeString(incomingRecipe.feedback_style, baseRecipe.feedback_style),
      challenge_level:
        incomingRecipe.challenge_level === "gentle" ||
        incomingRecipe.challenge_level === "stretch" ||
        incomingRecipe.challenge_level === "test_ready" ||
        incomingRecipe.challenge_level === "balanced"
          ? incomingRecipe.challenge_level
          : baseRecipe.challenge_level,
      pacing:
        incomingRecipe.pacing === "slow" ||
        incomingRecipe.pacing === "fast" ||
        incomingRecipe.pacing === "normal"
          ? incomingRecipe.pacing
          : baseRecipe.pacing,
      verification_preference: safeString(incomingRecipe.verification_preference, baseRecipe.verification_preference),
    },
  };
}

function stringifyAttempt(attempt: ProbeAnswerDraft | null) {
  if (!attempt) return "";
  if (attempt.text_response?.trim()) return attempt.text_response.trim();
  if (attempt.audio_response_transcript?.trim()) return attempt.audio_response_transcript.trim();
  if (attempt.selected_option_id) return attempt.selected_option_id;
  if (attempt.selected_option_ids?.length) return attempt.selected_option_ids.join(", ");
  if (attempt.ordered_item_ids?.length) return attempt.ordered_item_ids.join(" -> ");
  if (attempt.placements && Object.keys(attempt.placements).length > 0) return JSON.stringify(attempt.placements);
  if (typeof attempt.numeric_response === "number") return String(attempt.numeric_response);
  if (attempt.graph_features?.length) return attempt.graph_features.join("\n");
  if (typeof attempt.selected_click_seconds === "number") return String(attempt.selected_click_seconds);
  return "";
}

function normalizeGraphFunctions(value: unknown): ProbeGraphFunctionDraft[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  const functions: ProbeGraphFunctionDraft[] = [];
  for (const item of value.slice(0, 4)) {
    const r = asRecord(item);
    const expression = safeString(r.expression);
    if (!expression) continue;
    functions.push({
      id: safeString(r.id, `fn_${functions.length + 1}`),
      label: safeString(r.label, `f${functions.length + 1}`),
      expression,
      enabled: typeof r.enabled === "boolean" ? r.enabled : true,
    });
  }
  return functions;
}

function normalizeGraphParameters(value: unknown): ProbeGraphParameterDraft[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  const parameters: ProbeGraphParameterDraft[] = [];
  for (const item of value.slice(0, 5)) {
    const r = asRecord(item);
    const name = safeString(r.name).replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, "").slice(0, 1);
    const valueNumber = typeof r.value === "number" && Number.isFinite(r.value) ? r.value : null;
    if (!name || valueNumber === null) continue;
    parameters.push({
      name,
      value: valueNumber,
      min: typeof r.min === "number" ? r.min : -5,
      max: typeof r.max === "number" ? r.max : 5,
      step: typeof r.step === "number" ? r.step : 0.1,
    });
  }
  return parameters;
}

function seedDraftForProbe(probe: EngineRenderableProbe): ProbeAnswerDraft {
  const draft = createEmptyProbeAnswerDraft(probe.expected_attempt_type);
  const rendererParams = asRecord(probe.renderer_params);
  const graph = asRecord(rendererParams.graph);
  const mode = graph.mode === "3d" ? "3d" : graph.mode === "2d" ? "2d" : null;

  if (probe.probe_type === "graph_relationship" || probe.expected_attempt_type === "graph") {
    return {
      ...draft,
      graph_mode: mode ?? draft.graph_mode,
      graph_functions: normalizeGraphFunctions(graph.functions),
      graph_parameters: normalizeGraphParameters(graph.parameters),
      graph_window: asRecord(graph.window) as ProbeAnswerDraft["graph_window"],
      graph_3d_window: asRecord(graph.window3d) as ProbeAnswerDraft["graph_3d_window"],
      graph_3d_view: asRecord(graph.view3d) as ProbeAnswerDraft["graph_3d_view"],
      graph_surface_expression: safeString(graph.surface_expression, draft.graph_surface_expression ?? ""),
      graph_notes: safeString(graph.prompt_notes, draft.graph_notes ?? ""),
    };
  }

  return draft;
}

function misconceptionLabels(probe: EngineRenderableProbe) {
  return (probe.misconception_markers ?? [])
    .map((marker) => marker.label || marker.misconception_id)
    .filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.trim().length > 0)
    .slice(0, 8);
}

function applyDelta(topic: SandboxTopicState, delta?: MetricDelta | null) {
  if (!delta) return topic;
  return {
    ...topic,
    confusion: round3(clamp01(topic.confusion + delta.confusion_delta)),
    insight: round3(clamp01(topic.insight + delta.insight_delta)),
    mastery: round3(clamp01(topic.mastery + delta.mastery_delta)),
    evidence_count: Math.max(0, topic.evidence_count + Math.max(0, Math.floor(delta.evidence_count_delta))),
    verification_needed: delta.verification_needed,
    updated_at: nowIso(),
  };
}

function topicFromGenerate(args: { previous?: SandboxTopicState; output: GenerateOutput; fallbackTopicId?: string | null }) {
  const diagnosis = asRecord(args.output.diagnosis);
  const topicDecision = args.output.topic_decision;
  const label = topicDecision?.topic_label || safeString(diagnosis.target_topic_label, "Auto-detected topic");
  const topicId = topicDecision?.topic_id || args.fallbackTopicId || stableIdFromLabel(label);
  const base = args.previous ?? createTopic({ topicId, topicLabel: label, diagnosis: safeString(diagnosis.diagnosis, "unknown"), rootProblem: safeString(diagnosis.root_problem) });
  const withDelta = applyDelta(base, args.output.metric_delta ?? null);
  return {
    ...withDelta,
    topic_id: base.topic_id || topicId,
    topic_label: label,
    message_count: base.message_count + 1,
    active_diagnosis: safeString(diagnosis.diagnosis, base.active_diagnosis ?? "unknown"),
    diagnosis_belief: round3(clamp01(Math.max(base.diagnosis_belief, Number(diagnosis.diagnosis_confidence ?? 0.5) * 0.82))),
    diagnosis_confidence: round3(clamp01(Number(diagnosis.diagnosis_confidence ?? base.diagnosis_confidence))),
    diagnosis_status: withDelta.verification_needed ? "active" : withDelta.mastery > 0.72 ? "weakening" : "active",
    root_problem: safeString(diagnosis.root_problem, base.root_problem ?? ""),
    misconception_patterns: [...new Set([...base.misconception_patterns, ...misconceptionLabels(args.output.probe)])].slice(0, 12),
    latest_probe_type: args.output.probe.probe_type,
    updated_at: nowIso(),
  } satisfies SandboxTopicState;
}

function topicFromEvaluation(topic: SandboxTopicState, evaluation: EvaluationOutput, probe: EngineRenderableProbe): SandboxTopicState {
  const next = applyDelta(topic, evaluation.metric_delta ?? null);
  const hits = (evaluation.misconception_hits ?? [])
    .map((hit) => hit.label || hit.misconception_id)
    .filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.trim().length > 0);
  const evidence = asRecord(evaluation.understanding_evidence);
  return {
    ...next,
    evidence_count: Math.max(next.evidence_count, topic.evidence_count + 1),
    verification_needed: Boolean(evidence.needs_verification_probe ?? next.verification_needed),
    diagnosis_status: evaluation.correctness >= 0.88 && !Boolean(evidence.needs_verification_probe) ? "weakening" : "active",
    latest_probe_type: probe.probe_type,
    misconception_patterns: [...new Set([...next.misconception_patterns, ...hits])].slice(0, 12),
    updated_at: nowIso(),
  };
}

function probeEvidenceContext(probe: EngineRenderableProbe | null) {
  if (!probe) return null;
  const selectedOptionProbe = probe.expected_attempt_type === "single_choice" || probe.expected_attempt_type === "multi_choice";
  const placementProbe = probe.expected_attempt_type === "drag_drop_placements" || probe.expected_attempt_type === "ordered_items";
  const graphProbe = probe.expected_attempt_type === "graph";
  const textProbe = probe.expected_attempt_type === "text";

  return {
    probe_type: probe.probe_type,
    expected_attempt_type: probe.expected_attempt_type,
    evidence_bandwidth: selectedOptionProbe ? "low" : placementProbe || graphProbe ? "medium" : textProbe ? "high" : "medium",
    can_measure: selectedOptionProbe
      ? ["recognition", "basic discrimination"]
      : placementProbe
        ? ["classification", "mapping", "pattern separation"]
        : graphProbe
          ? ["visual model", "representation mapping", "feature noticing"]
          : textProbe
            ? ["explanation", "reasoning", "transfer depth"]
            : ["task performance"],
    cannot_measure: selectedOptionProbe
      ? ["explanation", "reasoning chain", "transfer depth"]
      : [],
    evaluator_rule: selectedOptionProbe
      ? "Do not criticize the learner for not explaining. The probe did not ask for explanation. Treat a correct selected option as recognition evidence and cap evidence strength unless another response field shows reasoning."
      : "Judge only what the delivered probe actually asked the learner to do.",
  };
}

function messageUnderstandingLabel(output: GenerateOutput | null) {
  const score = output?.message_understanding?.confidence;
  if (typeof score !== "number") return "unknown";
  if (score < 0.45) return "low";
  if (score < 0.72) return "medium";
  return "high";
}

function JsonPanel({ title, value, open = true }: { title: string; value: unknown; open?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <details className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-black/25 p-4" open={open}>
      <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-zinc-300/75">
        {title}
      </summary>
      <pre className="mt-3 max-h-96 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 p-4 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-200">
        {value ? JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) : "Not generated yet."}
      </pre>
    </details>
  );
}

function MetricCard({ label, value, help }: { label: string; value: number; help: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-zinc-300/70">{label}</span>
        <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{value.toFixed(2)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/10">
        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-white/55" style={{ width: `${Math.round(clamp01(value) * 100)}%` }} />
      </div>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-400">{help}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export function OpenAIFullLoopLab() {
  const [learnerMessage, setLearnerMessage] = useState(DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
  const [topicLabel, setTopicLabel] = useState("Auto-detect from learner message");
  const [topicMode, setTopicMode] = useState<TopicMode>("auto");
  const [model, setModel] = useState("gpt-5.5");
  const [imageModel, setImageModel] = useState("gpt-image-1");
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<PersonalizationProfile>(() => defaultProfile());
  const [topics, setTopics] = useState<Record<string, SandboxTopicState>>({});
  const [activeTopicId, setActiveTopicId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<SandboxTurn[]>([]);
  const [generateResult, setGenerateResult] = useState<ApiResult<GenerateOutput> | null>(null);
  const [evaluationResult, setEvaluationResult] = useState<ApiResult<EvaluationOutput> | null>(null);
  const [relationshipResult, setRelationshipResult] = useState<RelationshipResult | null>(null);
  const [imageResult, setImageResult] = useState<ImageResult | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ProbeAnswerDraft | null>(null);
  const [submittedAttempt, setSubmittedAttempt] = useState<ProbeRendererSubmitPayload | null>(null);
  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [isEvaluating, setIsEvaluating] = useState(false);
  const [isGeneratingImage, setIsGeneratingImage] = useState(false);
  const [isBuildingRelationships, setIsBuildingRelationships] = useState(false);
  const [loadedFromStorage, setLoadedFromStorage] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as {
          topics?: Record<string, SandboxTopicState>;
          activeTopicId?: string | null;
          history?: SandboxTurn[];
          profile?: PersonalizationProfile;
        };
        setTopics(parsed.topics ?? {});
        setActiveTopicId(parsed.activeTopicId ?? null);
        setHistory(parsed.history ?? []);
        setProfile(normalizeProfile(parsed.profile));
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore local sandbox restore failures.
    } finally {
      setLoadedFromStorage(true);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loadedFromStorage) return;
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify({ topics, activeTopicId, history: history.slice(-40), profile }),
    );
  }, [activeTopicId, history, loadedFromStorage, profile, topics]);

  const output = generateResult?.output ?? null;
  const probe = output?.probe ?? null;
  const visibleProbe = output?.turn_mode === "clarify_first" ? null : probe;
  const activeTopic = activeTopicId ? topics[activeTopicId] ?? null : null;
  const activeDraft = useMemo(() => draft ?? (probe ? seedDraftForProbe(probe) : null), [draft, probe]);
  const attemptPreview = stringifyAttempt(activeDraft);
  const topicList = useMemo(() => Object.values(topics).sort((a, b) => b.updated_at.localeCompare(a.updated_at)), [topics]);
  const interests = useMemo(
    () => profile.interests_text.split(",").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 8),
    [profile.interests_text],
  );

  function upsertTopic(nextTopic: SandboxTopicState, turn?: SandboxTurn) {
    setTopics((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [nextTopic.topic_id]: {
        ...nextTopic,
        history: turn ? [...nextTopic.history, turn].slice(-20) : nextTopic.history,
      },
    }));
    setActiveTopicId(nextTopic.topic_id);
    if (turn) setHistory((current) => [turn, ...current].slice(0, 60));
  }

  function currentPayload() {
    return {
      active_topic_id: activeTopicId,
      existing_topics: topicList.map(topicSummary),
      current_topic_state: activeTopic ? topicSummary(activeTopic) : null,
      recent_history: history.slice(0, 10),
      personalization_context: {
        schema_version: "sandbox_personalization_profile_v31",
        bridge_level: profile.bridge_level,
        language_policy: { jargon_level: profile.jargon_level },
        preferred_style: profile.preferred_style,
        teaching_recipe: {
          preferred_styles: profile.teaching_recipe.preferred_styles_text
            .split(",")
            .map((item) => item.trim())
            .filter(Boolean)
            .slice(0, 10),
          avoid_styles: profile.teaching_recipe.avoid_styles_text
            .split(",")
            .map((item) => item.trim())
            .filter(Boolean)
            .slice(0, 10),
          explanation_shape: profile.teaching_recipe.explanation_shape,
          feedback_style: profile.teaching_recipe.feedback_style,
          challenge_level: profile.teaching_recipe.challenge_level,
          pacing: profile.teaching_recipe.pacing,
          verification_preference: profile.teaching_recipe.verification_preference,
        },
        user_interests: interests,
        interests: interests.map((domain) => ({
          domain,
          usefulness: 0.5,
          use_when: profile.interest_use_policy,
          avoid_when: "Avoid using this interest when it does not clarify the target concept or would make the probe feel gimmicky.",
          recent_use_count: 0,
        })),
        interest_use_policy: profile.interest_use_policy,
        profile_summary: profile.profile_summary,
        learner_notes: profile.learner_notes,
        personalization_events: profile.personalization_events.slice(-8),
      },
    };
  }

  async function generateProbe(reason: "message" | "followup_from_attempt" = "message") {
    setIsGenerating(true);
    setGenerateResult(null);
    setEvaluationResult(reason === "message" ? null : evaluationResult);
    setImageResult(null);
    setDraft(null);
    setSubmittedAttempt(null);

    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/sandbox/probe-lab/openai-full-loop/generate", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          learner_message:
            reason === "followup_from_attempt"
              ? `Generate the next best follow-up probe from the latest evaluated attempt. Original learner signal: ${learnerMessage}`
              : learnerMessage,
          target_topic_label: topicLabel,
          topic_mode: reason === "followup_from_attempt" ? "continue_active" : topicMode,
          bridge_level: profile.bridge_level,
          jargon_level: profile.jargon_level,
          preferred_style: profile.preferred_style,
          user_interests: interests,
          profile_summary: profile.profile_summary,
          previous_evaluation: evaluationResult?.output ?? null,
          turn_mode: output?.turn_mode ?? "probe_only",
          generate_reason: reason,
          model,
          ...currentPayload(),
        }),
      });

      const data = (await response.json()) as ApiResult<GenerateOutput>;
      setGenerateResult(data);

      if (data.output?.probe) {
        setDraft(seedDraftForProbe(data.output.probe));
      }

      if (data.output) {
        const decision = data.output.topic_decision;
        const diagnosis = asRecord(data.output.diagnosis);
        const targetId =
          decision?.action === "continue_existing_topic" && activeTopic
            ? activeTopic.topic_id
            : decision?.action === "switch_to_existing_topic" && decision.topic_id && topics[decision.topic_id]
              ? decision.topic_id
              : decision?.action === "create_new_topic"
                ? decision.topic_id || stableIdFromLabel(decision.topic_label)
                : activeTopic?.topic_id ?? decision?.topic_id ?? stableIdFromLabel(decision?.topic_label || safeString(diagnosis.target_topic_label, "New topic"));
        const previous = topics[targetId] ?? null;
        const nextTopic = topicFromGenerate({ previous: previous ?? undefined, output: data.output, fallbackTopicId: targetId });
        const turn: SandboxTurn = {
          id: `turn_${Date.now()}`,
          kind: reason === "followup_from_attempt" ? "followup_probe" : "generated_probe",
          topic_id: nextTopic.topic_id,
          topic_label: nextTopic.topic_label,
          created_at: nowIso(),
          summary: `${reason === "followup_from_attempt" ? "Follow-up" : "Generated"} ${data.output.probe.probe_type} probe · ${data.output.rationale.what_it_measures}`,
          probe_type: data.output.probe.probe_type,
          diagnosis: safeString(diagnosis.diagnosis, null as unknown as string) || null,
          correctness: null,
          metric_delta: data.output.metric_delta ?? null,
        };
        upsertTopic(nextTopic, turn);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setGenerateResult({ ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown generate error" });
    } finally {
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }
  }

  async function evaluateAttempt() {
    if (!probe || !activeDraft) return;
    setIsEvaluating(true);
    setEvaluationResult(null);

    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/sandbox/probe-lab/openai-full-loop/evaluate", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model,
          learner_message: learnerMessage,
          diagnosis: output?.diagnosis ?? null,
          probe,
          attempt: activeDraft,
          probe_evidence_context: probeEvidenceContext(probe),
          topic_state: activeTopic ? topicSummary(activeTopic) : null,
          ...currentPayload(),
        }),
      });

      const data = (await response.json()) as ApiResult<EvaluationOutput>;
      setEvaluationResult(data);

      const evaluationOutput = data.output;
      if (!evaluationOutput) {
        return;
      }

      if (data.output && activeTopic) {
        const nextTopic = topicFromEvaluation(activeTopic, data.output, probe);
        const turn: SandboxTurn = {
          id: `turn_${Date.now()}`,
          kind: "attempt_evaluation",
          topic_id: nextTopic.topic_id,
          topic_label: nextTopic.topic_label,
          created_at: nowIso(),
          summary: String(evaluationOutput.correctness_summary ?? "Attempt evaluated."),
          probe_type: probe.probe_type,
          diagnosis: nextTopic.active_diagnosis,
          correctness: evaluationOutput.correctness,
          metric_delta: evaluationOutput.metric_delta ?? null,
        };
        upsertTopic(nextTopic, turn);

        if (evaluationOutput.personalization_delta) {
          setProfile((current) => ({
            ...current,
            personalization_events: [...current.personalization_events, evaluationOutput.personalization_delta].slice(-20),
            profile_summary: `${current.profile_summary}\nLatest evidence: ${(evaluationOutput.learner_feedback as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.suggested_next_step ?? String(evaluationOutput.correctness_summary ?? "Attempt evaluated.")}`.slice(0, 1200),
          }));
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setEvaluationResult({ ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown evaluate error" });
    } finally {
      setIsEvaluating(false);
    }
  }

  async function generateImage() {
    if (!output?.image_prompt) return;
    setIsGeneratingImage(true);
    setImageResult(null);

    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/sandbox/probe-lab/openai-full-loop/generate-image", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: output.image_prompt, model: imageModel, size: "1536x1024" }),
      });
      setImageResult((await response.json()) as ImageResult);
    } catch (error) {
      setImageResult({ ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown image error" });
    } finally {
      setIsGeneratingImage(false);
    }
  }

  async function buildRelationships() {
    setIsBuildingRelationships(true);
    setRelationshipResult(null);
    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/sandbox/probe-lab/openai-full-loop/relationships", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ topics: topicList.map(topicSummary) }),
      });
      setRelationshipResult((await response.json()) as RelationshipResult);
    } catch (error) {
      setRelationshipResult({ ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown relationship error" });
    } finally {
      setIsBuildingRelationships(false);
    }
  }

  function resetSandbox() {
    setTopics({});
    setActiveTopicId(null);
    setHistory([]);
    setGenerateResult(null);
    setEvaluationResult(null);
    setRelationshipResult(null);
    setDraft(null);
    setSubmittedAttempt(null);
    window.localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  }

  return (
    <section className="grid gap-5">
      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_23rem]">
        <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.055] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <label className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-emerald-100/70">
              Learner message or new topic switch
            </span>
            <textarea
              value={learnerMessage}
              onChange={(event) => setLearnerMessage(event.target.value)}
              className="min-h-36 rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-emerald-200/45"
            />
          </label>

          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
            <label className="grid gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-zinc-300/70">Topic mode</span>
              <select value={topicMode} onChange={(event) => setTopicMode(event.target.value as TopicMode)} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-200/45">
                <option value="auto">Auto route topic</option>
                <option value="continue_active">Continue active topic</option>
                <option value="force_new">Force new topic</option>
                <option value="switch_selected">Switch to selected topic</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-zinc-300/70">Topic hint</span>
              <input value={topicLabel} onChange={(event) => setTopicLabel(event.target.value)} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-200/45" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-zinc-300/70">OpenAI model</span>
              <input value={model} onChange={(event) => setModel(event.target.value)} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-200/45" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-zinc-300/70">Image model</span>
              <input value={imageModel} onChange={(event) => setImageModel(event.target.value)} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-200/45" />
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => generateProbe("message")} disabled={isGenerating || !learnerMessage.trim()} className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100/30 bg-emerald-300/16 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-50 transition hover:bg-emerald-300/22 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50">
              {isGenerating ? "Generating..." : "Send message / generate probe"}
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={evaluateAttempt} disabled={isEvaluating || !visibleProbe || !activeDraft || !attemptPreview} className="rounded-2xl border border-purple-100/25 bg-purple-300/13 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-purple-50 transition hover:bg-purple-300/20 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45">
              {isEvaluating ? "Evaluating..." : "Evaluate current attempt"}
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => generateProbe("followup_from_attempt")} disabled={isGenerating || !evaluationResult?.output || !activeTopic} className="rounded-2xl border border-cyan-100/25 bg-cyan-300/13 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-cyan-50 transition hover:bg-cyan-300/20 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45">
              Generate next probe from attempt
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={generateImage} disabled={isGeneratingImage || !output?.image_prompt} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.07] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 transition hover:bg-white/[0.11] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45">
              {isGeneratingImage ? "Generating image..." : "Generate optional image"}
            </button>
          </div>

          {generateResult?.error ? <div className="mt-4 rounded-3xl border border-red-200/20 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-red-100">{generateResult.error}</div> : null}
          {evaluationResult?.error ? <div className="mt-4 rounded-3xl border border-red-200/20 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-red-100">{evaluationResult.error}</div> : null}
        </section>

        <aside className="grid gap-4 rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.055] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-emerald-100/70">Active topic</p>
              <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-white">{activeTopic?.topic_label ?? "No topic yet"}</h2>
            </div>
            <button type="button" onClick={resetSandbox} className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/25 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-200 hover:bg-white/10">Reset</button>
          </div>

          {activeTopic ? (
            <div className="grid gap-3">
              <MetricCard label="confusion" value={activeTopic.confusion} help="0 = clarity, 1 = total confusion" />
              <MetricCard label="insight" value={activeTopic.insight} help="0 = no insight, 1 = breakthrough synthesis" />
              <MetricCard label="mastery" value={activeTopic.mastery} help="Cautious sandbox learning score" />
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-3 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-300">
                <p><b>Diagnosis:</b> {activeTopic.active_diagnosis ?? "unknown"}</p>
                <p><b>Evidence:</b> {activeTopic.evidence_count}</p>
                <p><b>Verification:</b> {activeTopic.verification_needed ? "needed" : "not needed right now"}</p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">{activeTopic.root_problem ?? "No root problem yet."}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-300/80">Send a message to create or continue a topic.</p>
          )}
        </aside>
      </div>

      <section className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[23rem_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <aside className="grid gap-4 rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.045] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-zinc-300/70">Personalization</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-300/80">
              Richer sandbox-local profile. This should shape probe choice, explanation shape, feedback, verification, and interest use over time.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-3">
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Bridge
              <select value={profile.bridge_level} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, bridge_level: event.target.value as BridgeLevel, jargon_level: event.target.value === "bridge_0" ? "none" : current.jargon_level }))} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case tracking-normal text-white outline-none">
                <option value="bridge_0">bridge_0 · no jargon</option>
                <option value="bridge_1">bridge_1 · guided</option>
                <option value="bridge_2">bridge_2 · independent</option>
                <option value="full_bridge">full_bridge · test</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Jargon
              <select value={profile.jargon_level} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, jargon_level: event.target.value as JargonLevel }))} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case tracking-normal text-white outline-none">
                <option value="none">none</option><option value="light">light</option><option value="standard">standard</option><option value="full">full</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Primary style key
              <input value={profile.preferred_style} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, preferred_style: event.target.value }))} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Preferred styles
              <textarea value={profile.teaching_recipe.preferred_styles_text} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, preferred_styles_text: event.target.value } }))} className="min-h-20 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Avoid styles
              <textarea value={profile.teaching_recipe.avoid_styles_text} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, avoid_styles_text: event.target.value } }))} className="min-h-20 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Explanation shape
              <textarea value={profile.teaching_recipe.explanation_shape} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, explanation_shape: event.target.value } }))} className="min-h-24 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Feedback style
              <textarea value={profile.teaching_recipe.feedback_style} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, feedback_style: event.target.value } }))} className="min-h-20 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Challenge
                <select value={profile.teaching_recipe.challenge_level} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, challenge_level: event.target.value as PersonalizationProfile["teaching_recipe"]["challenge_level"] } }))} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case tracking-normal text-white outline-none">
                  <option value="gentle">gentle</option><option value="balanced">balanced</option><option value="stretch">stretch</option><option value="test_ready">test ready</option>
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Pacing
                <select value={profile.teaching_recipe.pacing} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, pacing: event.target.value as PersonalizationProfile["teaching_recipe"]["pacing"] } }))} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case tracking-normal text-white outline-none">
                  <option value="slow">slow</option><option value="normal">normal</option><option value="fast">fast</option>
                </select>
              </label>
            </div>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Verification rule
              <textarea value={profile.teaching_recipe.verification_preference} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, teaching_recipe: { ...current.teaching_recipe, verification_preference: event.target.value } }))} className="min-h-20 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Interests
              <input value={profile.interests_text} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, interests_text: event.target.value }))} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Interest use policy
              <textarea value={profile.interest_use_policy} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, interest_use_policy: event.target.value }))} className="min-h-24 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Learner notes
              <textarea value={profile.learner_notes} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, learner_notes: event.target.value }))} className="min-h-24 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
            <label className="grid gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-zinc-300/70">Profile summary
              <textarea value={profile.profile_summary} onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, profile_summary: event.target.value }))} className="min-h-32 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm normal-case leading-6 tracking-normal text-white outline-none" />
            </label>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <div className="grid gap-5">
          {output ? (
            <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.055] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-zinc-300/70">Message understanding</p>
                  <h3 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold text-white">{messageUnderstandingLabel(output)} confidence · {output.turn_mode ?? "probe_only"}</h3>
                </div>
                <span className="rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-200">
                  {typeof output.message_understanding?.confidence === "number" ? output.message_understanding.confidence.toFixed(2) : "n/a"}
                </span>
              </div>
              {output.message_understanding ? (
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-300">{output.message_understanding.what_myway_thinks_user_means}</p>
              ) : null}
              {output.message_understanding?.ambiguity ? (
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-100/85">Ambiguity: {output.message_understanding.ambiguity}</p>
              ) : null}
            </section>
          ) : null}

          {output?.turn_mode === "clarify_first" && output.clarification ? (
            <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-amber-200/20 bg-amber-300/10 p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-amber-100/75">Clarification needed before teaching/probing</p>
              <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold text-white">{output.clarification.question}</h3>
              <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-50/80">{output.clarification.reason}</p>
              {output.clarification.options?.length ? (
                <div className="mt-4 grid gap-2">
                  {output.clarification.options.map((option) => (
                    <button key={option} type="button" onClick={() => setLearnerMessage(`${learnerMessage}\n\nClarification answer: ${option}`)} className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-100/20 bg-black/20 px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm text-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-200/10">
                      {option}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </section>
          ) : null}

          {output?.orientation ? (
            <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-cyan-100/20 bg-cyan-300/10 p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-cyan-100/75">Orientation before the check</p>
              <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold text-white">{output.orientation.title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-6 text-cyan-50/88">{output.orientation.body}</p>
              {output.orientation.visual_scene ? <p className="mt-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-200">{output.orientation.visual_scene}</p> : null}
              {output.orientation.key_points?.length ? (
                <ul className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-200">
                  {output.orientation.key_points.map((point) => <li key={point}>• {point}</li>)}
                </ul>
              ) : null}
              {output.orientation.bridge_note ? <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-400">{output.orientation.bridge_note}</p> : null}
            </section>
          ) : null}

          {output?.interest_discovery ? (
            <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-purple-100/20 bg-purple-300/10 p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-purple-100/75">Interest discovery</p>
              {output.interest_discovery.should_ask ? <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-purple-50/85">{output.interest_discovery.question}</p> : null}
              {output.interest_discovery.inferred_candidates?.length ? (
                <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2">
                  {output.interest_discovery.inferred_candidates.map((candidate) => (
                    <div key={candidate.interest} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-200">
                      <b>{candidate.interest}</b> · confidence {candidate.confidence.toFixed(2)}
                      <p className="text-zinc-400">Use when: {candidate.use_when}</p>
                      <p className="text-zinc-500">Avoid when: {candidate.avoid_when}</p>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </section>
          ) : null}

          {imageResult?.image_url ? (
            <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.055] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-zinc-300/70">Generated image stimulus</p>
              <img src={imageResult.image_url} alt="Generated probe stimulus" className="mt-4 w-full max-w-4xl rounded-3xl border border-white/10 object-cover shadow-2xl" />
            </section>
          ) : imageResult?.error ? <div className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200/20 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-red-100">{imageResult.error}</div> : null}

          {visibleProbe ? (
            <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-black/[0.22] p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
              <ProbeRenderer
                key={`${visibleProbe.probe_type}-${visibleProbe.expected_attempt_type}-${visibleProbe.prompt.task}`}
                probe={visibleProbe}
                initialDraft={activeDraft ?? undefined}
                showDebug
                onDraftChange={(nextDraft) => { setDraft(nextDraft); setSubmittedAttempt(null); }}
                onSubmit={(payload) => { setSubmittedAttempt(payload); setDraft(payload.attempt); }}
              />
            </section>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <div className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.045] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-zinc-300/70">Topics</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">Switch topics without losing progress.</p>
            </div>
            <button type="button" onClick={buildRelationships} disabled={isBuildingRelationships || topicList.length < 2} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.07] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 hover:bg-white/[0.11] disabled:opacity-45">
              {isBuildingRelationships ? "Building..." : "Build relationships"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-2">
            {topicList.length ? topicList.map((topic) => (
              <button key={topic.topic_id} type="button" onClick={() => setActiveTopicId(topic.topic_id)} className={`rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left transition ${topic.topic_id === activeTopicId ? "border-emerald-200/35 bg-emerald-200/12" : "border-white/10 bg-black/15 hover:bg-white/[0.075]"}`}>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><p className="font-semibold text-white">{topic.topic_label}</p><span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{topic.evidence_count} evidence</span></div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-400">{topic.active_diagnosis ?? "unknown"} · confusion {topic.confusion.toFixed(2)} · insight {topic.insight.toFixed(2)} · mastery {topic.mastery.toFixed(2)}</p>
              </button>
            )) : <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">No topics yet.</p>}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.045] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-zinc-300/70">Relationship preview</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">Shared diagnosis links are only between different topics. Continuation of one topic should update the same topic, not create a relationship.</p>
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3">
            {relationshipResult?.result?.relationships?.length ? relationshipResult.result.relationships.map((relationship) => (
              <div key={relationship.relationship_id} className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><span className="rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-zinc-200">{relationship.relationship_type}</span><span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">strength {relationship.strength.toFixed(2)} · confidence {relationship.confidence.toFixed(2)}</span></div>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-300">{relationship.evidence_summary ?? "No summary."}</p>
                {relationship.reasons?.length ? <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-500">{relationship.reasons.slice(0, 5).join(" · ")}</p> : null}
              </div>
            )) : <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Build relationships after at least two topics exist.</p>}
            {relationshipResult?.error ? <div className="rounded-2xl border border-red-200/20 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-red-100">{relationshipResult.error}</div> : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
<JsonPanel title="Message understanding / orientation" value={{ message_understanding: output?.message_understanding, turn_mode: output?.turn_mode, clarification: output?.clarification, orientation: output?.orientation, interest_discovery: output?.interest_discovery }} />
        <JsonPanel title="Diagnosis + probe output" value={output} />
        <JsonPanel title="Current attempt" value={{ submitted_attempt: submittedAttempt?.attempt ?? null, active_draft: activeDraft, response_summary: attemptPreview || null }} />
        <JsonPanel title="Evaluation output" value={evaluationResult?.output ?? evaluationResult} />
        <JsonPanel title="Relationship graph output" value={relationshipResult} open={false} />
        <JsonPanel title="Sandbox topics" value={topicList.map(topicSummary)} open={false} />
        <JsonPanel title="History" value={history} open={false} />
      </section>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { EngineRenderableProbe, ProbeAttemptType, ProbeType } from "@/lib/engine";
import {
  ProbeRenderer,
  createEmptyProbeAnswerDraft,
  type ProbeAnswerDraft,
  type ProbeRendererSubmitPayload,
} from "@/ui/learning-space/probes";

type ProbeTemplateId =
  | "single_choice"
  | "multi_choice"
  | "drag_drop_placements"
  | "sequence"
  | "slider"
  | "graph_relationship"
  | "audio_clip_question"
  | "audio_response_question"
  | "video_click_interval"
  | "video_explanation";

type ProbeTemplate = {
  id: ProbeTemplateId;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  useWhen: string;
  status: "interactive" | "placeholder" | "media-needed";
  probe: EngineRenderableProbe;
};

function makeProbe(args: {
  probeType: ProbeType;
  attemptType: ProbeAttemptType;
  task: string;
  fullPrompt: string;
  rootProblem?: string;
  reshaping?: string;
  rendererParams?: Record<string, unknown>;
  answerKey?: Record<string, unknown>;
}): EngineRenderableProbe {
  return {
    schema_version: "engine_renderable_probe_v1",
    probe_type: args.probeType,
    expected_attempt_type: args.attemptType,
    prompt: {
      root_problem_explanation:
        args.rootProblem ?? "This template checks one specific piece of the learner's thinking.",
      reshaping_explanation:
        args.reshaping ?? "The probe should make the learner's current mental model visible.",
      task: args.task,
      full_prompt: args.fullPrompt,
    },
    presentation_support: undefined,
    answer_key: (args.answerKey ?? {}) as EngineRenderableProbe["answer_key"],
    misconception_markers: [],
    renderer_params: (args.rendererParams ?? {}) as EngineRenderableProbe["renderer_params"],
    delivery_context: null,
    confidence: 0.72,
    renderer_compatibility: {
      renderer_kind: args.probeType,
      is_renderable: true,
      blocking_reasons: [],
      warnings: [],
    },
  };
}

const TEMPLATES: ProbeTemplate[] = [
  {
    id: "single_choice",
    title: "Single choice",
    subtitle: "One best answer with targeted distractors.",
    useWhen: "Best for discrimination gaps, quick misconception checks, and confidence-friendly first probes.",
    status: "interactive",
    probe: makeProbe({
      probeType: "single_choice",
      attemptType: "single_choice",
      task: "Choose the sentence where Spanish se is reflexive.",
      fullPrompt:
        "The word se appears in both examples. Do not decide from se alone. Choose the example where the action returns to the same person.",
      rootProblem: "The learner is treating se as if it has one fixed meaning.",
      rendererParams: {
        options: [
          {
            id: "self_action",
            label: "A",
            text: "Marta se lava las manos. The action starts with Marta and returns to Marta.",
          },
          {
            id: "passive_action",
            label: "B",
            text: "Se venden boletos. The sentence says tickets are sold, but no seller is named.",
          },
          {
            id: "surface_match",
            label: "C",
            text: "Any sentence with se must be reflexive because the same word appears.",
          },
        ],
      },
      answerKey: { correct_option_id: "self_action" },
    }),
  },
  {
    id: "multi_choice",
    title: "Multi choice",
    subtitle: "Several true cues can be selected together.",
    useWhen: "Useful when the learner needs to combine evidence instead of relying on one surface cue.",
    status: "interactive",
    probe: makeProbe({
      probeType: "multi_choice",
      attemptType: "multi_choice",
      task: "Select every cue that helps decide what se is doing.",
      fullPrompt:
        "Choose the cues that actually help you decide whether se is reflexive or passive-like. Ignore cues that only look helpful.",
      rootProblem: "The learner needs a better evidence checklist.",
      rendererParams: {
