function statusLabel(status: ProbeTemplate["status"]) {
  if (status === "interactive") return "interactive";
  if (status === "media-needed") return "needs media";
  return "placeholder";
}

function statusTone(status: ProbeTemplate["status"]) {
  if (status === "interactive") return "border-emerald-200/25 bg-emerald-200/10 text-emerald-100";
  if (status === "media-needed") return "border-amber-200/25 bg-amber-200/10 text-amber-100";
  return "border-zinc-200/20 bg-zinc-200/10 text-zinc-100";
}

export function ProbeTemplateGallery() {
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<ProbeTemplateId>("single_choice");
  const [latestDraft, setLatestDraft] = useState<ProbeAnswerDraft>(() =>
    createEmptyProbeAnswerDraft("single_choice"),
  );
  const [lastSubmit, setLastSubmit] = useState<ProbeRendererSubmitPayload | null>(null);

  const selectedTemplate = useMemo(
    () => TEMPLATES.find((template) => template.id === selectedId) ?? TEMPLATES[0]!,
    [selectedId],
  );

  function selectTemplate(template: ProbeTemplate) {
    setSelectedId(template.id);
    setLatestDraft(createEmptyProbeAnswerDraft(template.probe.expected_attempt_type));
    setLastSubmit(null);
  }

  return (
    <section className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(19rem,0.38fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
      <aside className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.055] p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-purple-100/70">
              Probe templates
            </p>
            <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-white">
              Renderer gallery
            </h2>
          </div>
          <span className="rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-black/20 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-zinc-300">
            {TEMPLATES.length}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          {TEMPLATES.map((template) => {
            const active = template.id === selectedTemplate.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={template.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => selectTemplate(template)}
                className={`rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left transition ${
                  active
                    ? "border-purple-200/35 bg-purple-200/14 shadow-[0_16px_34px_rgba(88,28,135,0.20)]"
                    : "border-white/10 bg-black/15 hover:bg-white/[0.075]"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-white">{template.title}</p>
                  <span
                    className={`shrink-0 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] ${statusTone(
                      template.status,
                    )}`}
                  >
                    {statusLabel(template.status)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-300/78">{template.subtitle}</p>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="grid gap-5">
        <section className="rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.055] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="max-w-3xl">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-purple-100/70">
                Active template
              </p>
              <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-white">
                {selectedTemplate.title}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-zinc-200/78">
                {selectedTemplate.useWhen}
              </p>
            </div>

            <a
              href="/sandbox/probe-lab/generated-video"
              className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.07] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 transition hover:bg-white/[0.11]"
            >
              Open generated-video lab
            </a>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-black/[0.22] p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <ProbeRenderer
            key={selectedTemplate.id}
            probe={selectedTemplate.probe}
            initialDraft={latestDraft}
            showDebug
            onDraftChange={setLatestDraft}
            onSubmit={setLastSubmit}
          />
        </section>

        <section className="grid gap-4 rounded-[2rem] border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.045] p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl xl:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-zinc-300/70">
              Current draft
            </p>
            <pre className="mt-3 max-h-80 overflow-auto rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 p-4 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-200">
              {JSON.stringify(latestDraft, null, 2)}
            </pre>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-zinc-300/70">
              Last submit payload
            </p>
            <pre className="mt-3 max-h-80 overflow-auto rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 p-4 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-200">
              {lastSubmit ? JSON.stringify(lastSubmit.attempt, null, 2) : "Submit from a renderer to inspect the attempt payload."}
            </pre>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

# MyWay sandbox

This folder holds experiments, labs, and prototype routes that are not part of the active app runtime.

The goal is to keep production runtime code focused while preserving useful experiments for reference.

Current direction:

- Model outputs structured visual plans.
- MyWay validates and compiles those plans into supported primitives, actions, and interactions.
- Trusted renderers execute the compiled scene.

Useful sandbox code can later be promoted into `lib/visual-story`, `lib/visual-compiler`, or active UI renderers after it is generalized.

export type GeneratedVideoFormat = "16:9_laptop";

export type GeneratedVideoTemplateId = "saddle_surface_explanation_v1";

export type GeneratedVideoBridgeLevel =
  | "bridge_0"
  | "bridge_1"
  | "bridge_2"
  | "full_bridge";

export type GeneratedVideoSceneKind =
  | "hook"
  | "x_direction"
  | "y_direction"
  | "saddle_combine"
  | "real_world_bridge"
  | "checkpoint";

export type GeneratedVideoScene = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  caption: string;
  durationSeconds: number;
  visualKind: GeneratedVideoSceneKind;
};

export type GeneratedVideoCheckpoint = {
  prompt: string;
  expected_idea: string;
};

export type GeneratedVideoContract = {
  schema_version: "generated_video_contract_v1";
  contract_id: string;
  title: string;
  template: GeneratedVideoTemplateId;
  format: GeneratedVideoFormat;
  bridge_level: GeneratedVideoBridgeLevel;
  learner_message: string;
  diagnosis: string;
  learning_goal: string;
  real_world_bridge?: string | null;
  scenes: GeneratedVideoScene[];
  checkpoint: GeneratedVideoCheckpoint;
};

export type RendererParamsWithGeneratedVideo = {
  generated_animation?: GeneratedVideoContract | null;
};

export function getGeneratedVideoContractFromRendererParams(
  rendererParams: unknown,
): GeneratedVideoContract | null {
  const candidate = rendererParams as RendererParamsWithGeneratedVideo | null | undefined;
  return candidate?.generated_animation ?? null;
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ProbeShell } from "../../probe-shell";
import type { GenericProbeComponentProps, ProbeAnswerDraft } from "../../probe-ui-types";
import { ProbeButton, ProbePill, ProbeSection, ProbeTextArea } from "../../shared";
import {
  getGeneratedVideoContractFromRendererParams,
  SADDLE_SURFACE_ANIMATION_CONTRACT,
  type GeneratedVideoContract,
} from "./animation-contract";
import { SaddleSurfaceAnimationScene } from "./templates/saddle-surface-animation";

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function findSceneAtTime(contract: GeneratedVideoContract, elapsedSeconds: number) {
  let cursor = 0;

  for (let index = 0; index < contract.scenes.length; index += 1) {
    const scene = contract.scenes[index]!;
    const start = cursor;
    const end = cursor + scene.durationSeconds;

    if (elapsedSeconds <= end || index === contract.scenes.length - 1) {
      return {
        scene,
        sceneIndex: index,
        sceneStart: start,
        sceneEnd: end,
        sceneProgress: clamp((elapsedSeconds - start) / scene.durationSeconds, 0, 1),
      };
    }

    cursor = end;
  }

  const fallbackScene = contract.scenes[0] ?? SADDLE_SURFACE_ANIMATION_CONTRACT.scenes[0]!;
  return {
    scene: fallbackScene,
    sceneIndex: 0,
    sceneStart: 0,
    sceneEnd: fallbackScene.durationSeconds,
    sceneProgress: 0,
  };
}

function formatTime(seconds: number) {
  const safeSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds));
  const minutes = Math.floor(safeSeconds / 60);
  const remaining = safeSeconds % 60;
  return `${minutes}:${String(remaining).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function buildDraft(args: {
  baseDraft?: ProbeAnswerDraft;
  contract: GeneratedVideoContract;
  elapsedSeconds: number;
  totalDuration: number;
  checkpointResponse: string;
  completed?: boolean;
}): ProbeAnswerDraft {
  const completed = args.completed ?? args.elapsedSeconds >= args.totalDuration;
  return {
    ...(args.baseDraft ?? { attempt_type: "none" }),
    attempt_type: "none",
    text_response: args.checkpointResponse,
    generated_video_contract_id: args.contract.contract_id,
    generated_video_completed: completed,
    generated_video_elapsed_seconds: Number(args.elapsedSeconds.toFixed(2)),
    generated_video_checkpoint_response: args.checkpointResponse,
  };
}

export function hasGeneratedAnimationContract(probe: { renderer_params?: unknown }) {
  return Boolean(getGeneratedVideoContractFromRendererParams(probe.renderer_params));
}

export function GeneratedVideoPlayer({
  contract = SADDLE_SURFACE_ANIMATION_CONTRACT,
  disabled,
  checkpointResponse,
  onCheckpointResponseChange,
  onPlaybackDraftChange,
  showMetadata = true,
}: {
  contract?: GeneratedVideoContract;
  disabled?: boolean;
  checkpointResponse?: string;
  onCheckpointResponseChange?: (value: string) => void;
  onPlaybackDraftChange?: (args: { elapsedSeconds: number; totalDuration: number; completed: boolean }) => void;
  showMetadata?: boolean;
}) {
  const [elapsedSeconds, setElapsedSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
  const playbackCallbackRef = useRef(onPlaybackDraftChange);
  const totalDuration = useMemo(
    () => contract.scenes.reduce((sum, scene) => sum + scene.durationSeconds, 0),
    [contract.scenes],
  );
  const active = findSceneAtTime(contract, elapsedSeconds);

  useEffect(() => {
    playbackCallbackRef.current = onPlaybackDraftChange;
  }, [onPlaybackDraftChange]);

  useEffect(() => {
    playbackCallbackRef.current?.({
      elapsedSeconds,
      totalDuration,
      completed: elapsedSeconds >= totalDuration,
    });
  }, [elapsedSeconds, totalDuration]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying || disabled) return;

    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
      setElapsedSeconds((value) => {
        const next = value + 0.05;
        if (next >= totalDuration) {
          window.clearInterval(interval);
          setIsPlaying(false);
          return totalDuration;
        }
        return next;
      });
    }, 50);

    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
  }, [disabled, isPlaying, totalDuration]);

  function jumpToScene(sceneIndex: number) {
    const nextStart = contract.scenes
      .slice(0, sceneIndex)
      .reduce((sum, scene) => sum + scene.durationSeconds, 0);
    setElapsedSeconds(nextStart);
    setIsPlaying(false);
  }

  function togglePlayback() {
    if (disabled) return;

    if (elapsedSeconds >= totalDuration) {
      setElapsedSeconds(0);
      setIsPlaying(true);
      return;
    }

    setIsPlaying((value) => !value);
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: "1rem" }}>
      <ProbeSection
        tone="deep"
        title={contract.title}
        subtitle="Laptop-format generated explanation preview. This is a structured animation contract rendered inside MyWay."
        badge={<ProbePill tone="purple" active>{contract.format.replaceAll("_", " ")}</ProbePill>}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            position: "relative",
            aspectRatio: "16 / 9",
            overflow: "hidden",
            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
            borderRadius: "24px",
            background:
              "linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(5,5,16,0.98), rgba(39,13,64,0.82))",
            boxShadow: "0 26px 80px rgba(0,0,0,0.32)",
          }}
        >
          <SaddleSurfaceAnimationScene scene={active.scene} progress={active.sceneProgress} />

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              left: "1rem",
              right: "1rem",
              bottom: "1rem",
              display: "grid",
              gap: "0.5rem",
              border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
              borderRadius: "20px",
              background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.48)",
              padding: "0.85rem 1rem",
              backdropFilter: "blur(14px)",
            }}
          >
            <p
              style={{
                margin: 0,
                color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)",
                fontSize: "0.78rem",
                fontWeight: 900,
                letterSpacing: "0.12em",
                textTransform: "uppercase",
              }}
            >
              {active.scene.title}
            </p>
            <p
              style={{
                margin: 0,
                color: "white",
                fontSize: "clamp(0.96rem, 1.7vw, 1.18rem)",
                lineHeight: 1.35,
                fontWeight: 850,
              }}
            >
              {active.scene.caption}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: "0.7rem", marginTop: "0.9rem" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "0.8rem" }}>
            <ProbeButton disabled={disabled} variant="primary" onClick={togglePlayback} style={{ minWidth: "6.6rem" }}>
              {isPlaying ? "Pause" : elapsedSeconds >= totalDuration ? "Replay" : "Play"}
            </ProbeButton>

            <input
              aria-label="Generated animation timeline"
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={totalDuration}
              step={0.05}
              value={elapsedSeconds}
              disabled={disabled}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setElapsedSeconds(Number(event.target.value));
                setIsPlaying(false);
              }}
              style={{ width: "100%", accentColor: "#c4b5fd" }}
            />

            <span
              style={{
                color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)",
                fontSize: "0.8rem",
                fontWeight: 850,
                whiteSpace: "nowrap",
              }}
            >
              {formatTime(elapsedSeconds)} / {formatTime(totalDuration)}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: "0.42rem" }}>
            {contract.scenes.map((scene, index) => (
              <button
                key={scene.id}
                type="button"
                disabled={disabled}
                onClick={() => jumpToScene(index)}
                style={{
                  border:
                    index === active.sceneIndex
                      ? "1px solid rgba(221,214,254,0.36)"
                      : "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)",
                  borderRadius: "999px",
                  background:
                    index === active.sceneIndex
                      ? "rgba(221,214,254,0.16)"
                      : "rgba(255,255,255,0.055)",
                  color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)",
                  padding: "0.36rem 0.58rem",
                  fontSize: "0.72rem",
                  fontWeight: 800,
                  cursor: disabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
                  opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,
                }}
              >
                {index + 1}. {scene.title}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </ProbeSection>

      {showMetadata ? (
        <section
          style={{
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fit, minmax(18rem, 1fr))",
            gap: "0.9rem",
          }}
        >
          <ProbeSection title="Learner signal" subtitle={`“${contract.learner_message}”`}>
            {null}
          </ProbeSection>
          <ProbeSection title="MyWay diagnosis" subtitle={contract.diagnosis}>
            {null}
          </ProbeSection>
          <ProbeSection title="Learning goal" subtitle={contract.learning_goal}>
            {null}
          </ProbeSection>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      <ProbeSection
        title="Quick checkpoint"
        subtitle={contract.checkpoint.prompt}
        badge={<ProbePill tone={checkpointResponse?.trim() ? "success" : "default"}>{checkpointResponse?.trim() ? "answered" : "optional"}</ProbePill>}
      >
        <ProbeTextArea
          value={checkpointResponse ?? ""}
          disabled={disabled}
          rows={3}
          placeholder="Type what changed in your understanding..."
          onChange={(value) => onCheckpointResponseChange?.(value)}
          ariaLabel="Generated video checkpoint response"
        />
      </ProbeSection>
    </div>
  );
}

export function GeneratedVideoProbe(props: GenericProbeComponentProps) {
  const contract =
    getGeneratedVideoContractFromRendererParams(props.probe.renderer_params) ??
    SADDLE_SURFACE_ANIMATION_CONTRACT;
  const checkpointResponse = props.draft.generated_video_checkpoint_response ?? props.draft.text_response ?? "";
  const [playbackState, setPlaybackState] = useState({
    elapsedSeconds: props.draft.generated_video_elapsed_seconds ?? 0,
    totalDuration: contract.scenes.reduce((sum, scene) => sum + scene.durationSeconds, 0),
    completed: props.draft.generated_video_completed ?? false,
  });

  function updateGeneratedVideoDraft(next: {
    checkpointResponse?: string;
    elapsedSeconds?: number;
    totalDuration?: number;
    completed?: boolean;
  }) {
    const nextPlayback = {
      elapsedSeconds: next.elapsedSeconds ?? playbackState.elapsedSeconds,
      totalDuration: next.totalDuration ?? playbackState.totalDuration,
      completed: next.completed ?? playbackState.completed,
    };
    setPlaybackState(nextPlayback);

    props.onDraftChange(
      buildDraft({
        baseDraft: props.draft,
        contract,
        elapsedSeconds: nextPlayback.elapsedSeconds,
        totalDuration: nextPlayback.totalDuration,
        completed: nextPlayback.completed,
        checkpointResponse: next.checkpointResponse ?? checkpointResponse,
      }),
    );
  }

  return (
    <ProbeShell {...props}>
      <GeneratedVideoPlayer
        contract={contract}
        disabled={props.disabled}
        checkpointResponse={checkpointResponse}
        onCheckpointResponseChange={(value) => updateGeneratedVideoDraft({ checkpointResponse: value })}
        onPlaybackDraftChange={(state) => updateGeneratedVideoDraft(state)}
        showMetadata
      />
    </ProbeShell>
  );
}

export function GeneratedVideoProbeLabPreview() {
  return <GeneratedVideoPlayer contract={SADDLE_SURFACE_ANIMATION_CONTRACT} showMetadata />;
}

"use client";

import type { GeneratedVideoScene } from "../animation-contract";

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function easeOutCubic(value: number) {
  const t = clamp(value, 0, 1);
  return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
}

function easeInOutSine(value: number) {
  const t = clamp(value, 0, 1);
  return -(Math.cos(Math.PI * t) - 1) / 2;
}

function buildCurvePath(args: {
  kind: "up" | "down";
  progress: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
}) {
  const width = args.width ?? 720;
  const height = args.height ?? 310;
  const points: string[] = [];
  const centerY = height / 2 + 16;
  const amount = easeOutCubic(args.progress) * 105;

  for (let index = 0; index <= 80; index += 1) {
    const t = -1 + (index / 80) * 2;
    const x = 54 + ((t + 1) / 2) * (width - 108);
    const bend = t * t * amount;
    const y = args.kind === "up" ? centerY - bend : centerY + bend;
    points.push(`${x.toFixed(2)},${y.toFixed(2)}`);
  }

  return points.join(" ");
}

function projectPoint(x: number, y: number, z: number) {
  const scale = 82;
  const screenX = 360 + (x - y) * scale;
  const screenY = 240 + (x + y) * scale * 0.35 - z * 58;
  return [screenX, screenY] as const;
}

function buildSurfaceLines(progress: number) {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  const bend = easeInOutSine(progress) * 0.55;
  const gridValues = [-2, -1.5, -1, -0.5, 0, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2];
  const samples = 34;

  function zFor(x: number, y: number) {
    return bend * (x * x - y * y);
  }

  for (const fixedY of gridValues) {
    const points: string[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index <= samples; index += 1) {
      const x = -2 + (index / samples) * 4;
      const [px, py] = projectPoint(x, fixedY, zFor(x, fixedY));
      points.push(`${px.toFixed(2)},${py.toFixed(2)}`);
    }
    lines.push(points.join(" "));
  }

  for (const fixedX of gridValues) {
    const points: string[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index <= samples; index += 1) {
      const y = -2 + (index / samples) * 4;
      const [px, py] = projectPoint(fixedX, y, zFor(fixedX, y));
      points.push(`${px.toFixed(2)},${py.toFixed(2)}`);
    }
    lines.push(points.join(" "));
  }

  return lines;
}

export function SaddleSurfaceAnimationScene({
  scene,
  progress,
}: {
  scene: GeneratedVideoScene;
  progress: number;
}) {
  const eased = easeOutCubic(progress);
  const pulse = 0.5 + Math.sin(progress * Math.PI * 2) * 0.5;

  if (scene.visualKind === "hook") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 720 360" style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}>
        <defs>
          <radialGradient id="mywayGeneratedHookGlow" cx="50%" cy="45%" r="65%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="rgba(216,180,254,0.42)" />
            <stop offset="58%" stopColor="rgba(88,28,135,0.14)" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="rgba(0,0,0,0)" />
          </radialGradient>
        </defs>
        <rect width="720" height="360" fill="url(#mywayGeneratedHookGlow)" />
        <circle cx="220" cy="180" r={60 + eased * 28} fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" stroke="rgba(221,214,254,0.4)" />
        <circle cx="500" cy="180" r={60 + (1 - eased) * 22} fill="rgba(125,211,252,0.08)" stroke="rgba(125,211,252,0.35)" />
        <path d="M280 180 C340 110 380 250 440 180" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.72)" strokeWidth="6" strokeLinecap="round" />
        <text x="220" y="188" textAnchor="middle" fill="white" fontSize="24" fontWeight="800">one curve?</text>
        <text x="500" y="188" textAnchor="middle" fill="white" fontSize="24" fontWeight="800">two directions</text>
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (scene.visualKind === "x_direction" || scene.visualKind === "y_direction") {
    const kind = scene.visualKind === "x_direction" ? "up" : "down";
    const axisLabel = scene.visualKind === "x_direction" ? "x direction" : "y direction";
    const equation = scene.visualKind === "x_direction" ? "+ x² bends upward" : "− y² flips downward";

    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 720 360" style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}>
        <rect width="720" height="360" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.02)" />
        {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((line) => (
          <line
            key={line}
            x1="54"
            x2="666"
            y1={70 + line * 38}
            y2={70 + line * 38}
            stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.06)"
          />
        ))}
        <line x1="54" x2="666" y1="196" y2="196" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.18)" strokeWidth="2" />
        <polyline
          points={buildCurvePath({ kind, progress })}
          fill="none"
          stroke={scene.visualKind === "x_direction" ? "rgba(221,214,254,0.98)" : "rgba(125,211,252,0.95)"}
          strokeWidth="8"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
        />
        <circle
          cx={360 + Math.sin(progress * Math.PI * 2) * 205}
          cy={scene.visualKind === "x_direction" ? 196 - eased * 54 : 196 + eased * 54}
          r="12"
          fill="white"
          opacity={0.72 + pulse * 0.22}
        />
        <text x="78" y="52" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.72)" fontSize="18" fontWeight="800">{axisLabel}</text>
        <text x="78" y="318" fill="white" fontSize="28" fontWeight="900">{equation}</text>
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (scene.visualKind === "saddle_combine") {
    const lines = buildSurfaceLines(progress);
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 720 360" style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}>
        <defs>
          <radialGradient id="mywayGeneratedSurfaceGlow" cx="50%" cy="45%" r="65%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="rgba(168,85,247,0.24)" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="rgba(0,0,0,0)" />
          </radialGradient>
        </defs>
        <rect width="720" height="360" fill="url(#mywayGeneratedSurfaceGlow)" />
        {lines.map((points, index) => (
          <polyline
            key={index}
            points={points}
            fill="none"
            stroke={index < 9 ? "rgba(221,214,254,0.62)" : "rgba(125,211,252,0.52)"}
            strokeWidth={index === 4 || index === 13 ? 3.2 : 1.5}
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        ))}
        <text x="60" y="54" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.78)" fontSize="18" fontWeight="800">z = x² − y²</text>
        <text x="60" y="318" fill="white" fontSize="25" fontWeight="900">up one way + down the other = saddle</text>
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (scene.visualKind === "real_world_bridge") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 720 360" style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}>
        <path d="M0 300 C130 210 240 255 360 172 C480 92 575 145 720 70 L720 360 L0 360 Z" fill="rgba(76,29,149,0.5)" />
        <path d="M0 294 C125 222 238 246 360 180 C480 114 575 145 720 86" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.32)" strokeWidth="8" />
        <path d="M180 285 C255 205 470 202 540 115" fill="none" stroke="rgba(125,211,252,0.95)" strokeWidth="10" strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={`${eased * 520} 520`} />
        <circle cx={180 + eased * 360} cy={285 - eased * 170 + Math.sin(eased * Math.PI) * -28} r="13" fill="white" />
        <text x="56" y="60" fill="white" fontSize="28" fontWeight="900">A mountain pass has the same idea.</text>
        <text x="56" y="96" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.72)" fontSize="18" fontWeight="700">One path feels like it rises. The crossing path can curve the other way.</text>
      </svg>
    );
  }

  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 720 360" style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}>
      <rect width="720" height="360" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.02)" />
      <circle cx="360" cy="150" r={72 + pulse * 10} fill="rgba(221,214,254,0.1)" stroke="rgba(221,214,254,0.36)" />
      <text x="360" y="141" textAnchor="middle" fill="white" fontSize="27" fontWeight="900">Quick check</text>
      <text x="360" y="179" textAnchor="middle" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.74)" fontSize="18" fontWeight="700">What does the minus sign do?</text>
      <path d="M210 275 C290 210 430 210 510 275" fill="none" stroke="rgba(125,211,252,0.8)" strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" />
      <path d="M210 275 C290 322 430 322 510 275" fill="none" stroke="rgba(221,214,254,0.8)" strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" />
    </svg>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent, type WheelEvent as ReactWheelEvent } from "react";
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree, type ThreeEvent } from "@react-three/fiber";
import * as THREE from "three";
import type { GenericProbeComponentProps } from "../probe-ui-types";

import {
  hasVisualStoryContract,
  normalizeVisualStoryScene,
  type BeatProgress,
  type VisualStoryBeat,
  type VisualStoryEntity,
  type VisualStoryScene,
  type VisualStorySupportingExample,
} from "@/lib/sandbox/visual-story";

export { hasVisualStoryContract };
function clamp(value: number, min = 0, max = 1) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}
const FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT =
  "This visual story turns the idea into a scene you can watch, explore, and connect back to the question.";

function easeInOut(value: number) {
  const t = clamp(value);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

type DragState = {
  mode: "rotate" | "phase";
  x: number;
  y: number;
  yaw: number;
  pitch: number;
  phase: number;
};

function totalDuration(beats: VisualStoryBeat[]) {
  return beats.reduce((sum, beat) => sum + beat.duration_ms, 0);
}

function beatInfo(beats: VisualStoryBeat[], playheadMs: number): BeatProgress {
  let cursor = 0;

  for (let index = 0; index < beats.length; index += 1) {
    const beat = beats[index];
    const nextCursor = cursor + beat.duration_ms;

    if (playheadMs <= nextCursor || index === beats.length - 1) {
      const localProgress = clamp((playheadMs - cursor) / Math.max(1, beat.duration_ms));
      return { beat, index, localProgress };
    }

    cursor = nextCursor;
  }

  const first = beats[0] ?? {
    id: "beat_1",
    script_segment: FALLBACK_ORIENTATION_SCRIPT,
    duration_ms: 5000,
    active_entity_ids: [],
    actions: [],
  };
  return { beat: first, index: 0, localProgress: 1 };
}

function revealScriptSegment(text: string, progress: number, enabled: boolean) {
  if (!enabled) return text;
  const words = text.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length <= 5) return text;
  const visibleCount = Math.max(3, Math.ceil(words.length * clamp((progress - 0.04) / 0.62)));
  return words.slice(0, visibleCount).join(" ");
}

function storyLooksMotionBased(scene: VisualStoryScene) {
  const lower = scene.orientation_script.toLowerCase();
  return (
    scene.scene_family === "motion_to_graph" ||
    /sin|cos|sine|cosine|π|pi|wheel|circle|turn|wave|cycle|graph|sound|engine|tide|season/.test(lower) ||
    scene.entities.some((entity) => ["wheel", "dot", "graph_2d", "wave", "circle_arc", "machine"].includes(entity.kind))
  );
}

function LinePath({ points, color, opacity = 1 }: { points: THREE.Vector3[]; color: string; opacity?: number }) {
  const geometry = useMemo(() => new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points), [points]);
  const material = useMemo(
    () => new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color, transparent: true, opacity }),
    [color, opacity],
  );
  const line = useMemo(() => new THREE.Line(geometry, material), [geometry, material]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      geometry.dispose();
      material.dispose();
    };
  }, [geometry, material]);

  return <primitive object={line} />;
}

function CameraRig({ scene, progress, interactive, zoom }: { scene: VisualStoryScene; progress: number; interactive: boolean; zoom: number }) {
  const { camera } = useThree();

  useFrame(() => {
    const motion = storyLooksMotionBased(scene);
    const t = easeInOut(progress);
    const baseDistance = interactive ? zoom : motion ? 7.4 - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 1.0 : 7.0 - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 0.7;
    const yaw = interactive ? -0.42 : motion ? -0.62 + t * 0.45 : -0.36 + t * 0.24;
    const height = interactive ? 3.0 : motion ? 3.08 - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 0.42 : 3.1;
    const next = new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(yaw) * baseDistance, height, Math.cos(yaw) * baseDistance);

    camera.position.lerp(next, interactive ? 0.075 : 0.045);
    camera.lookAt(0.12, 0.08, 0);
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  });

  return null;
}

function WaveTrace({ phase, storyProgress, interactive, onCompleteAction }: {
  phase: number;
  storyProgress: number;
  interactive: boolean;
  onCompleteAction: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const baseX = 0.28;
  const baseY = -0.78;
  const width = 4.36;
  const amplitude = 0.82;
  const visible = interactive ? 1 : clamp((storyProgress - 0.30) / 0.42);
  const highlight = interactive ? 1 : clamp((storyProgress - 0.67) / 0.22);
  const liveX = baseX + phase * width;
  const liveY = baseY + Math.sin(phase * Math.PI * 2) * amplitude;

  const fullWave = useMemo(() => {
    const points: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= 190; i += 1) {
      const t = i / 190;
      points.push(new THREE.Vector3(baseX + t * width, baseY + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * amplitude, 0));
    }
    return points;
  }, []);

  const visibleWave = useMemo(() => {
    const samples = Math.max(2, Math.floor(190 * visible));
    const points: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= samples; i += 1) {
      const t = i / 190;
      points.push(new THREE.Vector3(baseX + t * width, baseY + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * amplitude, 0.05));
    }
    return points;
  }, [visible]);

  return (
    <group>
      <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(baseX - 0.18, baseY, 0), new THREE.Vector3(baseX + width + 0.24, baseY, 0)]} color="#ffffff" opacity={0.22} />
      <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(baseX, baseY - 1.05, 0), new THREE.Vector3(baseX, baseY + 1.05, 0)]} color="#ffffff" opacity={0.16} />
      <LinePath points={fullWave} color="#a78bfa" opacity={0.18} />
      <LinePath points={visibleWave} color="#fef3c7" opacity={0.98} />
      {highlight > 0 ? (
        <group>
          <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(baseX, baseY - 1.13, 0.08), new THREE.Vector3(baseX + width, baseY - 1.13, 0.08)]} color="#fbbf24" opacity={0.24 + highlight * 0.55} />
          <mesh position={[baseX + width * 0.25, baseY + amplitude, 0.18]} onClick={(event: ThreeEvent<MouseEvent>) => { event.stopPropagation(); onCompleteAction("tap_peak"); }}>
            <sphereGeometry args={[0.09 + highlight * 0.05, 32, 32]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" emissive="#f59e0b" emissiveIntensity={1.2} roughness={0.2} />
          </mesh>
        </group>
      ) : null}
      {interactive ? (
        <group>
          <mesh position={[baseX + width / 2, baseY - 1.13, 0.2]} onClick={(event: ThreeEvent<MouseEvent>) => { event.stopPropagation(); onCompleteAction("mark_one_repeat"); }}>
            <boxGeometry args={[width, 0.24, 0.08]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#fbbf24" transparent opacity={0.28} emissive="#a16207" emissiveIntensity={0.42} />
          </mesh>
          <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(liveX, baseY - 1.06, 0.16), new THREE.Vector3(liveX, liveY, 0.16)]} color="#7dd3fc" opacity={0.62} />
          <mesh position={[liveX, liveY, 0.24]}>
            <sphereGeometry args={[0.075, 24, 24]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#7dd3fc" emissive="#0284c7" emissiveIntensity={0.9} roughness={0.22} />
          </mesh>
        </group>
      ) : null}
    </group>
  );
}

function ExampleEchoIcon({ example, index, visible }: { example: VisualStorySupportingExample; index: number; visible: number }) {
  const x = 0.9 + (index % 3) * 1.16;
  const y = 1.28 - Math.floor(index / 3) * 0.78;
  const opacity = clamp(visible - index * 0.08);
  if (opacity <= 0.02) return null;

  const type = example.example_type.toLowerCase();

  if (type.includes("engine")) {
    return (
      <group position={[x, y, 0.15]} scale={[opacity, opacity, opacity]}>
        <mesh position={[-0.18, 0, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.35, 0.28, 0.08]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#c4b5fd" emissive="#581c87" emissiveIntensity={0.55} transparent opacity={0.72} />
        </mesh>
        <mesh position={[0.18, 0, 0]}>
          <sphereGeometry args={[0.14, 24, 24]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#fef3c7" emissive="#f59e0b" emissiveIntensity={0.9} transparent opacity={0.82} />
        </mesh>
        <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(-0.01, 0, 0.05), new THREE.Vector3(0.1, 0, 0.05)]} color="#fef3c7" opacity={0.78} />
      </group>
    );
  }

  if (type.includes("tide")) {
    return (
      <group position={[x, y, 0.15]} scale={[opacity, opacity, opacity]}>
        <mesh position={[0, -0.12, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.72, 0.14, 0.06]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" emissive="#0369a1" emissiveIntensity={0.55} transparent opacity={0.55} />
        </mesh>
        <LinePath points={[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((i) => new THREE.Vector3(-0.34 + i * 0.136, 0.02 + Math.sin(i) * 0.05, 0.05))} color="#7dd3fc" opacity={0.82} />
      </group>
    );
  }

  if (type.includes("season")) {
    return (
      <group position={[x, y, 0.15]} scale={[opacity, opacity, opacity]}>
        <mesh>
          <torusGeometry args={[0.28, 0.018, 16, 48]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#86efac" emissive="#15803d" emissiveIntensity={0.62} transparent opacity={0.72} />
        </mesh>
        {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((step) => {
          const a = step * Math.PI / 2;
          return (
            <mesh key={step} position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.28, Math.sin(a) * 0.28, 0.04]}>
              <sphereGeometry args={[0.045, 16, 16]} />
              <meshStandardMaterial color="#fef3c7" emissive="#f59e0b" emissiveIntensity={0.65} />
            </mesh>
          );
        })}
      </group>
    );
  }

  return (
    <group position={[x, y, 0.15]} scale={[opacity, opacity, opacity]}>
      <LinePath points={[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7].map((i) => {
        const t = i / 7;
        return new THREE.Vector3(-0.36 + t * 0.72, Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * 0.18, 0.05);
      })} color={type.includes("sound") || type.includes("signal") ? "#7dd3fc" : "#fef3c7"} opacity={0.82} />
      <mesh position={[-0.42, 0, 0]}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.045, 16, 16]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#c4b5fd" emissive="#7e22ce" emissiveIntensity={0.7} />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  );
}

function ExampleEchoes({ examples, storyProgress }: { examples: VisualStorySupportingExample[]; storyProgress: number }) {
  if (!examples.length) return null;
  const visible = clamp((storyProgress - 0.70) / 0.24);

  return (
    <group>
      {examples.slice(0, 6).map((example, index) => (
        <ExampleEchoIcon key={example.id} example={example} index={index} visible={visible} />
      ))}
      {visible > 0.1 ? (
        <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(0.45, -0.1, 0.03), new THREE.Vector3(0.95, 1.04, 0.03)]} color="#7dd3fc" opacity={0.16 + visible * 0.22} />
      ) : null}
    </group>
  );
}

function MotionToGraphLayer({ storyProgress, phase, interactive, scene, onCompleteAction, onDotPointerDown }: {
  storyProgress: number;
  phase: number;
  interactive: boolean;
  scene: VisualStoryScene;
  onCompleteAction: (id: string) => void;
  onDotPointerDown: (event: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent>) => void;
}) {
  const wheelCenter = new THREE.Vector3(-2.55, 0.05, 0);
  const radius = 0.92;
  const progressPhase = interactive ? phase : clamp(storyProgress * 1.08);
  const angle = progressPhase * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
  const dot = new THREE.Vector3(
    wheelCenter.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius,
    wheelCenter.y + Math.sin(angle) * radius,
    0.18,
  );
  const waveProgress = clamp((storyProgress - 0.30) / 0.44);
  const liveWaveX = 0.28 + (interactive ? phase : waveProgress) * 4.36;
  const liveWaveY = -0.78 + Math.sin((interactive ? phase : waveProgress) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.82;
  const arcVisibility = clamp((storyProgress - 0.18) / 0.3);
  const projectionVisibility = interactive ? 1 : clamp((storyProgress - 0.22) / 0.22);

  const arcPoints = useMemo(() => {
    const points: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    const visibleSamples = Math.max(3, Math.floor(100 * (interactive ? 1 : arcVisibility)));
    for (let i = 0; i <= visibleSamples; i += 1) {
      const t = i / 100;
      const a = -Math.PI / 2 + t * Math.PI * 2;
      points.push(new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x + Math.cos(a) * 1.12, wheelCenter.y + Math.sin(a) * 1.12, 0.11));
    }
    return points;
  }, [arcVisibility, interactive]);

  return (
    <group>
      <mesh position={wheelCenter} rotation={[0, 0, angle]}>
        <torusGeometry args={[radius, 0.045, 24, 120]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#a78bfa" emissive="#581c87" emissiveIntensity={0.46} roughness={0.32} />
      </mesh>
      <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x - radius, wheelCenter.y, 0.05), new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x + radius, wheelCenter.y, 0.05)]} color="#ddd6fe" opacity={0.24} />
      <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x, wheelCenter.y - radius, 0.05), new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x, wheelCenter.y + radius, 0.05)]} color="#ddd6fe" opacity={0.18} />
      <LinePath points={arcPoints} color="#fbbf24" opacity={0.72} />
      <mesh position={dot} onPointerDown={(event: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent>) => { event.stopPropagation(); onDotPointerDown(event); }}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[interactive ? 0.125 : 0.105, 32, 32]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" emissive="#f59e0b" emissiveIntensity={1.65} roughness={0.2} />
      </mesh>
      <LinePath points={[dot, new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x + 1.24, dot.y, 0.05)]} color="#fef3c7" opacity={0.16 + projectionVisibility * 0.58} />
      <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x + 1.24, wheelCenter.y - radius, 0.02), new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x + 1.24, wheelCenter.y + radius, 0.02)]} color="#fef3c7" opacity={0.1 + projectionVisibility * 0.32} />
      <LinePath points={[new THREE.Vector3(wheelCenter.x + 1.24, dot.y, 0.06), new THREE.Vector3(liveWaveX, liveWaveY, 0.08)]} color="#7dd3fc" opacity={0.16 + projectionVisibility * 0.36} />
      <WaveTrace phase={phase} storyProgress={storyProgress} interactive={interactive} onCompleteAction={onCompleteAction} />
      <ExampleEchoes examples={scene.supporting_examples} storyProgress={interactive ? 1 : storyProgress} />
    </group>
  );
}

function materialForEntity(entity: VisualStoryEntity, focused: boolean, progress: number) {
  const emphasis = entity.visual_style?.emphasis;
  const color = emphasis === "high" || focused
    ? "#fef3c7"
    : emphasis === "low"
      ? "#7dd3fc"
      : "#c4b5fd";

  return {
    color,
    emissive: focused ? "#f59e0b" : "#312e81",
    emissiveIntensity: focused ? 0.72 : 0.26,
    opacity: clamp(0.24 + progress * (focused ? 0.76 : 0.54), 0.18, 1),
  };
}

function GenericSceneLayer({ scene, activeBeat, storyProgress, interactive, onCompleteAction }: {
  scene: VisualStoryScene;
  activeBeat: VisualStoryBeat;
  storyProgress: number;
  interactive: boolean;
  onCompleteAction: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const entities = scene.entities.slice(0, 16);

  function positionFor(index: number): [number, number, number] {
    const columns = 4;
    const col = index % columns;
    const row = Math.floor(index / columns);
    return [-2.8 + col * 1.85, 0.88 - row * 1.1, 0];
  }

  return (
    <group>
      {entities.slice(0, -1).map((entity, index) => {
        const from = positionFor(index);
        const to = positionFor(index + 1);
        return <LinePath key={`line-${entity.id}`} points={[new THREE.Vector3(...from), new THREE.Vector3(...to)]} color="#7dd3fc" opacity={0.14 + storyProgress * 0.32} />;
      })}
      {entities.map((entity, index) => {
        const position = positionFor(index);
        const focused = activeBeat.active_entity_ids?.includes(entity.id) ?? false;
        const material = materialForEntity(entity, focused, storyProgress);
        const scale = focused ? 1.08 + Math.sin(storyProgress * Math.PI * 8) * 0.04 : 1;
        return (
          <mesh
            key={entity.id}
            position={position}
            scale={[scale, scale, scale]}
            onClick={(event: ThreeEvent<MouseEvent>) => {
              if (!interactive) return;
              event.stopPropagation();
              onCompleteAction(`selected_${entity.id}`);
            }}
          >
            {entity.kind === "container" || entity.kind === "surface" ? <boxGeometry args={[0.78, 0.52, 0.08]} /> : <sphereGeometry args={[0.3, 32, 32]} />}
            <meshStandardMaterial color={material.color} emissive={material.emissive} emissiveIntensity={material.emissiveIntensity} transparent opacity={material.opacity} roughness={0.24} />
          </mesh>
        );
      })}
    </group>
  );
}

function VisualStoryWorld({ scene, playheadMs, totalMs, active, phase, stageYaw, stagePitch, zoom, interactive, onCompleteAction, onDotPointerDown }: {
  scene: VisualStoryScene;
  playheadMs: number;
  totalMs: number;
  active: BeatProgress;
  phase: number;
  stageYaw: number;
  stagePitch: number;
  zoom: number;
  interactive: boolean;
  onCompleteAction: (id: string) => void;
  onDotPointerDown: (event: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent>) => void;
}) {
  const storyProgress = interactive ? phase : clamp(playheadMs / Math.max(1, totalMs));
  const motionBased = storyLooksMotionBased(scene);

  return (
    <>
      <CameraRig scene={scene} progress={storyProgress} interactive={interactive} zoom={zoom} />
      <color attach="background" args={["#070014"]} />
      <ambientLight intensity={0.74} />
      <directionalLight position={[4, 7, 6]} intensity={1.32} />
      <pointLight position={[-4, 2, 4]} intensity={0.58} color="#c4b5fd" />
      <group rotation={[stagePitch, stageYaw, 0]}>
        {motionBased ? (
          <MotionToGraphLayer
            storyProgress={storyProgress}
            phase={phase}
            interactive={interactive}
            scene={scene}
            onCompleteAction={onCompleteAction}
            onDotPointerDown={onDotPointerDown}
          />
        ) : (
          <GenericSceneLayer scene={scene} activeBeat={active.beat} storyProgress={storyProgress} interactive={interactive} onCompleteAction={onCompleteAction} />
        )}
      </group>
    </>
  );
}

function ensureActionIds(scene: VisualStoryScene) {
  const check = scene.optional_check?.success_criteria;
  const hasExplicitCheck = check && Object.keys(check).length > 0;
  if (hasExplicitCheck && storyLooksMotionBased(scene)) return ["mark_one_repeat", "tap_peak"];
  return ["explored_scene"];
}

export function VisualStoryProbe(props: GenericProbeComponentProps) {
  const scene = useMemo(() => normalizeVisualStoryScene(props.probe), [props.probe]);
  const totalMs = useMemo(() => totalDuration(scene.beats), [scene.beats]);
  const [playheadMs, setPlayheadMs] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);
  const [completedActionIds, setCompletedActionIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [interactionMode, setInteractionMode] = useState<"rotate" | "phase">("rotate");
  const [stageYaw, setStageYaw] = useState(0);
  const [stagePitch, setStagePitch] = useState(0);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState(1);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(7.4);
  const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<DragState | null>(null);
  const [hasExplored, setHasExplored] = useState(false);
  const [showScriptDuringInteraction, setShowScriptDuringInteraction] = useState(false);
  const lastFrameRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const lastDraftTextRef = useRef("");

  const done = playheadMs >= totalMs;
  const interactive = done && !props.disabled;
  const active = beatInfo(scene.beats, playheadMs);
  const requiredActionIds = ensureActionIds(scene);
  const requiredComplete = requiredActionIds.every((id) => id === "explored_scene" ? hasExplored : completedActionIds.includes(id));
  const completionReady = done && requiredComplete;
  const visibleScript = revealScriptSegment(active.beat.script_segment, active.localProgress, scene.script_display.animate_text);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying || props.disabled) {
      lastFrameRef.current = null;
      return;
    }

    let frameId = 0;

    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const last = lastFrameRef.current ?? now;
      const delta = Math.min(80, Math.max(0, now - last));
      lastFrameRef.current = now;

      setPlayheadMs((current) => {
        const next = Math.min(totalMs, current + delta);
        if (next >= totalMs) {
          setIsPlaying(false);
          setPhase(1);
        }
        return next;
      });

      frameId = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    frameId = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => window.cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
  }, [isPlaying, props.disabled, totalMs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const payload = {
      visual_story_completed: completionReady,
      visual_story_schema_version: scene.schema_version,
      visual_story_title: scene.title,
      visual_story_scene_family: scene.scene_family,
      visual_story_completed_action_ids: completedActionIds,
      visual_story_required_action_ids: requiredActionIds,
      orientation_script: scene.orientation_script,
      explored_scene: hasExplored,
      phase,
      elapsed_seconds: Math.round(playheadMs / 1000),
    };

    const nextText = completionReady ? JSON.stringify(payload) : "";
    if (lastDraftTextRef.current === nextText) return;
    lastDraftTextRef.current = nextText;

    props.onDraftChange({
      ...props.draft,
      attempt_type: "text",
      text_response: nextText,
      generated_video_completed: completionReady,
      generated_video_elapsed_seconds: Math.round(playheadMs / 1000),
      visual_story_completed: completionReady,
      visual_story_scene_family: scene.scene_family,
      visual_story_completed_action_ids: completedActionIds,
      visual_story_interaction_notes: completionReady
        ? `Explored visual story scene: ${scene.title}.`
        : "",
    });
    // Publish draft only when learner-visible completion state changes.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [completionReady, completedActionIds.join("|"), hasExplored, phase, scene.schema_version, scene.scene_family, scene.title, scene.orientation_script]);

  function completeAction(actionId: string) {
    setHasExplored(true);
    setCompletedActionIds((current) => current.includes(actionId) ? current : [...current, actionId]);
  }

  function replay() {
    setPlayheadMs(0);
    setIsPlaying(true);
    setCompletedActionIds([]);
    setHasExplored(false);
    setShowScriptDuringInteraction(false);
    setStageYaw(0);
    setStagePitch(0);
    setPhase(0);
    setZoom(7.4);
    setDragState(null);
    lastFrameRef.current = null;
  }

  function handleCanvasPointerDown(event: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (!interactive) return;
    const mode = interactionMode;
    setDragState({ mode, x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, yaw: stageYaw, pitch: stagePitch, phase });
    setHasExplored(true);
  }

  function handleCanvasPointerMove(event: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (!interactive || !dragState) return;
    const dx = event.clientX - dragState.x;
    const dy = event.clientY - dragState.y;

    if (dragState.mode === "phase") {
      setPhase(((dragState.phase + dx / 360) % 1 + 1) % 1);
      completeAction("explored_scene");
      return;
    }

    setStageYaw(dragState.yaw + dx * 0.008);
    setStagePitch(clamp(dragState.pitch + dy * 0.005, -0.38, 0.38));
    completeAction("explored_scene");
  }

  function handleCanvasPointerUp() {
    setDragState(null);
  }

  function handleWheel(event: ReactWheelEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (!interactive) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    setZoom((current) => clamp(current + event.deltaY * 0.006, 5.1, 10.2));
    setHasExplored(true);
    completeAction("explored_scene");
  }

  return (
    <section aria-label="Visual story probe" style={shellStyle}>
      <Canvas
        camera={{ position: [0, 3.2, 7.4], fov: 43, near: 0.1, far: 100 }}
        dpr={[1, 1.75]}
        onPointerDown={handleCanvasPointerDown}
        onPointerMove={handleCanvasPointerMove}
        onPointerUp={handleCanvasPointerUp}
        onPointerLeave={handleCanvasPointerUp}
        onWheel={handleWheel}
        style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, width: "100%", height: "100%", cursor: interactive ? (interactionMode === "phase" ? "ew-resize" : "grab") : "default" }}
      >
        <VisualStoryWorld
          scene={scene}
          playheadMs={playheadMs}
          totalMs={totalMs}
          active={active}
          phase={phase}
          stageYaw={stageYaw}
          stagePitch={stagePitch}
          zoom={zoom}
          interactive={interactive}
          onCompleteAction={completeAction}
          onDotPointerDown={(event) => {
            if (!interactive) return;
            event.stopPropagation();
            const native = event.nativeEvent;
            setInteractionMode("phase");
            setHasExplored(true);
            setDragState({ mode: "phase", x: native.clientX, y: native.clientY, yaw: stageYaw, pitch: stagePitch, phase });
            completeAction("explored_scene");
          }}
        />
      </Canvas>

      <div style={vignetteStyle} />

      <div style={topControlRowStyle}>
        <button type="button" disabled={props.disabled || done} onClick={() => setIsPlaying((value) => !value)} style={controlButtonStyle}>
          {isPlaying ? "Pause" : done ? "Finished" : "Play"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" disabled={props.disabled} onClick={replay} style={controlButtonStyle}>
          Replay
        </button>
        {done ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={props.disabled}
            onClick={() => setShowScriptDuringInteraction((value) => !value)}
            style={showScriptDuringInteraction ? activeButtonStyle : controlButtonStyle}
          >
            Script
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          ...captionRailStyle,
          opacity: interactive && !showScriptDuringInteraction ? 0 : 1,
          transform: interactive && !showScriptDuringInteraction ? "translateY(0.6rem)" : "translateY(0)",
          transition: "opacity 180ms ease, transform 180ms ease",
        }}
      >
        <div style={captionCardStyle}>
          <p style={captionKickerStyle}>orientation script · {active.index + 1}/{scene.beats.length}</p>
          <p style={captionTextStyle}>{interactive && showScriptDuringInteraction ? scene.orientation_script : visibleScript}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={bottomRailStyle}>
        <div style={progressTrackStyle}>
          <div
            style={{
              height: "100%",
              width: `${Math.round((playheadMs / Math.max(1, totalMs)) * 100)}%`,
              borderRadius: "999px",
              background: "linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(168,85,247,0.95), rgba(251,191,36,0.95))",
              transition: isPlaying ? "none" : "width 240ms ease",
            }}
          />
        </div>

        {done ? (
          <div style={interactionPanelStyle}>
            <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
              <p style={interactionTitleStyle}>Explore the scene</p>
              <p style={interactionHintStyle}>
                {scene.interaction_phase.free_exploration?.instructions ?? "Drag the scene to rotate, scroll to zoom, or grab the glowing dot to scrub the motion directly."}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div style={interactionActionsStyle}>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={props.disabled}
                onClick={() => setInteractionMode("rotate")}
                style={interactionMode === "rotate" ? activeButtonStyle : controlButtonStyle}
              >
                Drag scene
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={props.disabled}
                onClick={() => setInteractionMode("phase")}
                style={interactionMode === "phase" ? activeButtonStyle : controlButtonStyle}
              >
                Move dot
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={props.disabled}
                onClick={() => completeAction("mark_one_repeat")}
                style={completedActionIds.includes("mark_one_repeat") ? successButtonStyle : controlButtonStyle}
              >
                Mark repeat
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={props.disabled}
                onClick={() => completeAction("tap_peak")}
                style={completedActionIds.includes("tap_peak") ? successButtonStyle : controlButtonStyle}
              >
                Tap peak
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

const shellStyle = {
  position: "relative",
  minHeight: "min(76vh, 790px)",
  overflow: "hidden",
  borderRadius: "30px",
  border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
  background: "radial-gradient(circle at 30% 20%, rgba(168,85,247,0.28), transparent 36%), linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(6,6,19,0.96), rgba(35,10,60,0.92))",
  boxShadow: "0 28px 90px rgba(0,0,0,0.38)",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const vignetteStyle = {
  pointerEvents: "none",
  position: "absolute",
  inset: 0,
  background: "radial-gradient(circle at center, transparent 48%, rgba(0,0,0,0.28) 100%)",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const topControlRowStyle = {
  position: "absolute",
  right: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  top: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  display: "flex",
  gap: "0.5rem",
  flexWrap: "wrap",
  justifyContent: "flex-end",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const captionRailStyle = {
  pointerEvents: "none",
  position: "absolute",
  left: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  right: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  bottom: "clamp(5.9rem, 12vh, 7.4rem)",
  display: "flex",
  justifyContent: "center",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const captionCardStyle = {
  width: "min(58rem, 100%)",
  border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.13)",
  borderRadius: "20px",
  background: "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(7,0,20,0.56), rgba(7,0,20,0.38))",
  padding: "0.78rem 0.95rem",
  backdropFilter: "blur(14px)",
  boxShadow: "0 16px 48px rgba(0,0,0,0.24)",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const captionKickerStyle = {
  margin: 0,
  color: "rgba(221,214,254,0.84)",
  fontSize: "0.66rem",
  fontWeight: 850,
  letterSpacing: "0.18em",
  textTransform: "uppercase",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const captionTextStyle = {
  margin: "0.35rem 0 0",
  color: "white",
  fontSize: "clamp(0.98rem, 1.55vw, 1.34rem)",
  lineHeight: 1.34,
  letterSpacing: "-0.018em",
  fontWeight: 820,
  textShadow: "0 10px 34px rgba(0,0,0,0.42)",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const bottomRailStyle = {
  position: "absolute",
  left: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  right: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  bottom: "clamp(0.9rem, 2.5vw, 1.4rem)",
  display: "grid",
  gap: "0.7rem",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const progressTrackStyle = {
  height: "0.42rem",
  overflow: "hidden",
  borderRadius: "999px",
  background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)",
} satisfies CSSProperties;

const interactionPanelStyle = {
  display: "flex",
  alignItems: "center",
  justifyContent: "space-between",
